### [1] [Exact SU($N$) symmetry resolution of bosonic Hubbard models: general boson-fermion mappings and flat-band singlet-cluster states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14183)
*Loïc Herviou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用U(L)与U(N)之间的Howe对偶性，对SU(N)对称Bose-Hubbard模型在各不可约表示中分别进行精确对角化，揭示三条结构性质，并给出有限温度相图与阻挫晶格上单态团簇态的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SU(N)对称的Bose-Hubbard模型在冷原子、多轨道体系等领域具有重要意义，但传统精确对角化方法未利用SU(N)不可约表示结构，导致在较大N与有限温度时计算效率低，难以系统研究激发态、阻挫晶格及有限温度行为。

Method: 基于U(L)与U(N)之间的Howe对偶性，将Hilbert空间按SU(N)不可约表示（颜色轨道扇区）分解，构造颜色分辨的精确对角化方法，从而对任意晶格和由U(L)生成元构成的Hamiltonian分块求解。

Result: 得到三条结构结论：(i) 给定轨道扇区的谱与N及粒子统计无关；(ii) 玻色子与费米子配分函数满足精确恒等式 Z_b(-N)=(-1)^{N_b}Z_f(N)；(iii) 无阻挫跃迁时基态总是颜色铁磁体。应用上给出大N下吸引SU(N)玻色子在少格点环上的有限温度相图，并在锯齿链与Kagome晶格中发现阻挫跃迁诱导的单态团簇态。

Conclusion: 颜色分辨对角化方法为研究SU(N)对称模型的激发扇区、阻挫晶格与有限温度性质提供了有效工具，并揭示了玻色-费米对偶与颜色铁磁/单态团簇等普适结构。

Abstract: We show how to perform exact diagonalization of SU($N$)-symmetric Bose-Hubbard models separately in each irreducible representation of SU($N$), by exploiting the Howe duality between U($L$) and U($N$). Three structural consequences follow for any lattice and any Hamiltonian built from the U($L$) generators: (i) the spectrum in a given orbital sector is independent of $N$ and of the particle statistics, (ii) the canonical partition functions for bosons and fermions obey the exact identity $Z_{\mathrm{b}}(-N)=(-1)^{N_\mathrm{b}}Z_{\mathrm{f}}(N)$, (iii) with unfrustrated hopping the groundstate is always a color ferromagnet, so that the color-resolved method we introduce is useful to study excited sectors, frustrated lattices and finite temperatures. As applications, we first derive the finite-temperature phase diagram of attractive SU($N$) bosons on few-site rings at large $N$ for arbitrary filling. We then show the emergence of singlet-cluster states from frustrated hopping, the counterpart of fermionic ferromagnetism, with the example of the sawtooth and kagome lattice.

</details>


### [2] [Exotic metallic phase in a heterotic Kondo lattice: violation of Luttinger's theorem and absence of Kondo gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14556)
*Hanting Zhong,Shuxiang Yang,Chao Cao,Xiao-Yong Feng,Jianhui Dai,Hai-Qing Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在顺磁相下研究具有异质层状结构的近藤晶格系统，发现弱近藤耦合区呈现分数化狄拉克液体，强耦合区自旋子与狄拉克费米子杂化形成大费米面，且在精确半填充时系统仍保持金属性并违反Luttinger定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米面转变伴随量子相变在重费米子材料与范德华量子异质结中普遍存在，统一的Anderson晶格描述认为电子半填充时强近藤耦合区的相互作用基态总是不稳定金属相，本文旨在检验这一论断。

Method: 在具有异质层状结构的近藤晶格系统的顺磁相中，分别考察弱近藤耦合区与强近藤耦合区的基态性质，并分析不同层状堆叠构型对能带与近藤能隙的影响。

Result: 弱近藤耦合区出现分数化狄拉克液体，包含无质量或有质量狄拉克费米子以及电荷中性的自旋子费米面；强耦合区自旋子与狄拉克费米子杂化，形成中间近平坦杂化带及导带与价带重费米子带，产生大电费米面；依堆叠构型不同，这些能带间不存在近藤能隙；在精确半填充时系统仍为金属，且跨越临界点时Luttinger定理被违反。

Conclusion: 该结果表明统一Anderson晶格描述中"强耦合区半填充必破坏金属相"的结论并非普适，层状异质结构可维持半填充金属态并违反Luttinger定理，对范德华量子异质结的相关研究具有启示意义。

Abstract: Fermi surface transformation accompanied by quantum phase transition is widely observed in correlated electron systems including heavy fermion materials and van der Waals quantum heterostructures. The unified Anderson lattice description for this phenomenon suggests that their interacting ground states in the case of electron half filling always destabilize the metallic phase in the strong Kondo-coupling regime. We examine this issue within the paramagnetic phase of a Kondo lattice system with a heterotic layered structure. In the weak Kondo-coupling regime, the system exhibits a fractional Dirac liquid involving the massless or massive Dirac fermions and a charge neutral spinon Fermi surface. In the strong Kondo-coupling regime, the spinons hybridize with the Dirac fermions resulting in an intermediate nearly-flat hybridizing band together with the conduction and valence heavy fermion bands, yielding a large electric Fermi surface. Absence of the Kondo gap between these bands is observed depending on the stacking configurations of the layered structure. In particular, the system remains metallic even at the exact half filling in this regime and Luttinger's theorem is violated across the critical point. Implications of these results on the van der Waals quantum heterostructures are briefly discussed.

</details>


### [3] [Emergent one-dimensional Bloch oscillations in coupled Ising layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14857)
*E. S. Ma,Z. Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在不对称纵向场下耦合Ising层中，层间Heisenberg耦合产生有效横向耦合，导致涌现的畴壁准粒子在一维子空间中呈现Bloch振荡，其机制源于纵向场失配诱导的线性势与Wannier–Stark阶梯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Bloch振荡需要周期系统加恒定力（倾斜势）。本文旨在探索在无常规倾斜势条件下产生Bloch振荡的新机制，并揭示耦合Ising层中畴壁动力学的涌现量子行为。

Method: 研究不对称纵向场下耦合Ising层的畴壁动力学；层间Heisenberg耦合在涌现赝自旋表象中充当有效横向耦合。通过分析梯级赝自旋结构导出的动力学约束，将多体动力学约化到一维准粒子子空间；纵向场失配为涌现畴壁激发提供有效线性势。结合解析推导与时间依赖可观测量数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 梯级赝自旋结构产生动力学约束，使二维系统中的多体动力学限制在涌现的一维准粒子子空间；纵向场失配诱导有效线性势，将畴壁激发限制在两个相反极化的铁磁畴之间；由此形成的畴壁激发Wannier–Stark阶梯在无常规倾斜势时产生Bloch振荡。数值模拟结果与解析预测一致。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了无需常规倾斜势即可实现Bloch振荡的独特机制，表明耦合Ising层中涌现的畴壁准粒子可作为研究相干量子动力学的平台，并拓展了对受约束多体系统中Bloch振荡起源的理解。

Abstract: Bloch oscillations typically arise in periodic systems subjected to a constant force generated by a potential gradient. Here, we study domain-wall dynamics in coupled Ising layers under asymmetric longitudinal fields. The interlayer coupling is of Heisenberg type and acts as an effective transverse coupling in the emergent pseudospin representation. We show that the rung pseudospin structure gives rise to kinetic constraints, restricting the many-body dynamics to an emergent one-dimensional quasiparticle subspace within the original two-dimensional system. Furthermore, the imbalance between the longitudinal fields of the two layers induces an effective linear potential for the emergent domain-wall excitations, leading to their confinement between two oppositely polarized ferromagnetic domains. Remarkably, the resulting Wannier--Stark ladder of domain-wall excitations gives rise to Bloch oscillations in the absence of a conventional tilted potential, revealing a distinct mechanism for coherent quantum dynamics. Numerical simulations of time-dependent observables confirm the analytical predictions.

</details>


### [4] [Spectral Mapping Theorem between SU(N) and SU(Ns/N) Heisenberg Models and Exact Diagonalization of SU(4) and SU(7) Long-Range Ordered Phases on the Triangular Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15162)
*Samuel Gozel,Pierre Nataf*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明SU(N)与SU(Ns/N)海森堡模型在单态谱上的负对称性，并基于标准杨表（SYT）基的高效实现，将SU(4)、SU(7)三角晶格模型精确对角化扩展到Ns=28，确认次近邻耦合稳定四子晶格长程序，且增加色数到N=7可在无额外耦合时实现类Néel基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SU(N)海森堡模型的精确对角化受限于希尔伯特空间维度，难以处理大系统与多色情形；同时SU(4)三角晶格最近邻基态性质仍存争议，需要更大规模数值研究来澄清其磁序及获得类Néel序的途径。

Method: 利用标准杨表（SYT）基中的转置操作，证明单态扇区全谱的负对称定理；并针对该基设计节省内存的高效实现，从而对三角晶格上SU(4)与SU(7)海森堡模型进行精确对角化，规模达Ns=28。

Result: 理论部分给出SU(N)与SU(Ns/N)单态谱互为负谱的严格定理；数值部分将SU(4)、SU(7)三角晶格计算扩展至Ns=28，确认SU(4)中次近邻耦合可稳定四子晶格长程序，并发现将色数由N=4增至N=7即使无次近邻耦合也能通向类Néel基态。

Conclusion: SYT基的转置对称性与高效实现显著提升了SU(N)海森堡模型的可计算规模；在三角晶格中，次近邻耦合与增加色数是获得长程磁序的两条有效路径。

Abstract: We first show that, for the SU(N) Heisenberg model with isotropic two-site interactions on an arbitrary lattice of Ns sites, the full spectrum in its singlet sector (when N divides Ns) is the negative of the full spectrum in its singlet sector of the SU(Ns/N) Heisenberg model defined on the same lattice. This theorem, valid for models with the fundamental irreducible representation (irrep) at each lattice site, follows naturally from a transposition operation in the basis of Standard Young Tableaux (SYTs). Second, this basis -- previously recognized as particularly convenient for exact diagonalization (ED) of SU(N) Heisenberg models -- admits an efficient, memory-saving implementation, thereby enabling large-scale diagonalizations for unprecedented system sizes Ns and numbers of colors N>3. We apply these ideas to an ED study of the SU(4) and SU(7) Heisenberg models on the triangular lattice (TL), extending the calculations up to Ns=28 sites. In particular, starting from the SU(4) case, whose nearest-neighbor ground state remains debated, we confirm that next-nearest-neighbor couplings stabilize four-sublattice long-range order. We further find that increasing the number of colors from N=4 to N=7 provides another route toward a Néel-like ground state, even without such additional couplings. N = 4 to N = 7 provides another route toward a Néel-like ground state, even without such additional couplings.

</details>


### [5] [Superlattice-Stabilized Fractional Chern Insulators in a "Berry Basin" Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15197)
*Yongxin Zeng,Andrew J. Millis*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出"Berry basin"模型，刻画菱面石墨烯中色散带平底、层自由度动量空间绕转及库仑相互作用，揭示整数/分数陈绝缘体的稳定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱面石墨烯中整数与分数陈绝缘体的发现（继扭转MoTe₂孤立拓扑平带之后），促使人们寻找电子能带承载拓扑关联态的普适条件；需超越孤立拓扑平带范式，研究量子几何、超晶格调制与电子相互作用三者关联。

Method: 构建"Berry basin"模型：包含平底的色散带、层自由度的动量空间绕转、库仑相互作用，并引入周期性势场，从而可控地研究量子几何、超晶格调制与相互作用的协同效应。

Result: 在无超晶格势的强耦合下即出现整数陈绝缘体；而分数态的稳定在所有可及的相互作用强度下都需要非零超晶格势。并解释了当电子偏离莫尔界面极化时菱面石墨烯中整数与分数陈绝缘体更稳健的原因。

Conclusion: 确立了可承载稳健分数陈绝缘体的新一类体系，并提供可控数值平台以检验相互作用拓扑相的竞争理论。

Abstract: The discovery of integer and fractional Chern insulators in rhombohedral graphene, following their discovery in the isolated topological flat band in twisted MoTe$_2$, motivates a search for the conditions under which an electronic band can host a topological correlated state. In this Letter we introduce a ``Berry basin" model which captures the essential features of rhombohedral graphene: a dispersive band with a flat bottom, momentum-space winding of a ``layer" degree of freedom, and Coulomb interactions, while also including periodic potentials, enabling controlled studies of the interplay of quantum geometry, superlattice modulation, and electron-electron interactions in producing correlated topological states beyond the isolated topological band paradigm. While integer Chern insulators emerge at strong coupling without a superlattice potential, stabilizing the fractional state requires nonzero potential for all interaction strengths we access. We provide insight into the greater robustness of integer and fractional Chern insulators in rhombohedral graphene when electrons are polarized away from the moiré interface. Our results establish a new class of systems that host robust fractional Chern insulators and provide a controlled numerical platform to test competing theories of interacting topological phases.

</details>


### [6] [Dispersion of low-energy optical excitations in NiPS$_3$ explained by singlet polaron theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15230)
*I. J. Hamad,C. S. Helman,A. E. Feiguin,L. O. Manuel,A. A. Aligia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文采用单态极化子理论(SPT)计算NiPS₃中~1.5 eV激子激发的动量依赖谱函数与色散，理论得到两个单态激发，其最低能准粒子色散与RIXS测量的激子色散在形状和带宽上高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NiPS₃光学与RIXS实验在~1.5 eV处观测到窄激子共振，但其微观起源仍存争议，亟需理论澄清其本质及动量依赖行为。

Method: 在先前针对NiPS₃发展的单态极化子理论框架下，构建有效模型并计算其谱函数，从中提取准粒子色散；理论中激发在S=1的锯齿状反铁磁背景中传播，并与双磁子过程耦合。

Result: 理论上自然给出两个单态激发；其中最低能准粒子的色散与RIXS测量的激子色散高度一致，沿布里渊区高对称方向再现了整体形状与带宽。

Conclusion: NiPS₃的光学激发可自洽地解释为色散型单态极化子，类似机制可能主导其他层状范德华反铁磁体中的激子动力学。

Abstract: Optical and resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) experiments in the van der Waals antiferromagnet NiPS$_3$ have revealed narrow excitonic resonances near 1.5 eV whose microscopic origin remains under debate. In this work we study the momentum dependence of these excitations using the singlet-polaron theory (SPT) previously developed for NiPS$_3$. Within its framework, we calculate the spectral function of the corresponding effective model and extract the dispersion of the resulting quasiparticles. The theory naturally predicts two singlet excitations whose dynamics arise from their propagation in the zig-zag antiferromagnetic background ($S=1$) coupled to two-magnon processes. We show that the dispersion of the lowest-energy quasiparticle agrees very well with the excitonic dispersion measured by RIXS, reproducing both its overall shape and bandwidth along high-symmetry directions of the Brillouin zone. Our results provide a consistent interpretation of the optical excitations in terms of dispersive singlet polarons, and suggest that similar mechanisms may govern excitonic dynamics in other layered van der Waals antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [7] [Matrix-product-state GPLHR for non-Hermitian many-body eigenproblems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15387)
*Xingyao Wu,Zhangyu Yang,Yanbin Chen,Yu Wang,Feng Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出在矩阵乘积态（MPS）框架下构建广义预处理局部谐波残差（GPLHR）方法，用于高效计算非厄米多体算符在复平面上指定位移附近的多个右本征态，并采用逐次随机压缩（SRC）加速算符作用，在转相关与复数Hubbard链、相互作用非厄米SSH链及Liouvillian模型上验证了方法的效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算非厄米多体算符的多个本征态对研究相互作用与开放量子系统的激发谱、谱性质及弛豫动力学具有重要意义。然而该任务面临两大困难：一是本征向量一般非正交，二是希尔伯特空间维度随系统尺寸指数增长，导致传统方法效率与精度受限。

Method: 将广义预处理局部谐波残差（GPLHR）方法推广至矩阵乘积态（MPS）框架，以高效求解本征值位于复平面指定位移附近的多个目标右本征态；同时引入逐次随机压缩（SRC）技术加速算符作用过程。

Result: 在转相关与复数Hubbard链、相互作用非厄米Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链以及Liouvillian模型上进行测试，结果表明GPLHR与MPS结合能够高效、准确地计算非厄米多体算符的多个本征态。

Conclusion: GPLHR结合MPS为非厄米多体算符多本征态的计算提供了一种高效且准确的途径，适用于相互作用与开放量子系统的谱与动力学研究。

Abstract: Computing multiple eigenstates of non-Hermitian many-body operators plays an important role in investigating excitation spectra, spectral properties, and relaxation dynamics in interacting and open quantum systems. However, this task is challenging because the eigenvectors are generally nonorthogonal and the Hilbert space dimension grows exponentially with system size. Here, we formulate the generalized preconditioned locally harmonic residual (GPLHR) method in a matrix product state (MPS) framework to efficiently compute multiple targeted right eigenstates whose eigenvalues lie near a specified shift in the complex plane. Successive randomized compression (SRC) is employed to accelerate operator applications. We test the method on transcorrelated and complex Hubbard chains, an interacting non-Hermitian Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain, and a Liouvillian model. The results demonstrate that GPLHR combined with MPS provides an efficient and accurate approach for computing multiple eigenstates of non-Hermitian many-body operators.

</details>


### [8] [Dynamical chiral symmetry breaking in rotating QED$_3$: critical flavor number and the occupation-reweighting channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15568)
*Wei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在刚性旋转圆盘上的(2+1)维QED中，用彩虹Schwinger-Dyson方法研究动力学手征对称性破缺：零温下旋转对凝聚体响应严格为零，临界味数不变；有限温度下旋转通过占据重加权通道压制凝聚体，并给出定标指数与交叉味数单调下降的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为(2+1)维QED模型建立有限温度下的参考基准，厘清旋转（转动）对动力学手征对称性破缺及临界味数的影响，并区分零温与有限温度两类情形。

Method: 在彩虹Schwinger-Dyson框架下，于刚性旋转圆盘（ΩR&lt;1）上研究(2+1)维QED；采用三种操作性定义提取临界味数N_c^SD，并通过线性响应分析分离占据重加权通道与屏蔽通道的贡献（含自洽反馈）。

Result: 零温：凝聚体的旋转响应恒为零，N_c^SD(Ω,T=0)=N_c^SD(0)=2.60±0.01（三种定义）；有限温度：旋转压制凝聚体，以占据重加权通道为主导，κ̂_occ=-1.55，占线性化总响应≥99.995%，自洽反馈放大因子26.5；屏蔽通道在固定正则化下κ̂_scr(ε=0.01)=-0.97，其ε→0行为对数发散；固定温度下交叉味数N*(Ω)单调下降。

Conclusion: 零温下临界味数不随旋转改变（定理性结论）；有限温度下旋转通过占据重加权机制主导压制手征凝聚体，交叉味数单调下降为推论。这些结果共同确立了该模型有限温度的参考基准。

Abstract: We study dynamical chiral symmetry breaking in $(2{+}1)$-dimensional QED on a rigidly rotating disc, $ΩR<1$, within the rainbow Schwinger--Dyson framework. At zero temperature the rotation response of the condensate vanishes identically, so $N_c^{\rm SD}(Ω,T{=}0)=N_c^{\rm SD}(0)=2.60\pm0.01$ (using three operational definitions). At finite temperature, rotation suppresses the condensate through a dominant occupation-reweighting channel, $\hatκ_{\rm occ}=-1.55$, accounting for $\ge 99.995\%$ of the linearized total response at the reference parameters, with self-consistent feedback amplifying it by $26.5$. The screening channel is reported at fixed regularization, $\hatκ_{\rm scr}(\varepsilon = 0.01)=-0.97$; its $\varepsilon \to 0$ behavior is logarithmically divergent. The crossover flavor $N^{*}(Ω)$ falls monotonically at fixed temperature (a corollary). The critical flavor number is unchanged by rotation at $T=0$ (theorem). Together these results set the finite-temperature reference for the model.

</details>

Abstract: The Unified Field Bosonization Technique (UFBT) is employed to investigate the single-particle properties of inhomogeneous Luttinger liquids containing localized clusters of impurities in the presence of forward-scattering interactions. Within this framework, fermionic two-point correlation functions are used to derive the leading-order singular contributions to the dynamical density of states (DDOS) and the cross-barrier tunneling conductance. The results reveal distinct power-law behaviours of the DDOS near the impurity cluster and in the far-field regime. The tunneling conductance exhibits a power-law temperature dependence, decreasing with decreasing temperature for repulsive interactions and increasing for attractive interactions, while remaining temperature-independent in the noninteracting limit. The impurity cluster modifies the amplitudes and spatial structure of these observables, whereas their characteristic power-law exponents are determined solely by the interaction parameters. The formulation accommodates arbitrary impurity strengths without requiring perturbative expansions in the impurity or interaction strengths. The characteristic scaling behaviour emerges directly from the fermionic correlation functions, without an explicit renormalization-group (RG) treatment of the impurity strength, while the weak-interaction limit recovers the leading perturbative correction to the tunneling response. These findings establish UFBT as a unified framework for describing local spectral properties and cross-barrier transport in inhomogeneous Luttinger liquids.

Abstract: Magnetic circular dichroism (MCD) is an optical probe of orbital time reversal symmetry breaking. Although frequency integrated MCD is related to the self-rotation contribution to orbital magnetization at zero field, its magnetic response generally differs from the thermodynamic orbital susceptibility. Here, we define the MCD susceptibility and derive general expressions for multiband systems using an electromagnetic $U(1)$ gauge invariant approach. For insulators, the response contains contributions from the non-Abelian quantum metric, Berry curvature, and field induced interband mixing. For metals, a finite temperature Green function formulation separates the smooth response into Fermi sea and Fermi surface contributions. An independent Landau level calculation confirms the expressions for the zero-field MCD moment and its linear magnetic response. In a continuum model for twisted bilayer graphene, the MCD susceptibility vanishes within the charge neutrality gap, while the thermodynamic orbital susceptibility remains markedly diamagnetic. Near the gap edges, the orbital moment term dominates the response. Features near the van Hove energies are associated with density of states enhancement. These results clarify how the two susceptibilities reflect different orbital processes even for the same electronic structure and thus establish the MCD susceptibility as a distinct probe of orbital magnetic response.

Abstract: Strongly interacting fermionic chains obeying a $Z_n$ symmetry can host topological phases with highly degenerate ground-state manifolds. The different ground states are connected by strongly bound fractionalized edge modes called parafermionic zero modes. While exactly preserved under time evolution at zero temperature, interactions lead to a destabilization of these modes at finite energy densities, and, given a generic Hamiltonian, obtaining the lifetime of these modes is often impossible as it requires knowledge of the full spectrum of the Hamiltonian or long time evolutions. Here, we propose a novel quantum-inspired algorithm that can be used to variationally search for these almost strong zero modes in generic parafermionic chain models. We demonstrate analytically and numerically that our ansatz faithfully reproduces the exact results and finds strongly bound parafermionic operators in the non-integrable Kitaev-Hubbard chain and chiral Potts model. Our results further allow us to verify the existence of a true strong zero mode in the symmetrical limit of the chiral Potts model for $Z_3$-parafermions.

Rev. B \textbf{87}, 195124 (2013)]. On the generic irreducible two-channel branch, braid consistency locks these coordinates through an exact spectrum-geometry relation. *Michael G. Scheer,Eslam Khalaf*

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于自举（bootstrap）与半定规划（SDP）的方法，推导球面上第一 Haldane 赝势哈密顿量（Laughlin 母哈密顿量）能隙的下界，覆盖任意磁通量 N_Φ 及热力学极限，并将数值 SDP 界转化为严格数学证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔效应中模型赝势哈密顿量的谱隙（如 Laughlin 能隙）是核心问题，但缺乏普遍、严格的解析下界方法；同时，数值 SDP 给出的界通常难以升级为严格数学证明。

Result: 获得第一 Haldane 赝势哈密顿量谱隙的数值精确（至求解器精度）下界，并转化为严格数学界；建立了 Laughlin 能隙，且方法原则上可推广至 Moore-Read、Read-Rezayi 等赝势。

Conclusion: 提供了将数值半定规划界转化为严格数学证明的通用方法，具有广泛适用性，并为其他模型波函数对应赝势的谱隙下界推导提供了潜在途径。

Abstract: We develop a bootstrap approach to deriving lower bounds on the spectral gap of the first Haldane pseudopotential Hamiltonian on the sphere with $N_Φ$ flux quanta for arbitrary $N_Φ$, including in the thermodynamic limit $N_Φ\to \infty$. Our approach can in principle be generalized to pseudopotentials describing other model wavefunctions such as the Moore-Read state or Read-Rezayi sequence. Our approach is based on Knabe's observation that if $H$ is a Hamiltonian with ground state energy $0$ then the spectral gap is at least $ε> 0$ if and only if $H^2 \succeq εH$. To show this inequality, we split $H^2$ into two parts, one that can be analytically bounded and another that can be bounded using a finite semidefinite program (SDP). Crucially, the size of this program is determined only by cutoff parameters, and the program depends on $N_Φ$ only parametrically, allowing us to obtain numerically exact (up to solver precision) lower bounds on the gap. In order to turn these numerical bounds into mathematically rigorous bounds, we introduce an algorithm that can produce nearly-optimal exact feasible points for large rational SDPs. We additionally provide an algorithm that establishes the rank stability of the semidefinite program in the limit $N_Φ\to \infty$ which is needed to certify the bound in the thermodynamic limit. Beyond establishing the Laughlin gap and revealing a potential method for deriving spectral gap lower bounds for other pseudopotentials, our work provides a general method for turning numerical semidefinite programming bounds into rigorous mathematical proofs, which may have wide applicability.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文通过含s、p轨道的多轨道紧束缚模型重新审视共线反铁磁zigzag链的磁电效应微观机制，发现其由s-p杂化激活的轨道自由度主导，而非低能哈密顿量本身。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 结果显示磁电响应由s-p杂化激活的轨道自由度主导，而自旋贡献因自旋守恒而为零；在有效模型内直接套用Kubo公式无法捕捉磁电响应，但加入轨道杂化顶点修正后可解决该问题；重整化Kubo公式能够准确复现完整多轨道模型的结果并保持守恒律。

Conclusion: 该研究修正了传统低能视角，指出磁电效应源于编码在顶点修正中的虚拟轨道间过程，而非裸低能哈密顿量；所建立的有效框架为轨道驱动的磁电响应提供了统一的微观理解，并给出了在简单低能模型之外纳入杂化效应的系统途径。

Abstract: Magnetoelectric (ME) effects in antiferromagnets provide a fertile platform for exploring symmetry-driven cross-correlated responses. However, their microscopic origin remains elusive and is often obscured in simplified low-energy descriptions. In this study, we revisit the microscopic mechanism of the ME effect in a collinear antiferromagnetic zigzag chain by employing a multi-orbital tight-binding model that explicitly includes both $s$- and $p$-orbital degrees of freedom. Using analytical and numerical calculations based on the Kubo formula, we demonstrate that the ME response is governed by orbital degrees of freedom activated through $s$--$p$ hybridization, while the spin contribution vanishes due to spin conservation. To elucidate the low-energy description, we derive an effective Hamiltonian projected onto the $s$-orbital subspace using the Schur complement. We show that a naive application of the Kubo formula within this effective model fails to capture the ME response. This issue is resolved by systematically incorporating vertex corrections in terms of orbital hybridization into the response functions. Furthermore, by introducing a quasiparticle renormalization scheme, we formulate a renormalized Kubo formula that preserves conservation laws and accurately reproduces the full multi-orbital results. Our analysis revisits the conventional low-energy perspective and reveals that the ME effect originates from virtual interorbital processes encoded in vertex corrections, rather than from the bare low-energy Hamiltonian. The effective framework developed here provides a unified microscopic understanding of orbital-driven ME responses and offers a systematic route to incorporate hybridization effects beyond simple low-energy models.

</details>

For a capacitively shunted junction at fixed charging energy and arbitrary offset charge, it also preserves the complete quantum spectrum, enabling reversal of the odd nonlinear sector without spectral retuning. Abstract: Hydrogen-rich materials offer a route to high-temperature conventional superconductivity, but often require extreme pressures. Here we report a first-principles study of NaH$_6$, identified in our large-scale hydride search and independently predicted in a subsequent high-throughput study. Several phases compete, with $R\bar{3}m$ and $Pm\bar{3}m$ dominant over broad pressure ranges. The $R\bar{3}m$ phase remains dynamically stable down to 10 GPa, where its calculated $T_\mathrm{c}$ is 34 K, rising to 99 K at 50 GPa. Cubic $Pm\bar{3}m$ becomes thermodynamically competitive at higher pressures and reaches a calculated $T_\mathrm{c}$ of 230 K at 50 GPa, decreasing only weakly upon further compression. Molecular dynamics and phonon-renormalization calculations support structural persistence over the sampled timescale and indicate relatively small anharmonic effects. Despite their high $T_\mathrm{c}$, both phases contain elongated H$_2$-like units, often considered unfavorable for high-$T_\mathrm{c}$ hydrides. Mode-resolved electron-phonon coupling links Raman-active hydrogen vibrations to superconductivity. In $R\bar{3}m$, the linewidths of these modes increase with the pressure-induced tilt of H$_2$ units relative to the threefold axis, alongside the rise in $T_\mathrm{c}$. A minimal symmetry-based model of the vibration-induced second-moment tensor of the hydrogen sublattice shows that these trends are consistent with a significant contribution from response components perpendicular to the threefold axis. NaH$_6$ thus represents an unusual molecular-like hydride in which hydrogen-sublattice reorganization may enable high-$T_\mathrm{c}$ superconductivity at comparatively moderate pressures.

Here, we investigate the atomic-scale surface structure and local superconductivity of the misfit layered compound (SnS)1.17NbS2 using structural characterization, electrical transport, and low-temperature scanning tunnelling microscopy (STM). While X-ray diffraction and cross-sectional scanning transmission electron microscopy confirm the bulk misfit structure, STM resolves large SnS- and NbS2-terminated terraces, where the surface atomic registry differs from the bulk misfit structure. Furthermore, tunnelling spectroscopy at 2 K reveals nearly identical superconducting gaps on both terminations, and the spatial variation of the gap shows no visible modulation that corresponds to the misfit lattice. These results establish an atomic-scale picture of the surface structure and superconducting states of this naturally assembled misfit heterostructure.

Result: PBE泛函系统性高估晶格体积，导致声子软化、EPC增强及Peierls失稳；PBEsol则消除Rb(BN)5中的虚频，给出常压Tc=113 K、2 GPa下Tc=138 K的优异结果。增强的电子局域化和短程关联通过晶格尺度声子硬化与电子尺度能带展宽双重机制抑制Peierls畸变。压力可减弱泛函间的声子差异，而电子性质对费米能级附近精细能带的扰动仍高度敏感。

Conclusion: 金属共价键超导体对XC泛函选择异常敏感，PBEsol因更好处理短程相互作用而更准确，是实现可靠超导性质预测的关键；该发现为共价笼状超导体的计算发现提供重要参考。

Abstract: The recent experimental synthesis of covalent cage superconductors has ignited hopes for ambient-pressure high-temperature superconductivity, while the computational strategy for discovering new materials poses challenges for accurately estimating superconducting properties. Here, we quantified the response of electron-phonon coupling (EPC) properties in M(BN)5 superconductors to different exchange-correlation (XC) effects using high-throughput first-principles calculations. We found that the metallic covalent bonds responsible for superconductivity exhibit anomalously high sensitivity to functional selection uncertainty, distinguishing them from conventional superconductors mediated by metallic bonding. The widely used PBE functional systematically overestimates lattice volumes, inducing phonon softening, enhanced EPC, and consequent Peierls instability. In contrast, PBEsol, which is more accurate through enhanced treatment of short-range interactions, eliminates imaginary frequencies in Rb(BN)5 that appear at the PBE level, yielding an outstanding superconducting critical temperature (Tc) of 113 K at ambient pressure and 138 K at 2 GPa. Enhanced electron localization and short-range correlation in covalent bonds manifest through dual mechanisms: phonon hardening at the lattice scale and band widening at the electronic scale, which collectively suppress Peierls distortion and delocalize antibonding states. Pressure-dependent analysis reveals that compression attenuates phonon discrepancies between XC functionals, whereas electronic properties remain notably sensitive to XC-induced perturbations of fine electronic bands near the Fermi level.

</details>


### [57] [Quantitative Impact of Occupation Smearing Methods on Conventional Superconductivity: from Electronic Structure through Lattice Dynamics to Electron-Phonon Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18185)
*Zhongyu Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 以CsH6为基准，系统揭示第一性原理中展宽函数参数对声子介导超导计算的显著影响，并提出基于自由电子气模型的展宽宽度标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属体系第一性原理计算需借助展宽函数及其宽度（如常用的0.02 Ry Methfessel-Paxton）保证费米面处能量与占据数的连续性，但该经验参数对声子介导超导的定量影响长期被忽视，往往仅凭惯例选取，可能导致有潜力的高温超导体被误判。

Method: 以六氢化物CsH6为基准，将不同展宽函数与宽度同无参数优化的四面体方法进行对比，系统考察展宽诱导误差的大小与传播机制；并从自由电子气模型出发，推导适用于不同展宽函数的展宽宽度标度关系。

Result: 长尾展宽使占据窗口离域化，引起显著费米能级移动、态密度平坦化与声子模硬化，削弱电声耦合；短尾展宽采样不足，产生态密度振荡与增强的费米面嵌套，经增强电子屏蔽与Peierls不稳定性软化声学模并产生虚频，导致CsH6动力学不稳定而被弃用。优化四面体方法则表明CsH6在常压下动力学稳定，超导转变温度高达121 K。

Conclusion: 经验展宽参数可严重扭曲声子与超导预测结果，甚至埋没有潜力的高温超导体；所推导的普适标度关系为高通量筛选提供了客观、精确且高效的参数选取方案。

Abstract: In first-principles calculations of metallic systems, the discrete electronic mesh in the Brillouin zone necessitates a smearing function and its width (e.g., the widely used 0.02 Ry Methfessel-Paxton scheme) to enforce continuity of energy and occupation at the Fermi surface. However, the quantitative impact of such empirical parameters on phonon-mediated superconductivity has long been overlooked, leading to their habitual selection by mere convention. Here, using hexahydride CsH6 as a benchmark, we systematically investigate the magnitude and propagation mechanism of smearing-induced errors against the parameter-free optimized tetrahedron method. Long-tailed smearing delocalizes the occupation window, causing substantial Fermi-level shifts, flattening of the density of states, and hardening phonon modes, thereby weakening electron-phonon coupling. Conversely, short-tailed smearing suffers from insufficient sampling, producing density-of-states oscillations and enhanced Fermi-surface nesting; via amplified electronic screening and Peierls instabilities, this softens acoustic modes and generates imaginary frequencies, causing CsH6 to be dynamically unstable and discarded. However, the optimized tetrahedron method reveals that CsH6 is dynamically stable under ambient pressure with a remarkable superconducting transition temperature of 121 K. To prevent subjective empirical parameters from obscuring theoretically promising high-temperature superconductors, we derive a simple scaling relation for the smearing width, applicable to different smearing functions, from the free-electron-gas model, offering an objective, precise, and efficient protocol for parameter selection in high-throughput screening.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未扭转多层石墨烯中近期观测到超导性，且随位移场与载流子密度变化在多个不同区域出现，具有不同的温度和磁场响应，暗示可能存在多种微观机制。纯电子配对机制已被广泛研究，但声子介导超导的系统性考察仍缺失。

Abstract: The recent observation of superconductivity in untwisted multilayer graphene has sparked intense interest in its microscopic origin. As the displacement field and carrier density are varied, superconductivity emerges in several distinct regions with different temperature and magnetic-field responses. This behavior has been attributed to the presence of distinct electronic phases, raising the possibility that different superconducting regions originate from different microscopic mechanisms. While purely electronic pairing mechanisms have been extensively explored, an in-depth examination of phonon-mediated superconductivity is still missing. Here, we solve the coupled selfconsistent Migdal-Eliashberg equations using input from first-principles calculations and incorporating interaction-driven symmetry breaking of the electronic ground state. Varying the carrier density and displacement field, we reproduce the experimentally observed superconducting regions with comparable superconducting properties such as gap and $T_c$ and predict additional, as-yet undiscovered superconducting phases. Our findings exemplify that several superconductivity regions in rhombohedral graphene can be satisfactorily explained by phonon-mediated pairing.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建实验标定的电阻分流约瑟夫森结（RSJ）模型，研究热耦合非晶超导网络；其中依赖关系来自电流再分配与热媒介相互作用，并由热扩散率控制。

Conclusion: 结果为相互依赖网络的几何框架提供了可实验检验的补充视角，强调物理输运导致的依赖机制会改变级联与相稳定性结论。

Abstract: Interdependent networks can be highly fragile because dependency links amplify local failures into cascades that trigger global collapse. Much of the theoretical understanding comes from geometric network models, where dependency range is imposed externally and coupling strength is distance independent, and it is unclear how these insights apply to real materials, where interdependence arises from physical transport. Here we study thermally coupled amorphous superconducting networks, in which dependencies emerge from current redistribution and heat-mediated interactions governed by thermal diffusivity. Using an experimentally calibrated model of resistively shunted Josephson junctions, we show the system reproduces the three canonical transition regimes: continuous, nucleation-dominated, and mixed-order. However, because heat-driven dependency simultaneously broadens and weakens with distance, long-range thermal coupling can stabilize the superconducting phase upon heating, in contrast with its destabilizing role in geometric models. Cascades are anisotropic, with one-dimensional front propagation. These results offer an experimentally testable perspective complementing the geometric framework.

</details>


### [60] [Twofold Symmetry Observed in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe Interfacial Superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.05904)
*Xinru Han,Hailang Qin,Tianluo Pan,Bin Guo,Kaige Shi,Zijin Huang,Jie Jiang,Hangyu Yin,Hongtao He,Fei Ye,Wei-Qiang Chen,Jia-Wei Mei,Gan Wang*

TL;DR: 该文利用扫描隧道显微镜准粒子干涉与宏观磁阻测量，在界面超导体Bi₂Te₃/FeTe中观测到二重超导能隙对称性，该二重对称沿FeTe的Te-Te方向，削弱了双共线反铁磁序导致该对称性的可能，为理解该体系拓扑性质与配对机制提供关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导配对对称性是理解超导体微观超导机制的关键，而Bi₂Te₃/FeTe作为界面超导体其配对对称性尚不明确，需实验确定以厘清其拓扑性质及配对机理。

Method: 采用扫描隧道显微镜中的准粒子干涉（QPI）技术，结合宏观磁阻测量；分析能隙内外的QPI图案以判断能隙各向异性，并进行面内角度依赖磁阻与面内上临界场测量以检验对称性。

Conclusion: Bi₂Te₃/FeTe界面超导体具有二重超导能隙对称性，其取向沿Te-Te方向，从而削弱了双共线反铁磁序产生该对称性的可能性；该结果为进一步理解该体系拓扑性质并推动其配对机制的理论研究提供了关键依据。

Abstract: Superconducting pairing symmetry are crucial in understanding the microscopic superconducting mechanism of a superconductor. Here we report the observation of a twofold superconducting gap symmetry in an interfacial superconductor Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe, by employing quasiparticle interference (QPI) technique in scanning tunneling microscopy and macroscopic magnetoresistance measurements. The QPI patterns corresponding to energies inside and outside the gap reveal a clear anisotropic superconducting gap. Furthermore, both the in-plane angle-dependent magnetoresistance and in-plane upper critical field exhibit a clear twofold symmetry. This twofold symmetry align with the Te-Te direction in FeTe, which weakens the possible generation by bi-collinear antiferromagnetism order. Our finding provides key information in further understanding of the topological properties in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe superconducting system and propels further theoretical interests in the paring mechanism in the system.

</details>

### [61] [Hierarchical Multi-Agent Reasoning Systems for Knowledge-Grounded Material Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14123)
*Tarjei Paule Hage,Subhadeep Pal,Wei Lu,Yu-Chuan Hsu,Gayla Lyon,Jiezhu Jin,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出MARS——一个面向科学约束与制造约束下材料替代的分层多智能体推理系统，并在PFAS替代案例中验证其闭环工作流的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助科学发现需从"生成看似合理的想法"转向可复现、约束感知且能支撑实际工程决策的工作流；PFAS替代是同时要求功能性能、环境、实验与加工条件的严苛材料设计难题，现有方法难以胜任。

Method: MARS将替代任务分解为闭环流程：需求分析、有依据的候选生成、可制造性评估与工艺设计；融合检索增强生成、知识图谱推理、实验室可用材料数据库、中间结果持久化记忆以及拒绝感知的迭代反馈机制。

Result: 在PFAS替代案例中，MARS可生成结构化材料需求、识别有证据支撑的替代候选并设计实验室规模制造路线；多种子消融评估中，MARS在三个子系统上整体表现最优，且在固定骨干模型时增益依然保持。

Conclusion: 分层分解、知识 grounding 与可制造性感知的迭代优化能提升材料替代推理能力，其效果超越单纯依赖模型能力本身。

Abstract: AI-assisted scientific discovery increasingly seeks to move beyond generating plausible ideas toward reproducible, constraint-aware workflows that can support practical engineering decisions. We present MARS, a hierarchical Multi-Agent Reasoning System for knowledge-grounded material substitution under scientific and manufacturing constraints. We apply MARS to replace per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS), a demanding materials-design problem in which substitutes must satisfy functional performance, environmental, experimental, and processing requirements. MARS decomposes substitution into a closed-loop workflow comprising requirement analysis, grounded candidate generation, and manufacturability assessment with process design. The system integrates retrieval-augmented generation, knowledge-graph reasoning, a database of lab-available materials, persistent memory of intermediate results, and rejection-aware iterative feedback. In a PFAS-substitution case study, MARS generates structured material requirements, identifies an evidence-grounded substitute candidate, and develops a lab-scale manufacturing route. In a multi-seed ablation evaluation, MARS achieves the highest overall performance across all three subsystems, with gains that persist when the backbone model is held fixed. These results suggest that hierarchical decomposition, knowledge grounding, and manufacturability-aware refinement improve material-substitution reasoning beyond model capability alone.

</details>


### [62] [In-Situ TEM Observation of Solid-State Phase Evolution in High-Entropy Alloy Nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14147)
*Nirmal Kumar Katiyar,Noorbasha Bhavani Sai,Guilherme Gurian Dariani,Douglas S. Galvao,Pulickel M. Ajayan,Pedro Alves da Silva Autreto,Sriram Vijayan,Chandra Sekhar Tiwary*

Method: 对CoCrFeMnNi高熵合金纳米团簇在不同温度（室温、550 ℃、850 ℃）下进行高分辨透射电子显微镜（HRTEM）原位观察，并结合分子动力学模拟对实验现象进行机理验证。

Conclusion: 高熵合金纳米团簇在预熔化温度下表现出显著的原子迁移与相变行为，团簇粗化伴随成分异质化，表明该温区体系由焓驱动主导；HRTEM与分子动力学结合可有效揭示此类合金的原子尺度演化机制。

Abstract: The emergence of new materials has gone beyond conventional design principles. Among them are high entropy alloys (HEAs), in which multiple metallic elements coexist within a single unit cell. In this case, each atomic position in a solid material has a different chemical environment, leading to configurational entropy as high as that in the fully random or melted state, which has been frozen to a solid solution. However, the behaviour of such alloy systems at pre-melting temperatures is largely unknown, where atoms gain sufficient mobility and time to rearrange, leading to an enthalpic rather than an entropic interplay. This study demonstrated the phase transformation of HEA nanoclusters (CoCrFeMnNi) under high-resolution transmission electron microscopy (HRTEM) at different temperatures, including room temperature (RT), 550 °C, and 850 °C. The clustered nanoparticles coarsen into larger clusters, accompanied by compositional heterogeneity due to surface and lattice diffusion. This experimental finding of atomic mobility was further corroborated using molecular dynamics, which provides a detailed mechanistic description.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掺杂纳米晶的发光优化通常聚焦于激活剂本身，而基质阳离子比例这一设计变量被普遍忽视；作者希望证明该因素对发光性能同样具有决定性作用。

Method: 水热法生长Mn掺杂ZnGa2O4纳米晶（Mn:Zn = 4×10^-4），名义Zn/Ga比在0.30–0.75之间（化学计量为0.50），并在400–1100°C退火；采用X射线与紫外激发发光、EPR、Rietveld精修XRD、拉曼光谱和SEM对成分–温度矩阵进行系统表征。

Abstract: The luminescence of doped nanocrystals is usually optimised through the activator; here we show that the host cation ratio is an equally decisive, and largely overlooked, design variable. Mn-doped ZnGa$_2$O$_4$ nanocrystals (Mn:Zn = $4\times10^{-4}$) were grown hydrothermally at nominal Zn/Ga ratios between 0.30 and 0.75 (stoichiometric: 0.50) and post-annealed at 400--1100$^\circ$C, and the resulting composition--temperature matrix was mapped by X-ray- and UV-excited luminescence, EPR, Rietveld-refined X-ray diffraction, Raman spectroscopy and SEM. The as-grown crystals are phase-pure spinel at every composition and accommodate their off-stoichiometry internally as a point-defect inventory, reflected in a 0.4\% variation of the lattice parameter; on annealing, the excess gallium is exsolved as $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ in amounts set by the solution composition. The Mn$^{2+}$ emission separates into two optima, near-stoichiometric crystals annealed at 400--600$^\circ$C and Zn-excess crystals annealed at 1100$^\circ$C, the latter being twice as bright under X-ray excitation. Under X-ray and 365~nm excitation the two optima are separated by a minimum at 800$^\circ$C that coincides with a collapse of the Mn$^{2+}$ EPR population and has no counterpart in the Raman linewidths, identifying transient, defect-compensated oxidation of the dopant rather than lattice degradation as its origin. The most Zn-rich composition shows neither Mn$^{2+}$ nor Mn$^{4+}$ emission at any annealing stage, whereas its trace Cr$^{3+}$ emission is strong, indicating that Mn is rejected already during growth. Solution stoichiometry therefore acts on two levels, dopant capture and native-defect inventory, while annealing controls dopant valence and lattice order. The brightest high-temperature phosphor is thus not the nominally stoichiometric one, but the composition whose excess is consumed on its way to stoichiometry.

</details>

TL;DR: 该文用第一性原理计算研究了正交相Form III PTFE在1.503、3.452、5.056 GPa压力下热导率的各向异性响应，发现链轴方向热导率随压缩单调下降，而横向热导率在高压段回升，其机制源于热容-速度因子与有效寿命的差异变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩对聚合物热导率的影响在沿链方向与跨链方向上可能截然不同，其微观机制（声子速度与寿命）尚缺乏系统的定量分离与解释，因此需要研究PTFE这类典型聚合物的方向性压缩响应。

Method: 基于第一性原理计算正交相Form III PTFE的简谐与三次力常数，在单模弛豫时间近似（SMRTA）框架下计算1.503、3.452、5.056 GPa压力、100/200/300 K温度下的方向性热导率，并通过精确因式分解将热导率分解为"热容-速度因子"与加权有效寿命两部分进行定量分析。

Result: 在100、200、300 K下，链轴方向热导率在每个压力步长均下降；在3.452至5.056 GPa之间，两个横向分量回升，300 K时分别增加23.7%和13.7%，而纵向热导率下降14.5%。横向回升源于热容加权平方速度增大，与链间回复力增强一致；沿链方向则由更短的有效寿命主导，300 K下热导率加权的Normal与Umklapp散射率均增加。对比三个压力下相同三次力常数块，发现1.503 GPa时链间耦合已相当显著，并随压缩进一步增强。

Conclusion: 压缩对PTFE热导率的影响具有显著各向异性：链轴方向因有效声子寿命缩短而持续降低，横向方向在较高压力下因链间回复力增强、声子速度提升而回升。链间耦合在低压下已不可忽略并随压缩增强，说明跨链相互作用是理解聚合物压缩热输运的关键因素。

Abstract: Compression can change thermal conductivity differently along polymer chains and across them by altering phonon velocities and lifetimes. We examine this directional response in orthorhombic Form III polytetrafluoroethylene (PTFE) at calculated pressures of 1.503, 3.452, and 5.056 GPa, using first-principles harmonic and cubic force constants within the single-mode relaxation-time approximation (SMRTA). At 100, 200, and 300 K, the chain-axis conductivity decreases at every pressure step. Between 3.452 and 5.056 GPa, both transverse components recover: at 300 K they increase by 23.7% and 13.7%, while the longitudinal conductivity decreases by 14.5%. An exact factorization separates the conductivity into a heat-capacity--velocity factor and a weighted effective lifetime. The transverse recovery follows from larger heat-capacity-weighted squared velocities, consistent with stiffer interchain restoring forces. Along the chains, shorter effective lifetimes dominate, and both conductivity-weighted Normal and Umklapp rates increase at 300 K. Comparing the same cubic force-constant blocks at all three pressures, we find that interchain coupling is already substantial at 1.503 GPa and increases under compression.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑晶体绝缘体（如SnTe）的表面态受晶体对称性保护，区别于常规Z2拓扑绝缘体；其定量表征需借助磁输运提取相位相干长度与独立相干通道数，而薄膜织构与化学计量比对此类相干输运的影响尚需明确。

Method: 采用脉冲激光烧蚀在Bi2Te3缓冲的c面蓝宝石(Al2O3)上生长织构SnTe薄膜；通过沉积后退火工艺调控晶体织构，并进行晶体结构与输运表征：磁电阻（MR）在T < 10 K用强自旋轨道耦合极限下的Hikami-Larkin-Nagaoka (HLN)理论拟合，T > 20 K考察Kohler标度及经验温度依赖标度因子。

Result: 高温退火薄膜结晶性改善并增强沿[111]方向的取向；100 oC退火样品载流子浓度更高、电阻率更低，提示Sn空位作为本征受主缺陷，而200 oC退火样品趋近1:1化学计量比。T < 10 K时MR呈零场附近尖峰，指示弱反局域化；T > 20 K时MR为正且较小，偏离传统Kohler标度；用经验温度依赖标度因子可在50–250 K近似合并MR曲线，表明仅靠霍尔载流子浓度变化无法解释该标度行为。

Conclusion: 退火热处理显著影响SnTe薄膜中化学计量比、结晶性与相干输运之间的耦合关系，说明织构与缺陷工程是调控拓扑晶体绝缘体薄膜磁输运性质的关键手段。

Abstract: Topological crystalline insulators are distinguished from the conventional Z2 topological insulators by crystalline symmetry protection of the surface states. Quantitative characterization of these states requires magneto-transport measurements from which the phase coherence length and number of independent coherent channels can be extracted. Here we report such measurements on textured thin films of SnTe grown on Bi2Te3 buffered c-plane sapphire (Al2O3) by pulsed laser ablation. A post deposition annealing protocol was implemented to control the crystallographic texture, which reveals improved crystallinity and enhanced epitaxial alignment along the [111] direction in films annealed at elevated temperatures. The higher carrier density and lower resistivity seen in films annealed at 100 oC are suggestive of Sn vacancies which act as native acceptor defects, while films annealed at 200 oC approach the 1:1 stoichiometry. The magnetoresistance of all samples at T < 10 K characterized by a cusp centered around zero-field is suggestive of weak antilocalization and interpreted in the framework of the Hikami-Larkin-Nagaoka theory in the strong spin-orbit coupling limit. At T > 20 K, the MR is small and positive, and deviates from conventional Kohler scaling. An approximate collapse of the MR curves between 50 and 250 K is obtained using an empirical temperature-dependent scaling factor, indicating that changes in the Hall-derived carrier density alone cannot account for the observed scaling behavior. These results demonstrate that post growth annealing strongly influences the interplay between stoichiometry, crystallinity, and coherent transport in SnTe thin films.

</details>


### [66] [Benchmarking machine-learning interatomic potentials using classical force fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14239)
*Johannes Sandberg,Geneviève Dusson,Julien Lam*

TL;DR: 本文对比了线性原子团簇展开(ACE)、Behler-Parrinello高维神经网络势与图神经网络MACE势在拟合经典势(Lennard-Jones、Stillinger-Weber)时的表现，评估其在训练集规模、噪声鲁棒性、碳足迹及径向分布函数复现等方面的优劣。结果显示MACE精度最高且过拟合迹象少，但计算成本显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势种类繁多，但其在拟合任务中的优势与局限缺乏系统、可控的比较。以经典势为基准进行理性对比，可厘清不同模型在精度、鲁棒性与成本间的权衡，为计算材料科学中的模型选择提供依据。

Method: 以Lennard-Jones和Stillinger-Weber等经典势作为拟合目标，对比三种机器学习原子间势：线性原子团簇展开(ACE)、Behler-Parrinello高维神经网络势和基于图神经网络的MACE势。评估维度包括：训练数据集规模变化、对噪声的鲁棒性、碳足迹，以及作为代表性观测量复现径向分布函数的能力。

Result: 总体而言，MACE模型最精确地复现势能面，且几乎无过拟合迹象，但计算成本显著较高。其他模型在成本与精度上表现出不同的权衡特征。

Conclusion: 不同机器学习原子间势在精度与计算成本之间存在明显权衡；MACE精度最优但代价高，凸显了计算材料科学中模型选择的重要问题。

Abstract: We propose a rational comparison between several well-known machine-learning interatomic potentials (linear atomic cluster expansion, the Behler-Parrinello high-dimensional neural network potential, and the graph neural network-based MACE potential) by testing their performance for fitting classical potentials such as the Lennard-Jones and Stillinger-Weber potential. With this we assess their advantages and limitations regarding several aspects: varying training dataset size, robustness to noise, carbon footprint, as well as the ability to reproduce the radial distribution function as an example observable. Overall the MACE models are shown to most accurately reproduce the potential energy surface, with little sign of overfitting, but at a significant computational cost, highlighting important questions regarding the choice of models in computational materials science.

</details>

Abstract: Efficient and durable phosphors made from abundant elements underpin energy-saving solid-state lighting, near-infrared light sources for sensing, and X-ray and biomedical imaging. Co-doping is a standard route to brighter phosphors, yet the fate of the co-dopants is rarely established. The octahedral Mn lifts the inversion symmetry of the $\mathrm{Cr^{3+}}$ site and strengthens its parity-forbidden absorption, enhancing the $\mathrm{Cr^{3+}}$ emission without energy transfer. The population of the defect complex follows the law of mass action, and above a threshold Mn concentration, Mn re-partitions into EPR-silent octahedral configurations that quench both emissions. Concentration optima in co-doped phosphors can thus reflect defect speciation, not only energy-transfer efficiency, which opens a structural design handle for brighter emitters.

### [68] [A Solution-Processed Ti/Pt/NiOx/Ag RRAM Device with Multiple Forming-Free Non-Volatile Intermediate States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14322)
*Abhrajit Roy,Chopali Chanchal Sahu,Rudraksh Sangore,Raman Hissariya,Poulomi Chakrabarty,Harsh Roniyar,Merlyne De Souza,Debanjan Bhowmik*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交叉阵列上实现的人工神经网络（ANN）比传统计算单元能耗更低，而溶液法过渡金属氧化物RRAM因成本低、易制备、稳定性好且具备合适忆阻特性，适合作为突触器件。但已有报道（含溶液法与溅射法RRAM）大多需要forming过程才能表现出非易失性，forming会带来变异性、需要高压脉冲并增加阵列设计复杂度。

Method: 器件的RRAM结构为Ti/Pt底电极、溶液法沉积的NiO阻变层、Ag顶电极；测试其无需forming的双极阻变（HRS/LRS切换）以及脉冲驱动的LTP/LTD特性；通过30个中间阻态评估突触权重分辨率并测试状态保持性；利用XPS确认材料叠层中氧空位的存在；最后将实测LTP-LTD特性校准到MLP模型（MNIST数据集）和CNN模型（CIFAR-10数据集）的突触权重更新中进行分类验证。

Result: 器件实现无需forming的HRS-LRS双极切换及脉冲驱动的LTP/LTD，可在HRS与LRS之间获得30个中间突触态（约5位权重分辨率），其中多数状态在至少1000秒内保持稳定，表明其非易失性及在ANN交叉阵列中存储突触权重的适用性；XPS证实材料叠层中存在氧空位；基于实测LTP-LTD校准的MLP与CNN分别在MNIST和CIFAR-10上实现成功分类。

Conclusion: 无需forming的NiO基RRAM可降低器件变异性、免除高压脉冲需求并简化交叉阵列设计，同时提供多态、非易失的突触行为，支持与实测特性校准的ANN分类任务，显示了溶液法制备RRAM作为突触器件的应用潜力。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks (ANN) implemented on crossbar arrays of non-volatile synaptic devices are known to consume much less energy compared to that implemented on conventional computing units. Solution-processed resistive random-access memory (RRAM) devices based on transition-metal oxides are suitable as such synaptic devices because of their cheap and easy fabrication method, stability, and suitable memristive properties. Here, in NiO-based RRAM devices with Ti/Pt as the bottom electrode and Ag as the top electrode (NiO layer deposited through solution processing), we experimentally report forming-free binary switching between a high resistance state (HRS) and a low resistance state (LRS) as well as forming-free pulse-driven modulation (long-term potentiation (LTP), long-term depression (LTD)) through 30 intermediate synaptic states between HRS and LRS (around 5-bit synaptic weight resolution). On the contrary, most prior experimental reports (including reports both on solution-processing-based and sputtering-based RRAM devices) require forming to exhibit non-volatility. Further, we confirm the presence of oxygen vacancies in our materials stack using X-ray photo-electron spectroscopy (XPS). </details>

Abstract: General-purpose machine-learning force fields (MLFFs) for materials are now trained on the order of $100$ million structures, assuming transferability requires such scale. We test this premise on OMat24, the largest available materials dataset, by training MACE models on subsets of $6.5$ million atoms, less than $1\%$ of OMat24, selected in three complementary ways: at random, by latent-space clustering of a pretrained MLFF (DIRECT), and by a new model-free method maximizing the diversity of discrete, interpretable local environments (EnvTok). This near-independence from the selection strategy shows that chemical environment diversity, not size, defines OMat24's information content: the remaining $99\%$ of structures are largely redundant. Broad sampling of chemical space thus remains essential, but reference labels are needed only for structurally and chemically distinct configurations. Using atomic positions and species, optionally augmented by magnetic moments, EnvTok identifies these before any labelling or training, yielding compact, architecture-independent datasets. This hundredfold reduction makes hybrid-DFT or higher-level relabelling of general-purpose training sets feasible, and frees computational budget for extending environmental coverage and multi-fidelity learning.

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) polar oxides with built-in electric fields offer promising opportunities for nanoscale electronics, optoelectronics, and surface charge engineering. However, their discovery remains challenging due to the enormous structural complexity of nonlayered oxide networks. Here, we combine inverse structural design with a machine-learning interatomic potential and first-principles calculations to explore 2D oxide configurations. Using Bi2O3 as a representative system, we identify two previously unreported polar monolayers with tetragonal and orthorhombic symmetries. Both structures exhibit vertically separated Bi and O sublayers, giving rise to pronounced intrinsic polarity with Berry-phase 2D polarizations of 34.12 and 37.15 pC/m, respectively. Notably, the o-Bi2O3 monolayer exhibits one of the largest reported out-of-plane polarizations among 2D oxides. Both monolayers exhibit dynamical stability, finite-temperature structural integrity, and wide band gaps (~3.8 eV). The large polarization originates from dimensionality-induced reconstruction of the nonlayered Bi-O network, which breaks local coordination symmetry and produces out-of-plane charge redistribution, while the stereochemically active Bi 6s2 lone pair further favors asymmetric Bi-O bonding. These findings suggest dimensionality-induced local-coordination symmetry breaking as a promising design motif for high-polarization 2D oxides, and demonstrate the utility of combining inverse design with machine-learning-assisted screening to explore unconventional polar phases in nonlayered oxide networks.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 从基于能量的方差缩减BTE表述出发，建立偏差声子能量的空间展开与宏观热传导方程之间的关系，推导出热导率以偏差声子粒子的均方位移表示；采用涨落型蒙特卡洛方法，无需施加热库，对室温晶体硅的块体、方形纳米线与各向异性多晶纳米结构进行模拟。

Abstract: We develop a fluctuation based Monte Carlo method for evaluating thermal conductivity within the phonon Boltzmann transport equation (BTE) framework. Starting from the energy based variance reduced formulation of the BTE, we derive a relation between the spatial spreading of deviational phonon energy and the macroscopic heat conduction equation. The resulting formulation expresses thermal conductivity in terms of the mean square displacement of deviational phonon particles, without imposing thermal reservoirs. Consequently, thermal conductivity along multiple directions can be evaluated from a single simulation. The method is demonstrated for crystalline silicon at room temperature in bulk, square nanowires, and anisotropic polycrystalline nanostructures. The calculated thermal conductivities agree with theoretical predictions, including the size dependence induced by phonon boundary scattering and the mode-resolved contributions. For anisotropic polycrystalline structures, the method simultaneously resolves the directional thermal conductivities and captures their dependence on grain morphology. These results demonstrate the validity of the proposed fluctuation-based approach for evaluating thermal conductivity across different transport regimes and structural configurations.

### [72] [A Fisher-Geometric Approach to Supercell Pair Distribution Function Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14597)
*Dawei Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无矩阵、Fisher几何的优化方法，将观测似然的信息度量拉回原子坐标空间，用于粉末PDF精修原子超胞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粉末对分布函数(PDF)精修原子超胞时，需用少量径向测量优化大量耦合原子坐标。反向模式微分虽可提供梯度，但标准一阶优化器未显式利用耦合的PDF灵敏度来预条件坐标更新。

Method: 采用无矩阵的Fisher几何方法，将观测似然信息度量拉回原子坐标；更新按PDF响应与实验不确定度加权集体位移。在各向同性阻尼下，通过求解M个PDF通道的阻尼线性系统获得更新，并用自动微分计算Jacobian-向量与向量-Jacobian乘积，不构造完整Jacobian或坐标曲率矩阵。可处理相关高斯误差及稳健似然更新。

Result: 在高斯测量误差下，该更新恢复经典Levenberg-Marquardt精修效果，同时避免稠密坐标空间分解；同一框架可扩展至相关高斯误差。

Conclusion: 该方法将似然感知的集体更新与无矩阵反向传播相结合，实现大型原子超胞的直接二阶精修。

Abstract: Refining atomistic supercells against powder pair distribution functions (PDFs) requires optimizing many coupled atomic coordinates using a much smaller set of radial measurements. Reverse-mode differentiation makes gradients accessible for large systems, but standard first-order optimizers do not explicitly use coupled PDF sensitivities to precondition coordinate updates. We introduce a matrix-free, Fisher-geometric approach that pulls the information metric of the observation likelihood back onto atomic coordinates. The resulting update weights collective displacements according to their PDF response and experimental uncertainty. Under isotropic damping, the update is obtained by solving a damped linear system over $M$ PDF channels, using automatic differentiation to evaluate Jacobian--vector and vector--Jacobian products without constructing the full Jacobian or coordinate curvature matrix. For Gaussian measurement errors, the update recovers classical Levenberg--Marquardt refinement while avoiding dense coordinate-space factorization. The same computational framework accommodates correlated Gaussian errors and robust likelihood-based updates. This approach combines likelihood-aware collective updates with matrix-free backpropagation for direct second-order refinement of large atomistic supercells.

</details>


### [73] [Enhancing Quasi-Two-Dimensional Electronic Structure in Bi2O2Se via Chlorine Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14619)

Conclusion: Cl取代可在保留Bi2O2Se优异电子性质的前提下增强其二维特性，拓展其在光电子学、纳米电子学及基础研究中的应用潜力，并确立"空间解耦"作为调控准二维量子体系精细电子结构的可行策略。

Abstract: Bismuth oxyselenide (Bi2O2Se) has emerged as a leading semiconductor candidate for next-generation electronics and quantum phenomena research. However, its strong ionic interlayer bonding of Se layers fundamentally restricts the full potential of this otherwise promising quasi-two-dimensional (2D) material. In this work, we demonstrate chlorine substitution as an effective strategy to introduce van der Waals gaps and modify the electronic structure of the system. Through comprehensive transport and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) measurements on Bi6O6Se2Cl2 single crystal, an optimal Cl-doping compound, we show that the key electronic features, including indirect band gap, high electron mobility, and carrier effective mass, are well preserved, while the 2D character of the system is sufficiently enhanced. First-principles calculations attribute this selective tunability to the unique spatial separation between conductive Bi-O layers and dopant Se/Cl layers, which enables independent optimization of interlayer coupling and intralayer transport. Remarkably, our theoretical calculations combined with experimental observations project a broad bandgap engineering across a wide range (0.8 ~ 3.5 eV) within the Bi2O2Se system. Our findings not only expand the application potential of Bi2O2Se-based materials in optoelectronics, nanoelectronics, and fundamental research but also highlight spatial decoupling as a viable strategy for modifying detailed electronic structure of quasi-2D quantum systems.

</details>


### [74] [Crossed Electric Spin Generation in Multilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14668)
*Xukun Feng,Wei Du,Jin Cao,Qian Niu,Shengyuan A. Yang,Cong Xiao*

Conclusion: 该理论为2D材料中的全电控自旋操控提供了一条基于贝里曲率的新路径，凸显准二维体系杂化贝里曲率与自旋-层-轨道耦合的独特价值，有望推动二维自旋电子学器件的电学调控设计。

Abstract: We propose a crossed electric spin generation effect in multilayer structures. This effect combines the advantages of in-plane and gate electric fields for manipulating spin, and unveils a nice quantum geometric structure in terms of the underlying hybrid Berry curvatures that are unique to quasi-2D systems and manifest inherent spin-layer-orbital coupling. Combining our theory with first-principles calculations, we predict sizable spin generation in 2D ferromagnetic Fe3GeTe2 and altermagnetic V2Te2O. ### [75] [Finite-temperature adiabatic-connection reduced density-matrix functional theory: a unified framework for i-DMFT and TAO-DFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14706)
*Jingang Han,Huanjing Gong,Kai Luo,Hongming Weng,Xinguo Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出有限温度绝热连接约化密度矩阵泛函理论（FT-AC-RDMFT），将i-DMFT与TAO-DFT统一为同一框架下的不同约化，并推广至杂化与半局域泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确描述化学键断裂需要捕捉强/静态电子关联，这对单参考自洽场方法（如Hartree-Fock和Kohn-Sham DFT）是重大挑战。分数占据可显著改善该问题（如i-DMFT与TAO-DFT），但二者理论框架不同、带有半经验性，且相互关系不清。

Method: 建立FT-AC-RDMFT：由变分原理直接导出自然轨道的有效本征方程与Fermi-Dirac占据数；通过热绝热连接将i-DMFT的经验熵系数与辅助无相互作用系统的特征温度联系起来。i-DMFT对应基于精确交换的约化，TAO-DFT则对应以密度型交换关联泛函替代精确交换的约化。

Result: 数值计算证实i-DMFT在相差一常数意义下与该框架的精确交换实现一致；有限温度占据带来的解离行为改善可从精确交换推广至杂化和半局域密度泛函。对强关联V2O3，有限温度占据显著降低了严重高估的非磁性LDA体模量，使其接近实验值。

Conclusion: FT-AC-RDMFT为分子与固态体系中基于有限温度占据的电子结构方法提供了共同理论基础。

Abstract: Accurately describing chemical bond breaking requires capturing strong/static electron correlation, a major challenge for single-reference self-consistent-field methods such as Hartree--Fock and Kohn--Sham density-functional theory (DFT). Fractional occupations substantially improve this situation, as demonstrated by information-density-matrix-functional theory (i-DMFT) and thermally-assisted-occupation DFT (TAO-DFT). Although useful, they are formulated within rather different theoretical frameworks, are somewhat semi-empirical, and their relationship remains unclear. Here we introduce finite-temperature adiabatic-connection reduced density-matrix functional theory (FT-AC-RDMFT), in which i-DMFT and TAO-DFT emerge as different reductions of a unified framework. The effective eigenvalue equation for natural orbitals and the Fermi--Dirac occupation numbers follow directly from the variational principle, while a thermal adiabatic connection relates the empirical entropy coefficient of i-DMFT to a characteristic temperature of the auxiliary non-interacting system. Within this framework, i-DMFT is the exact-exchange-based reduction, whereas TAO-DFT results when exact exchange is replaced by a density-based exchange-correlation functional. Numerical calculations confirm that i-DMFT, apart from a constant, coincides with the exact-exchange realization of the framework, and show that the improved dissociation behavior from finite-temperature occupations extends beyond exact exchange to hybrid and semilocal density functionals. For strongly correlated V2O3, finite-temperature occupations substantially reduce the severely overestimated non-magnetic LDA bulk modulus, bringing it close to experiment. These results establish FT-AC-RDMFT as a common theoretical foundation for finite-temperature occupation-based electronic-structure methods in molecular and solid-state systems.

</details>


### [76] [Inverse Designing Reconfigurable Piezoelectric Tensors via Architectural and Modal Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14761)
*Anish Satpati,Marco Maurizi,Hyunmoon Cho,Victor Couedel,Nicolas Mule,Xiaoyu,Zheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出基于图表示的通用框架，通过调控宏观对称性、局部极化与结构单元变形来控制等效压电张量全部系数，并发现频率选择性张量重构，实现了体积缩减90%的多自由度致动器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然晶体的原子周期性与能量稳定性将压电对称性限制在20种点群各向异性内，难以自由调控压电张量的各个系数，制约了压电器件在方向与变形模式上的可编程性。

Method: 建立基于图表示的通用框架，将有效压电张量分解到结构单元，解析宏观对称性、局部极化与结构单元变形三者的协同作用，实现系数溯源与定向几何调控，并考察激励频率对主导系数的选择性重构。

Result: 可将每个压电系数追溯至起增强或抵消作用的结构单元并通过几何调整加以控制；发现单一结构在不同激励频率下主导压电系数发生切换；据此实现单块集成的多自由度致动器，体积较传统多叠层组件减少90%。

Conclusion: 该架构化设计范式突破了自然晶体对称性对压电张量的限制，使单一压电结构可在多方向、多变形状态下实现可编程传感、致动与模态转换。

Abstract: Piezoelectricity arises from the coupling between electric polarization and mechanical deformation, described by a third-rank tensor whose coefficients are constrained by crystal symmetry. In natural crystals, atomic periodicity and energetic stability restrict accessible symmetries, limited to 20 distinct anisotropies that can exist. Here we establish a general framework, based on graph representation, for controlling the full effective piezoelectric tensor through architecture by resolving how macroscopic symmetry, local polarization and deformation of structural elements act together. This enables each coefficient to be traced to the members that reinforce or cancel it and be controlled through targeted geometric changes. We further uncover frequency-selective tensor reconfiguration, in which excitation frequency switches the dominant piezoelectric coefficients of a single architecture. These principles open a broader class of applications in which a single piezoelectric architecture can deliver programmable sensing, actuation and mode conversion across multiple directions and deformation states. We realize a multi-degree-of-freedom actuator that integrates distinct actuation modes in one block and reduces volume by 90% relative to conventional multi-stack assemblies.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究hBN在弯曲金属基底上生长时诱导晶面化的机制，理解覆盖层-基底相互作用与洁净表面自由能对晶面稳定性的相对贡献。

Conclusion: 部分晶面因更强的覆盖层-基底相互作用而稳定，另一些则由洁净表面能量学主导；仅靠热力学论证无法完全解释该过程。

Abstract: This work combines experimental investigations and first-principles calculations to study hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) growth on a curved Pt(331) substrate. Low-energy electron diffraction (LEED) and scanning tunnelling microscopy (STM) were employed to investigate the structural properties, observing that hBN growth led to faceting with several stable facets: Pt(111), Pt(221), Pt(441), Pt(881), and Pt(110). Additionally, the electronic properties were examined by X-ray photoemission spectroscopy (XPS) and near-edge X-ray absorption fine structure (NEXAFS), revealing an increase in the hBN-substrate interaction strength, from the weakest at the Pt(111) to the strongest at the Pt(110). Density functional theory (DFT) calculations confirm that the selective faceting mechanism is induced by hBN, which changes the order of relative stability of the uncovered facets. Although a complete understanding of the process is not achievable using only thermodynamical arguments, the comparison between hBN adhesion energies and clean surface free energies clearly indicates that some facets are stabilized with respect to others by stronger overlayer-substrate interaction, whereas for others the clean surface energetics plays the dominant role.

</details>


### [78] [Pressure-transferable universal machine-learning potentials: the WBM-MatPES benchmark and Tuga-FF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14891)
*Pierre-Paul De Breuck,Thalis H. B. da Silva,Antoine Loew,Silvana Botti,Miguel A. L. Marques*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 发布WBM-MatPES数据集与高压测试基准，揭示现有MatPES势函数在150 GPa下误差骤增，并提出图变换器模型Tuga-FF，在常压和150 GPa下均取得最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势有望替代昂贵的从头计算，但其精度与可迁移性受限于训练数据的可得性，尤其缺乏系统的高压基准来评估势函数在极端条件下的表现。

Method: 发布WBM-MatPES数据集（18.2万个结构，采用一致的MatPES设置重算），提供两条不同深度的参考凸包及最高150 GPa六个压力下的测试集；在该基准上系统评测五个已发表的MatPES势函数，并训练图变换器模型Tuga-FF，其训练数据同时涵盖常压与高压构型。

Result: 五个MatPES势在25 GPa下误差一致在12 meV/atom以内，但在150 GPa下误差骤增至0.4–5 eV/atom；Tuga-FF在常压下误差为13.6 meV/atom，为基准中最优，在150 GPa下误差仅21 meV/atom。

Conclusion: 该基准可系统地在同一理论层级上对势函数进行排序，复现真实的材料发现工作流，将推动社区训练更高精度模型与构建更丰富数据集，整体促进机器学习原子间势的发展。

Abstract: Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials hold the promise to replace costly ab initio calculations in materials science workflows. However, the accuracy and transferability of current models are limited by the availability of training data. We release WBM-MatPES, a dataset of 182 thousand structures recomputed with consistent MatPES settings, together with two reference convex hulls of different depths and test sets at six pressures up to 150 GPa. These datasets reproduce a realistic discovery workflow and allow potentials to be ranked systematically at this level of theory, similar to the Matbench Discovery leaderboard at the Materials Project settings. In this benchmark, five published MatPES potentials agree within 12 meV/atom at 25 GPa, yet their errors increase dramatically to 0.4-5 eV/atom at 150 GPa. To address this limitation, we develop Tuga-FF, a graph transformer trained on datasets spanning ambient and high-pressure configurations alike. Tuga-FF is the most accurate model in the benchmark at ambient pressure, with an energy error of 13.6 meV/atom, and maintains an error of just 21 meV/atom at 150 GPa. We believe this benchmark will encourage the community to train higher-accuracy models and generate richer datasets, advancing machine-learned interatomic potentials as a whole.

</details>


### [79] [Spiral Edge States in Topological 2D Sb2Te3 Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14905)
