<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用量子几何调控边缘态能带的新途径，并揭示螺旋几何引入的边界条件如何改变二维晶体边缘态色散。

Method: 结合低损耗电子能量损失谱（EELS）、磁力显微镜（MFM）与传统的载流子输运测量手段。

Result: 晶体结构支持两个螺旋且打结的边缘导电通道内的空间连续边缘态，通道通过拓扑缺陷相连；在0.155 eV处识别出一个明显的激发，似与带隙内两个孤立态相关，且该态局域于晶体边缘。

Conclusion: 通过操纵体系量子几何来修饰边缘态能带，可能为二维拓扑系统的应用开辟新途径。

Abstract: This work explores the edge state conduction properties of twinned and spiraled, 2D Sb2Te3 topological insulator crystals. We show the structure supports spatially continuous edge states within two spiral and knotted edge conduction channels. The channels are connected through a topological defect. This geometry introduces boundary conditions into the 2D crystal that modifies the edge state dispersion. Using a combination of low loss Electron Energy Loss Spectroscopy, Magnetic Force Microscopy and traditional carrier transport measurements we identify a distinct excitation at 0.155 eV in the spiral that seems to be related to two isolated states within the gap. This state is localized to the crystal edges. The modification of edge state bands using manipulation of a systems quantum geometry may open new avenues for the use of 2D topological systems.

</details>


### [80] [Pressure-Driven Evolution of the Physical Properties of Layered Y2GaB and Y2InB MAB Phases: A Comparative First-Principles Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14908)
*M. Rabi Us Sani Shapon,Md. Asif Afzal,Jubair Hossan Abir,Raihana Shams Islam,Saleh Hasan Naqib*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用第一性原理计算对比MAB相Y2GaB与Y2InB在0–20 GPa下的结构、弹性、热物理、电子、键合、光学与振动响应，确定Y2GaB在0–10 GPa稳定、Y2InB仅在常压稳定，并阐明其多带金属性与混合离子-共价键合特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAB相为纳米层状三元硼化物，其面内强M-B键与层间弱相互作用共同决定稳定性与功能性；静水压可调控这两类相互作用。但Y2GaB与Y2InB在压力下的稳定性窗口及取代驱动的结构-性能关系尚不明确，需要系统对比研究。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，在0–20 GPa范围内对比Y2GaB与Y2InB的结构、弹性、热物理、电子结构、键合、光学和振动性质；通过弹性判据评估力学稳定性，分析费米面片数与自旋轨道耦合效应，考察电荷密度与轨道重叠以判断键合类型，并用单胞与扩胞声子谱检验动力学稳定性。

Result: 弹性判据显示Y2GaB在0、5、10 GPa稳定，Y2InB仅常压稳定。在Y2GaB稳定区间内，加压提高弹性模量、声速、德拜温度和晶格热导率，同时降低弹性各向异性。两者均为多带金属，自旋轨道耦合改变局部色散但不打开能隙。Y2GaB在0–10 GPa保持9个费米面片，Y2InB常压下为8个，无压力诱导的拓扑重构。电荷密度与重叠分析表明Y-B为混合离子-共价键，处于离域金属背景中。光学参数呈各向异性。单胞声子谱在0与10 GPa下频率为正，但扩胞计算显示低频敏感性。

Conclusion: 研究确立了Y2GaB与Y2InB力学可行的压力窗口，并厘清了取代（Ga/In）驱动的结构-性能关系，为这类硼化物在压力下的应用与设计提供依据。

Abstract: MAB phases are nanolaminated ternary borides in which strong in-plane M-B bonds and weaker interlayer interactions drive their distinctive stability and functionality. Hydrostatic pressure offers a direct route to tune the in-plane and interlayer interactions that govern the stability and functionality of layered Y2GaB and Y2InB borides. Here, first-principles calculations are used to compare their structural, elastic, thermophysical, electronic, bonding, optical, and vibrational responses from 0 to 20 GPa. Elastic criteria show that Y2GaB is stable at 0, 5, and 10 GPa, whereas Y2InB is stable only at ambient pressure. Within the stable Y2GaB range, compression increases the elastic moduli, acoustic velocities, Debye temperature, and lattice thermal conductivity while reducing the elastic anisotropy. Both compounds are multiband metals and spin-orbit coupling modifies local dispersions without opening a gap. Y2GaB retains nine Fermi-surface sheets from 0 to 10 GPa, whereas Y2InB has eight at ambient pressure, with no pressure-induced topological reconstruction. Charge-density and overlap analyses indicate mixed ionic-covalent Y-B bonding within a delocalized metallic background. Optical parameters are anisotropic. Unit-cell phonon spectra contain positive frequencies at 0 and 10 GPa, although expanded-supercell calculations reveal low-frequency sensitivity. These results establish mechanically viable pressure windows and clarify substitution-driven structure-property relationships for these borides.

</details>


### [81] [Investigation on Thermal Stability of Epi-GeSn Directly on Si (001)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14947)
*Tharundev V,Dushyant Singh,Vigneshswari SR,Subha Maity,Prince Rathor,Krista R Khiangte*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究了直接生长在Si(001)上的亚稳态Ge1-xSnx外延层（约4.5 at.% Sn）的结构与热稳定性，发现70 nm薄膜表面稳定，而140 nm薄膜严重退化，热稳定性由外延生长初始缺陷结构决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GeSn/Si异质结构在光电应用中具有潜力，但其亚稳态特性和热稳定性对器件制备至关重要。作者希望理解直接生长GeSn/Si异质结构的热稳定性机制及其与初始缺陷结构的关系，为GeSn基光电器件的设计提供依据。

Method: 采用高分辨X射线衍射（HRXRD）和拉曼光谱研究Ge1-xSnx外延层（约4.5 at.% Sn）直接生长在Si(001)上的结构性质；利用温度依赖拉曼光谱（532 nm激发，表面加权）和扫描电子显微镜在高达813 K评估热稳定性。

Result: 70 nm和140 nm薄膜均完全弛豫，较厚薄膜存在小的拉伸应变（+0.09%）。70 nm薄膜的拉曼响应和表面形貌高度稳定；140 nm薄膜严重退化，Ge-Ge纵向光学声子模显著衰减和展宽，并伴随表面Sn偏析增强。

Conclusion: 直接GeSn/Si异质结构的热稳定性由外延生长过程中形成的初始缺陷结构决定，这为理解GeSn基光电器件的制备提供了关键设计规则。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate the structural properties and thermal stability of metastable Ge1-xSnx epilayers (~4.5 at.% Sn) grown directly on Si(001) substrates by high-resolution X-ray diffraction (HRXRD) and Raman spectroscopy. Our results demonstrate that both 70 and 140 nm films are fully relaxed, with the thicker film exhibiting a small tensile strain (+0.09%). The thermal stability of the heterostructures was evaluated up to 813 K using temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy and scanning electron microscopy. The surface-weighted Raman response (532 nm excitation) and surface morphology of the 70 nm film remain highly stable. In contrast, the 140 nm film undergoes severe degradation, characterized by significant attenuation and broadening of the Ge-Ge longitudinal optical phonon mode, alongside enhanced surface Sn segregation. These findings demonstrate that the thermal stability of direct GeSn/Si heterostructures is governed by the initial defect structure established during epitaxial growth, providing key design rules for understanding the fabrication of GeSn-based optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [82] [EpNet-Si: Atomic Form Factors Learned from Band Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14992)
*Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出EpNet-Si：用等变网络从局域环境预测硅原子平面波形状因子，仅6195参数、77个参考DFT胞训练，即可端到端通过特征值求解器预测能带，在畸变与未见结构上达到meV级精度并复现有效质量、形变势等物性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验赝势方法（EPM）每个k点只需一次对角化且无需自洽，计算高效；但其固定的原子形状因子与具体晶体结构绑定，无法在不同结构间迁移，限制了作为通用快速能带计算工具的适用性。作者希望保留EPM的效率优势，同时让形状因子随局域结构自适应，从而把EPM扩展为可用于现代电子结构计算的快速替代方案。

Method: 提出EpNet-Si：以等变神经网络从每个原子的局域环境预测其平面波形状因子，形式为“物种势 + 单极、偶极、四极修正”，并通过学习到的路由（router）在原子密度上混合，另加s/p非局域投影子。模型仅6195个参数，训练时将网络嵌入特征值求解器进行端到端优化，监督信号为仅77个参考胞的DFT能带能量。

Result: 在训练结构类型的231个畸变胞中，142个有带隙胞的能带与带隙平均误差为15 meV和8 meV；在192个未见结构中，165个有带隙胞的平均误差为38 meV和49 meV。由EpNet-Si导出的有效质量、带隙压力系数、形变势以及费米能级处态密度均与参考DFT计算一致。

Conclusion: 经典EPM在少量参考数据上拟合后，可通过等变神经网络化的结构依赖形状因子扩展为快速能带计算方案；EpNet-Si以极小参数与训练集实现meV级精度并复现关键物性，表明EPM可作为现代电子能带结构计算的快速替代路径。

Abstract: The empirical pseudopotential method (EPM) computes band structures with one diagonalization per $k$ point and no self-consistency, but its fixed atomic form factors do not transfer between crystal structures. To address this limitation, we introduce EpNet-Si, an EPM for silicon in which an equivariant network predicts each atom's plane-wave form factor from its local environment: a species potential plus monopole, dipole and quadrupole corrections, mixed by a learned router over atomic densities, together with an $s/p$ nonlocal projector. With 6{,}195 parameters, the model is trained through the eigensolver on reference density functional theory (DFT) band energies of only 77 cells. Among 231 distortions of the trained structure types, the 142 gapped cells have mean band and gap errors of 15 and 8\,meV; among 192 unseen structures, the 165 gapped cells have mean errors of 38 and 49\,meV. Physical properties derived from EpNet-Si, including effective masses, gap pressure coefficients, deformation potentials and the density of states at the Fermi level, agree with those obtained from the reference DFT calculations. These results suggest that the classical EPM, fitted to a small set of reference data, can be extended into a fast alternative for modern electronic band-structure calculations.

</details>


### [83] [Framework for an AI-Ready ARPES Community Database](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15236)
*Zhi-Xun Shen,Jin Gene Wong,Karen Lei,Leon Garcia,Felix Baumberger,Sergey Borisenko,Aaron Bostwick,Ting Cao,Tanny Chavez,Yulin Chen,Monika Choudhary,Andrea Damascelli,Dan Dessau,Thomas P. Devereaux,Sandy A. Ekahana,Alexei Fedorov,Atsushi Fujimori,Yucheng Guo,M. Zahid Hasan,Makoto Hashimoto,Yu He,Philip Hofmann,Zahid Hussain,Harold Y. Hwang,Zhurun Ji,Chunjing Jia,Timur K. Kim,Phil D. C. King,Takeshi Kondo,Alessandra Lanzara,Chun Lin,Donghui Lu,Matteo Michiardi,Sung-Kwan Mo,Brian Moritz,Laurenz Rettig,Eli Rotenberg,Takafumi Sato,Kyle M. Shen,Jonathan A. Sobota,Inna Vishik,Shuolong Yang,Ming Yi,Timothy Zaklama*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该观点文章基于一次社区研讨会，提出为ARPES建立共享数据生态系统，以解决当前以论文为中心的工作流导致数据碎片化的困境，并分近、中、远三个时间尺度阐述其收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝聚态物理有望从人工智能中深度受益，但当前的数据组织方式阻碍了这一进程。作为材料性质电子结构首要表征手段，ARPES积累了四十余年的谱学数据，然而以论文发表为中心的工作流仅保留了实验记录中高度筛选的极小部分，大量测量及其上下文信息流失。共享数据生态系统可将这些碎片化测量转化为持久、集体的科学记忆，为可复现性、数据复用及AI驱动发现奠定基础。

Method: 本文属Perspective（观点/展望）类文章。作者报告近期一次社区研讨会的成果，提出构建共享ARPES数据生态系统的实践路径，梳理现有科学数据库的经验教训、AI在ARPES中的应用现状，并就数据组织、访问、署名、治理及分阶段试点项目等方面总结社区共识。

Result: 文章提出收益按三个时间尺度展开：近期，标准化、可检索、可引用的数据集可在不依赖AI的情况下强化分析流程；中期，经社区验证、跨实验室运行的工具可实现更广泛的数据复用与日益闭环的自动化实验；远期，多样化的实测电子谱语料库可支撑量子物质的基础模型与新发现。

Conclusion: 构建共享ARPES数据生态系统是一项可行且必要的举措：短期即产生可复现性与复用价值，中期推动自动化与跨实验室协作，长期为量子物质的AI基础模型提供数据支撑。文章给出了一条分阶段、以社区共识为基础的实施路径。

Abstract: Condensed matter stands to gain profoundly from widespread adoption of artificial intelligence (AI). However, its current means of data organization impedes this endeavor. As a premier measure of electronic structure underlying material properties, Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES) has accumulated over four decades of spectra, yet the community's publication-centered workflow preserves only a highly selected fraction of its experimental record. A shared data ecosystem could transform these fragmented measurements and their contexts into a persistent, collective scientific memory, creating immediate value for reproducibility and reuse while laying the foundation for AI-enabled discovery. In this Perspective, we report outcomes of a recent community workshop and propose a practical path toward such an ecosystem. Its benefits unfold along three timescales. In the near term, standardized, searchable, and citable datasets strengthen analysis pipelines independently of AI. In the intermediate future, community-validated tools operating across laboratories could enable broader reuse and increasingly closed-loop automated experiments. In the long term, a diverse corpus of measured electronic spectra could support foundation models for quantum matter. Looking forward, a diverse corpus of measured electronic spectra could support foundation models for quantum matter and new discoveries. We discuss lessons from existing scientific databases, the current state of AI in ARPES, as well as areas of community convergence on data organization, access, credit, governance, and a staged pilot program.

</details>


### [84] [DiffGA: Steering Frozen Diffusion Models for Materials Design via Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14999)
*Shivang Agarwal,Rodrigo Wang,Kevin Ryczko,Aayush R. Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: DiffGA：将进化搜索注入冻结扩散模型采样轨迹，实现免重训练的黑盒目标逆向材料设计，并用MLFF嵌入上的高斯过程代理降低oracle成本。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在三个晶体设计任务上，DiffGA在oracle评估的稳定、唯一、新颖命中率上优于仅扩散、算力匹配、纯进化和微调基线。使用DFT量化MLFF oracle保真度，显示其关键区间为忠实代理但其他区间存在系统性偏差。代表性样本中，DiffGA的顶级命中经第一性原理复评仍为物理上合理的结果，代理加速流程以极小代价保持该保真度。

Conclusion: 提出了免重训练的DiffGA框架，结合进化搜索与代理加速，在黑箱、非可微目标下实现高效逆向材料设计；同时界定了代理驱动生成式发现的可信边界，并证明其顶级结果可经受第一性原理验证。

Abstract: Steering candidate generation toward user-defined objectives is central to inverse materials design, yet remains difficult when objectives are black-box, changing, or non-differentiable, as in many simulation- and experiment-derived properties. Generative models offer a solution but typically require retraining or fine-tuning for each new objective, demanding labeled data and substantial compute, or rely on differentiable guidance that is brittle to gradient noise and undefined for many practical targets. We present DiffGA, a retraining-free framework that injects evolutionary search into the sampling trajectory of a retrained diffusion model, evolving partially denoised states under an arbitrary black-box oracle. Because oracle evaluation often dominates campaign cost, we couple DiffGA with an on-the-fly Bayesian surrogate that reuses representations from state-of-the-art machine-learned force fields (MLFFs). A Gaussian process is fit during the campaign on the force fields's graph embeddings and, once it demonstrably ranks an unseen generation, replaces expensive property evaluations under an uncertainty-aware acquisition rule, reducing oracle cost with negligible loss of accuracy. Across three crystal-design tasks using a frozen MatterGen backbone, DiffGA outperforms diffusion-only, compute-matched, purely evolutionary, and fine-tuned baselines in oracle-assessed rates of stable, unique, and novel hits. Rather than proposing scientific compounds, we use density functional theory to quantify MLFF oracle fidelity, showing that it is a faithful proxy in key regimes but systematically biased in others, delineating when proxy-driven generative discovery can be trusted. On a representative sample, DiffGA's top hits survive first principles re-evaluation as physically realistic hits, while the surrogate-accelerated pipeline preserves this fidelity at a fraction of the cost.

Abstract: Corrosion of structural alloys remains a critical challenge for advanced molten salt reactors, where aggressive fluoride salts promote continuous dissolution at the salt-metal interface. Abstract: Where crystal nuclei form in amorphous phase-change materials decides how they switch. Nucleation sites do not differ in composition or number density: the nuclei lie on the same robust HAADF-EDS relation as the matrix, and at fixed HAADF the spread of the summed EDS signal equals its Poisson counting noise (ratio 1.03 +- 0.05). The nuclei are nevertheless crystalline and stand out in the 4D-STEM max-intensity virtual image (median z = 11.0) but not in HAADF (z = 1.3) or EDS (z = 0.3). We classify the film in four ways (HAADF intensity, I(q) phase mapping, PCA of the whole diffraction patterns and a self-supervised SimCLR encoder) and compare the electron pair distribution functions (PDFs) of all classes after calibrating reciprocal space on the Au cap. The I(q) classification isolates, in every scan, an interface environment that holds 62-100% of the nucleus pixels (1.2-2.5-fold above depth) and persists when the nucleus pixels are removed before classification. The interface, rather than a distinct composition or density of the nucleating volume, therefore marks where nuclei form. Over 4.7e3 e/Å^2 the HAADF signal falls by 17%, the first shell shortens by 0.03 Å and the FSDP narrows by 9%, apparently ordering the film as it damages it.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Subnanometer transition-metal chalcogenide nanowires provide a one-dimensional platform in which electronic functionality and mechanical response can be tuned through chemical composition. The optimized structures are dynamically stable within the harmonic approximation, and their electronic character evolves systematically across the chalcogen series: MoS, MoSe, WS, and WSe are metallic, whereas MoTe and WTe are semiconducting, with HSE06 band gaps of 0.52 and 0.20 eV, respectively. The effective axial stiffness decreases from sulfides to selenides and tellurides, consistent with the increase in metal--chalcogen bond length; using a circular effective cross section, the corresponding Young moduli range from 392 to 718 GPa. All six nanowires have negative formation energies relative to the selected elemental reference phases. MLFF trajectories extending to 2.5 ns at 300 K preserve the one-dimensional morphology and local atomic order, providing evidence of finite-temperature dynamical stability on the simulated time scale. The corresponding MLFF molecular-dynamics energy traces are shown in. We further examine the response of the nanowires to axial strain and curvature, including strain-induced band-gap modulation in the tellurides. These results establish composition and mechanical deformation as complementary routes for tuning the electronic and mechanical properties of ultrathin transition-metal chalcogenide nanowires and support their further investigation as components of flexible nanoelectronic systems.

Motivation: 现有3D原子结构重建方法存在局限：倾转/散焦序列环形暗场技术更适合重原子，而动力学散射模型反演虽可用于单散焦4D-STEM，但需昂贵的实验后计算。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) atomic-structure analysis is of central importance in (scanning) transmission electron microscopy (STEM). STEM techniques to reconstruct 3D structure include tilt-series or defocus-series annular dark-field, best suited to heavier atomic species, and dynamical-scattering-model inversion applied to single-defocus four-dimensional (4D) STEM data, requiring computationally expensive post-experiment analysis. In this study, we propose a 3D-structure linear inversion (3D LINV) STEM algorithm, which enables a fast and direct reconstruction of 3D structures from a single-defocus 4D-STEM dataset. This method is based on solving linear equations relating the measured 4D-STEM data and the unknown 3D specimen potential under the thick weak phase object approximation. We show the capabilities of 3D LINV STEM through systematic 4D-STEM simulations across various observation parameters such as specimen thickness, partial coherence, and shot noise, and through an experimental demonstration of 3D structure reconstruction of a stacked freestanding membrane with atomic-scale lateral resolution and nanometre-scale depth resolution.

</details>

*Sichang Qiu,Yuxin Jiang,Yongchao Wu,Peng Han,Xinxin Gao,Zekun Huang,Ce Shang,Ronny Thomale,Tie Jun Cui,Shuo Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种模块化忆阻电路：以相同忆阻单元的二进制电导态作为Ising型赝自旋，通过可编程连接与带符号反馈实现均匀/交错序，并结合对称性编码器配置固定输运层，复现铁磁、反铁磁与交替磁（altermagnetic）输运对称性，并用于储备池计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程硬件通常采用固定架构，而复杂物理系统通过可复用局域单元间不同交互规则获得多样行为。忆阻器件兼具可写状态与非线性动力学，但如何将局域性质转化为可重构的集体序与受对称性支配的输运仍具挑战。

Method: 构建由相同忆阻单元经可编程连接组成的模块化电路；将二进制电导态定义为Ising型赝自旋；利用单元间带符号反馈调控均匀或交错赝自旋序；通过电路模型仿真研究可控畴壁运动与图案恢复；设计对称性编码器，将测得电导图案与子晶格标签及选定对称规则结合，配置固定输运层。

Result: 实验确立二进制电导态为Ising型赝自旋，带符号反馈可选择均匀或交错序；仿真展示可控畴壁运动与图案恢复；对称性编码器实现铁磁、反铁磁和交替磁输运对称性的电路类比；尽管反铁磁与交替磁编码共享补偿图案，其不同电压扩散表明编程电路对称性影响输运；器件校准仿真显示忆阻动力学为储备池计算产生历史依赖特征。

Conclusion: 通过将局域存储与可编程交互、对称性编码及任务专用读出相分离，该架构提供了一条从器件动力学到集体序、对称性依赖输运与学习的模块化路径。

Abstract: Programmable hardware typically uses fixed architectures, whereas complex physical systems obtain diverse behavior by connecting reusable local elements through different interaction rules. Memristive devices combine writable states with nonlinear dynamics, but translating these local properties into reconfigurable collective order and symmetry-governed transport remains challenging. Here we develop modular circuits configured through programmable connections between identical memristive units. Experiments establish binary conductance states as Ising-type pseudospins and show that signed feedback between units selects uniform or staggered pseudospin order. Circuit-model simulations then examine controllable domain-wall motion and pattern recovery. A symmetry encoder combines measured conductance patterns with sublattice labels and a selected symmetry rule to configure a fixed transport layer, implementing circuit analogs of ferromagnetic, antiferromagnetic, and altermagnetic transport symmetries. Although the antiferromagnetic and altermagnetic encodings share a compensated pattern, their distinct voltage spreading illustrates how programmed circuit symmetry affects transport. Device-calibrated simulations show that memristive dynamics generates history-dependent features for reservoir computing. By separating local memory from programmable interactions, symmetry encoding and task-specific readout, this architecture provides a modular route from device dynamics to collective order, symmetry-dependent transport, and learning.

</details>


### [92] [Enhancement of local magnetic ordering temperature due to non-magnetic disorder in anti-Heusler alloy Al$_2$MnCu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15622)
*Soumya Bhowmik,Suvayan Saha,Saikat Debnath,Arka Patra,Biswarup Satpati,Maxim Avdeev,Shishir Kumar Pandey,Chandan Mazumdar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文以反Heusler合金Al₂MnCu为平台，区分磁性原子无序与非磁性原子无序对磁性的不同作用，发现少量非磁性原子无序通过增强交换相互作用提升局域磁有序温度与T_C，而大量磁性原子无序对T_C几乎无影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构无序通常被认为有害于磁性。由于磁性质同时受磁性原子排布与晶体环境（包括介导交换作用的非磁性原子）支配，磁性无序与非磁性无序各自的独立作用尚不清楚。Al₂MnCu同时具有约50%涉及Mn的B2型同对称位点无序和约5%涉及非磁性Al、Cu的异对称位点无序，为解耦两类无序提供了理想体系。

Method: 在居里温度T_C附近开展磁临界分析，提取有效临界指数；结合直流磁强计与高阶谐波交流磁化率测量探测T_C以上的局域磁团簇；并配合理论分析，对比磁性原子无序与非磁性原子无序对交换相互作用和局域磁有序温度的影响。

Result: 磁临界分析未得到标准铁磁体的单一普适类，有效临界指数呈非单调温度依赖，指向类似非均匀铁磁体的复杂磁相互作用；直流磁性与高阶谐波交流磁化率证实T_C以上存在局域有序磁团簇；理论分析表明，少量非磁性无序增强交换相互作用，从而提升局域磁有序温度，而更大比例的磁性原子无序对T_C几乎无影响。

Conclusion: Al₂MnCu的实验与理论结合研究揭示并解释了非磁性无序诱导T_C升高的反常现象，为无序在磁性材料中的反直觉作用提供了范例。

Abstract: Structural disorder is generally regarded as detrimental to magnetism. Since magnetic properties are governed by both the arrangement of magnetic atoms and the crystal environment, including non-magnetic atoms that mediate exchange interactions, the distinct role of magnetic and non-magnetic disorder remains less understood. The recently reported anti-Heusler alloy, Al$_2$MnCu, forming with $\sim$50\% intra-symmetry site disorder (B2) involving the magnetic Mn atom and $\sim$5\% inter-symmetry site disorder associated with non-magnetic Al and Cu atoms, offers a good platform to disentangle the individual roles of magnetic and non-magnetic atomic disorder. Our magnetic critical analysis, in the vicinity of the Curie temperature ($T_{\rm C}$), does not lead to any conventional single universality class expected for a standard ferromagnet; instead, the non-monotonic temperature dependence of effective critical exponents points towards more complex magnetic interactions, akin to inhomogeneous ferromagnets. Dc magnetometry complemented by higher-harmonic ac magnetic susceptibility data, further confirms the persistence of locally ordered magnetic clusters above $T_{\rm C}$. Theoretical analysis establishes that the enhancement of the local magnetic ordering temperature arises directly from the strengthening of exchange interactions due to the small degree of non-magnetic disorder, whereas $T_{\rm C}$ is hardly influenced by the much larger level of magnetic-atom disorder. The combined experimental and theoretical investigations on the anti-Heusler alloy Al$_2$MnCu reveal and explain the unusual observation of nonmagnetic disorder-induced elevation of $T_{\rm C}$, providing a paradigm for the counterintuitive role of disorder in magnetic materials.

</details>


### [93] [Hidden Spin-Valley Hall Effect and Gate-Switchable Electric Hall Effect in 2D Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15630)
*Jiajun Lu,Mu Tian,Chaoxi Cui,Zhi-Ming Yu,Yugui Yao,Run-Wu Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在具有自旋-谷-层耦合（SVLC）的二维交错磁体中，栅极电场可解除谷间对称性强制的补偿，将隐藏的自旋-谷霍尔电流转化为可开关的净电荷霍尔信号（EHE），并通过理论分类、材料预测和第一性原理计算予以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体的电荷霍尔响应表面上消失，但其自旋向上与自旋向下的霍尔电流并未真正消失，而是符号相反、被对称性强制相互抵消而"隐藏"。当这些自旋态与不同谷锁定时，隐藏电流构成自旋-谷霍尔效应。作者希望找到一种全电学方式揭示并利用这一隐藏响应。

Method: 1）提出自旋-谷-层耦合（SVLC）机制，用栅极电场解除谷间层极化相反所带来的对称性强制的补偿；2）采用共线自旋层群（cSLG）理论，对全部158个交错磁cSLG进行分类，筛选支持SVLC的类别；3）预测候选材料（单层Mg₂Os₂(SO₅)₂及Ca(CoN)₂家族）；4）对Mg₂Os₂(SO₅)₂进行第一性原理计算验证。

Result: 在158个交错磁cSLG中识别出32个支持SVLC。第一性原理计算表明，Mg₂Os₂(SO₅)₂具有可逆的谷劈裂、栅极可开关的霍尔响应，以及超过1 mrad且随EHE信号一同反号的克尔旋转。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于对称性的全电学谷-自旋控制路径，并表明交错磁体是高性能谷输运与电可开关磁光学的有前景平台。

Abstract: Hidden beneath the vanishing charge Hall response of altermagnets are spin-up and spin-down Hall currents of opposite sign---concealed, not absent---whose cancellation is enforced by symmetry. When these spin states are locked to distinct valleys, the hidden currents constitute a spin-valley Hall effect. Here we show that in 2D altermagnets with spin-valley-layer coupling (SVLC), a gate field unveils this hidden spin-valley Hall response.~By lifting the symmetry-enforced compensation between valleys with opposite layer polarizations, the field converts the hidden response into a net charge Hall signal, yielding a gate-switchable electric Hall effect (EHE) enabled by SVLC. Using collinear spin layer group (cSLG) theory, we classify all 158 altermagnetic cSLGs, identify 32 that support SVLC, and predict monolayer Mg$_2$Os$_2$(SO$_5$)$_2$ and the Ca(CoN)$_2$-family as candidate materials. First-principles calculations for Mg$_2$Os$_2$(SO$_5$)$_2$ reveal reversible valley splitting, a gate-switchable Hall response, and a Kerr rotation exceeding 1 mrad whose sign reverses together with the EHE signal. These results establish a symmetry-based route to all-electrical valley-spin control and identify altermagnets as promising platforms for high-performance valley transport and electrically switchable magneto-optics.

</details>


### [94] [Indirect prediction and screening of frustrated magnetic materials using machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15683)
*Regina Burganova,Irina Piyanzina,Ruslan Akhmerov,Alexander Sigov,Maxim Fedorov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种机器学习工作流，通过分别预测居里-外斯温度与奈尔温度，间接预测磁阻挫指数并筛选潜在阻挫磁性化合物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁阻挫通常用阻挫指数（由居里-外斯温度和奈尔温度计算）来量化，但多数材料难以同时获得这两个量，因此直接预测阻挫指数很困难。

Method: 采用基于成分的CatBoost模型，在实验数据上训练，并通过化学信息驱动的数据集划分进行优化；分别独立预测奈尔温度与居里-外斯温度；引入集成不确定性，构建保守的阻挫指数以提升候选筛选可靠性。

Result: 稀土氧化物模型在预测奈尔温度上精度最高，稀土模型在预测居里-外斯温度上精度最高；对约67000个Materials Project化合物筛选，识别出20个高置信度阻挫磁体候选，其中包含若干实验已知的阻挫磁体及此前未探索的氧化物体系。

Conclusion: 通过间接预测与集成不确定性结合的方式，可有效筛选潜在阻挫磁性材料，并为后续实验研究提供候选体系。

Abstract: Magnetic frustration is commonly quantified by the frustration index, calculated from the Curie-Weiss and Neel temperatures. Because both quantities are not simultaneously available for most materials, direct prediction of the magnetic frustration index remains challenging. Here, we propose a machine learning workflow for indirect prediction of the frustration index and consequent screening of potential frustrated magnetic compounds through independent prediction of Curie-Weiss and Neel temperatures. In our work, composition-based models implemented via the CatBoost algorithm, trained on experimental data were optimized using chemically informed dataset subdivision. The highest predictive accuracy was achieved for the rare earth-oxide model in predicting Neel and for the rare earth model in predicting Curie-Weiss. Ensemble uncertainty was incorporated through a conservative frustration index to improve the reliability of candidate selection. Screening of approximately 67000 Materials Project compounds identified 20 high-confidence frustrated magnetic candidates, including several experimentally known frustrated magnets and previously unexplored oxide systems potential for further investigation.

</details>


### [95] [Reversible intrinsic-extrinsic crossover of the anomalous Hall effect in PrAlSi via Al/Si ratio tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15726)
*Huiyang Yang,Xiaohua Luo,Hongwei You,Wei Ren,Yuntao Gao,Changcai Chen,Zhishuo Zhang,Chunsheng Fang,Shengcan Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过调控PrAlSi单晶中Al/Si非化学计量比，实现AHE机制的可逆本征-非本征-本征转变，中间组分获得1413 Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹的巨反常霍尔电导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性Weyl半金属中AHE可由本征Berry曲率或非本征散射机制主导，但在同一材料平台内实现两种机制间的可逆切换仍未实现；本文旨在寻找一种化学调控手段来可逆地调控AHE机制。

Method: 对PrAlSi单晶进行Al/Si比例的非化学计量调控（Al/Si约1.05至0.75），制备四个组分（PrAl0.93Si0.89至PrAl0.79Si1.06），测量反常霍尔电导、载流子迁移率与载流子类型，并结合组分带填充与化学无序分析其作用。

Result: AHC呈非单调拱形变化（170→1413→507→164 Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹）；中间组分PrAl0.90Si0.94（Al/Si约0.96）具高迁移率和多载流子输运，获得1413 Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹的巨AHC，由非本征skew-scattering主导；贫Si与富Si组分AHC较低，由本征Berry曲率主导。偏离中间组分抑制迁移率并使载流子由空穴型转为电子型。

Conclusion: Al/Si比作为化学调控旋钮，主要通过组分带填充调节费米能级，非化学计量诱导的化学无序起次要作用，从而决定AHE机制；非化学计量PrAlSi可作为通过细微组分变化可逆切换AHE机制的可调平台。

Abstract: The anomalous Hall effect (AHE) in magnetic Weyl semimetals arises from distinct microscopic origins-intrinsic Berry curvature and extrinsic scattering mechanisms-yet reversible switching between these regimes within a single material platform remains elusive. Here, we demonstrate that off-stoichiometric tuning of the Al/Si ratio (from ~1.05 to ~0.75) reversibly drives an intrinsic-extrinsic-intrinsic crossover of the AHE in PrAlSi single crystals. Across four compositions spanning a narrow Si variation (PrAl0.93Si0.89 to PrAl0.79Si1.06), the anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) exhibits a non monotonic arch (170-1413- 507-164 Ω-1cm-1). The intermediate composition PrAl0.90Si0.94 (Al/Si ~0.96) exhibits high carrier mobility, multi carrier transport, yielding a giant AHC of 1413 Ω-1cm-1 dominated by extrinsic skew-scattering. The Si-poor and Si-rich compositions exhibit lower AHC values and intrinsic Berry-curvature-dominated AHE, establishing a composition-driven intrinsic-extrinsic-intrinsic crossover. Deviating from the intermediate composition suppresses mobility and reverses carrier type from hole-like to electron-like. The Al/Si ratio thus serves as a chemical knob that primarily modulates the Fermi level via compositional band filling, while off-stoichiometry-induced chemical disorder plays a subordinate role in determining the AHE mechanism. These results position off-stoichiometric PrAlSi as a tunable platform where subtle compositional variation reversibly switches the AHE mechanism.

</details>


### [96] [Ionomer-free Iridium Anodes with Low Loading for Zero-gap Electrolyzers via Direct Atomic Layer Deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15798)
*Peter M. Piechulla,Iris Blommestein,Anne van Gerven,Henri Pelzer,Ezgi Demiröz,Jonas Englhard,Mingliang Chen,Julien Bachmann,J. Ruud van Ommen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过定制微孔基底结合两种原子层沉积（ALD）工艺制备无离聚物阳极催化层，热ALD实现0.21 mg/cm²低铱载量并保持商用级性能与耐久性，等离子体增强ALD将载量降至0.04 mg/cm²但活性较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子交换膜水电解（PEMWE）是与间歇性可再生电力兼容的制氢技术，但阳极铱（Ir）供应有限，成为规模化瓶颈，因此亟需降低Ir载量同时维持性能。

Method: 以自制微孔基底为基础，采用两种ALD工艺（均使用Ir(EtCp)(CHD)前驱体）制备无离聚物催化层：1）热ALD，以O3为共反应物；2）等离子体增强ALD，用于调控催化剂分布。并进一步用低载量ALD阴极构建全电池，且将阳极用于阴离子交换膜的CO2电解实验。

Result: 热ALD主要生成金属Ir/IrOx，Ir载量0.21 mg/cm²；尽管载量低7倍，PEMWE单电池性能仍与商用催化剂涂层膜阳极相当，并在等效600小时间歇运行的加速耐久性测试中保持稳定。使用低载量ALD阴极时，铂族金属总载量仅0.24 mg/cm²。等离子体增强ALD通过调控催化剂分布将Ir载量降至0.04 mg/cm²，但生成活性较低的IrO2。

Conclusion: 直接ALD沉积催化剂无需含离聚物的油墨，使阳极可与多种膜材料兼容，并在阴离子交换膜的CO2电解实验中得到了验证。

Abstract: Proton exchange membrane water electrolysis (PEMWE) is among the most promising techniques to provide hydrogen as both energy carrier and feedstock for the chemical industry, due to its compatibility with intermittent renewable electricity sources. However, a major bottleneck in scaling up PEMWE is the limited supply of the anode catalyst Ir. This work shows a novel approach to tailoring ionomer-free catalyst layers based on custom-made microporous substrates and two different atomic layer deposition (ALD) processes, both using Ir(EtCp)(CHD) precursor. Thermal ALD using O3 as counter-reactant yields mostly metallic Ir/IrOx and an electrode Ir loading of 0.21 mg/cm2. The PEMWE cell-level performance is comparable to a commercial catalyst-coated membrane anode despite 7x lower loading, and is maintained over an accelerated durability test equivalent to 600 h of intermittent operation. Moreover, using a low-loading ALD-made cathode, the total platinum group metal loading is only 0.24 mg/cm2. Plasma-enhanced ALD reduces the Ir loading to 0.04 mg/cm2 by tailoring the catalyst distribution, however, yields lower-activity IrO2. Finally, direct ALD catalyst deposition needs no ionomer-bearing ink. The anodes are therefore compatible with various membrane materials, as demonstrated in a CO2 electrolysis experiment with an anion exchange membrane.

</details>


### [97] [Latent Heat and Raman Spectrum Change on Phase Transition of Water Including Calcium Chloride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15821)
*Dongha Kim,Changyoun Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过拉曼O-H伸缩振动谱追踪水相变过程中的潜热相关能量学，发现拉曼指标可重复反映相变窗口，且CaCl₂掺杂引起系统性位移与潜热特征改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索拉曼O-H伸缩振动特征能否追踪水相变过程中与潜热相关的能量学变化。

Method: 在O-H频移与氢键强度关联的框架下，将熵/焓变化与峰位及积分强度的温度依赖变化相联系；在控温条件下对纯水及CaCl₂掺杂样品进行拉曼实验，并对光谱进行解卷积与分波段积分。

Result: 拉曼指标呈现可重复的温度依赖性并捕获相变窗口；CaCl₂引起系统性偏移及表观潜热特征的可测改变。

Conclusion: 拉曼光谱可作为水及相关体系中热力学演化与杂质效应的定量探针。

Abstract: This study investigates how Raman O-H stretching signatures may be able to track latent-heat-related energetics during water's phase transitions. Prior work on latent heat and Raman analysis of phase change is reviewed to establish theoretical approach. Building on a framework that links O-H frequency shifts to hydrogen-bond strength, we relate entropy/enthalpy variations to temperature-dependent changes in peak position and integrated intensity. Experiments on pure water and impurity-doped samples, specifically CaCl$_2$, were performed under controlled temperatures, and spectra were deconvoluted and integrated over defined bands. The Raman metrics exhibit reproducible temperature dependence that captures the transition window, while CaCl$_2$ induces systematic shifts and measurable modifications in the apparent latent-heat signature. These results highlight Raman spectroscopy as a quantitative probe of thermodynamic evolution and impurity effects in water and related systems.

</details>


### [98] [Epitaxial Oxide Thin Films with High Entropy and Anomalous Crystallinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15861)
*Joseph Petruska,Christina M. Rost,Jon-Paul Maria,Saeed S. I. Almishal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦脉冲激光沉积（PLD）制备高熵氧化物（HEO）薄膜，以岩盐型 MgCoNiCuZnO 及其衍生物为主，关联生长动力学、外延约束与各阳离子作用，揭示化学无序与优异结晶性/外延有序性共存的机制及功能应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PLD 的远离平衡生长与亚稳构型的动力学冻结，可拓展 HEO 薄膜的成分与结构空间。但生长动力学、外延约束与各阳离子角色之间的关系尚需系统梳理，以指导高质量功能薄膜与异质结的设计。

Method: 综述性研究：以岩盐型 MgCoNiCuZnO 及衍生物为主要对象，辅以萤石、尖晶石、钙钛矿等示例；将生长动力学与外延约束同各阳离子作用相耦合，分析 Co 价态、Cu 配位、异价与尺寸失配掺杂对晶格调控、共格纳米结构与缺陷/成分景观的影响。

Result: 在 MgCoNiCuZnO 中，Co 价态与 Cu 配位表明化学与生长条件共同实现晶格调控与共格纳米结构形成；异价与尺寸失配添加拓展了可及的缺陷与成分空间。这些耦合自由度导致异常结晶性，即广泛的化学无序与卓越结晶质量、外延有序性共存，部分体系（尤其 MgO 上生长的岩盐）实现完全共格。

Conclusion: PLD 生长 HEO 薄膜中耦合自由度带来的“化学无序—高结晶质量—外延有序”共存，为在高质量单层薄膜与异质结中发现并调控功能构型提供了机会。

Abstract: Pulsed laser deposition (PLD) expands the compositional and structural possibilities of high-entropy oxide (HEO) thin films through far-from-equilibrium growth and kinetic arrest of metastable configurations. This review connects growth kinetics and epitaxial constraints to the roles of individual cations in shaping the resulting films. We focus primarily on rock salt MgCoNiCuZnO and its derivatives, with selected fluorite, spinel, and perovskite examples illustrating broader applicability. In MgCoNiCuZnO, Co valence and Cu coordination illustrate how chemistry and growth conditions jointly enable lattice tuning and coherent nanostructure formation, while aliovalent and size-misfit additions expand the accessible defect and compositional landscapes. These coupled degrees of freedom underpin anomalous crystallinity, the coexistence of extensive chemical disorder with exceptional crystalline quality and epitaxial order, including full coherence in selected systems, particularly rock salts grown on MgO. This combination opens opportunities to discover and engineer functional configurations in high-quality single-layer films and heterostructures.

</details>


### [99] [Crystal growth, magnetic, and transport properties of the candidate altermagnet Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17452)
*Nashra Pistawala,Martin R. Lees,Geetha Balakrishnan,Daniel A. Mayoh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 首次成功生长Cr₁/₄NbS₂高质量单晶，确认其六方结构、T_N~141 K反铁磁序及非常规磁输运行为，为交错磁体研究提供实验平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁（altermagnetism）是凝聚态磁性中快速兴起的新范式，但实验上已确认的交错磁候选材料数量极为有限。Cr₁/₄NbS₂长期被理论预言为潜在交错磁体，然而此前尚无单晶实验研究报道，因此亟需单晶样品来验证其结构与磁性质。

Method: 采用单晶生长技术制备高质量Cr₁/₄NbS₂单晶，并系统开展结构与物性表征：单晶X射线衍射确定晶体学信息与对称性；电阻率与热容测量确定磁有序温度；磁输运测量（磁阻与霍尔响应）探究输运行为。

Result: 单晶XRD确认六方对称性（空间群P6₃/mmc），满足交错磁行为判据之一；电阻率与热容测量明确给出反铁磁奈尔温度T_N ~ 141 K；磁输运测量在磁阻与霍尔响应中观测到非常规特征，指示量子扩散输运现象。

Conclusion: Cr₁/₄NbS₂首次以单晶形式被合成，具备六方结构与反铁磁序及量子扩散输运特征，可作为探测交错磁材料丰富预测现象的实验平台。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is a rapidly emerging paradigm in condensed matter magnetism, yet the number of experimentally confirmed altermagnetic candidates remains remarkably limited. Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$ has long been theoretically proposed as a promising altermagnet, but there are no reports on experimental investigation of single crystals. Here, we report the successful growth of high-quality single crystals of Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$ and present its structural, thermal, magnetic, and transport properties. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction provides detailed crystallographic information and confirms the hexagonal crystal symmetry ($P6_3/mmc$), one of the criteria for altermagnetic behavior. Resistivity and heat capacity measurements unambiguously identify an antiferromagnetic Néel temperature of $T_N \sim 141$ K. Magnetotransport measurements reveal non-trivial features in the magnetoresistance and Hall response, indicative of quantum diffusive transport phenomena. Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$, synthesized here for the first time in single-crystalline form, therefore serves as an experimental platform for probing the rich spectrum of phenomena predicted for altermagnetic materials.

</details>


### [100] [Extreme anharmonicity determines the superionic transition in the prototypical antifluorite Li$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15986)
*Lorenzo Bastonero,Chiara Cignarella,Nicola Marzari,Lorenzo Monacelli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合非微扰晶格动力学与机器学习原子间势，研究反萤石型Li$_2$O中超离子相变，证明强非谐性（而非单纯体积膨胀）决定转变温度与机制，并用TD-SCHA揭示准粒子图像失效，给出可实验探测的光谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反萤石型锂离子氧化物超离子导体中，高温快速离子转变长期被归因于体积驱动的谐声子软化，但该准谐近似忽略了快离子区固有的强晶格非谐性，转变的微观起源尚未澄清。

Method: 采用非微扰晶格动力学，结合第一性原理精度的机器学习原子间势（MLIP），研究Li$_2$O相变；并用时间依赖自洽谐近似（TD-SCHA）分析光谱性质。

Result: 非谐性而非体积膨胀在决定转变温度与转变机制中起决定性作用；TD-SCHA显示该材料中准粒子图像失效，谱函数与红外响应给出可经非弹性中子/X射线散射及红外光谱实验检验的相变特征。

Conclusion: 结果确立了Li$_2$O在超离子区附近的极端非谐性，并为理解和调控此类材料的快离子转变提供了晶格动力学框架。

Abstract: Superionic conductors are promising electrolyte candidates for all-solid-state batteries, thanks to their liquid-like ionic mobility. In the prototypical lithium-ion oxide superionic conductor with antifluorite structure, the high-temperature transition to the fast-ion regime has long been attributed to volume-driven harmonic phonon softening. However, this quasi-harmonic picture neglects the strong lattice anharmonicity inherent to the fast-ion regime, leaving the microscopic origin of the transition unresolved. Here, we apply non-perturbative lattice dynamics combined with a first-principles-accurate machine-learning interatomic potential to study the phase transition in Li$_2$O. We demonstrate that anharmonicity, rather than volume expansion alone as commonly assumed, plays the decisive role in determining the transition temperature and the underlying mechanism. Finally, we analyze the spectral properties using the time-dependent self-consistent harmonic approximation, revealing the breakdown of the quasi-particle picture in this material. The spectral function and the infrared response provide signtures of the phase transition that can be investigated experimentally using inelastic neutron or X-ray scattering and infrared spectroscopy. All these results establish the extreme anharmonicity of Li$_2$O near the superionic regime and provide a lattice-dynamic framework for understanding and engineering the fast-ion transition in this type of materials.

</details>


### [101] [An empirical model for deriving information on ion dynamics from $μ$SR data via the conventional Kubo-Toyabe function analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16006)
*Ryosuke Kadono*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 建立了扩展Kubo-Toyabe (EA-KT) 函数与传统KT函数参数间的解析经验模型，可将μSR分析离子扩散时出现的异常温度依赖"伪影"转化为提取活化能与前指因子的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用传统Kubo-Toyabe函数 $G_z^{\rm KT}(t)$ 分析离子扩散的μSR数据时，线宽 $Δ_{\rm KT}(T)$ 与涨落率 $ν_{\rm KT}(T)$ 会出现反常的温度依赖，这些是拟合近似带来的伪影而非真实物理；作者希望阐明其来源并加以校正利用。

Method: 建立经验模型，解析地将含Edwards-Anderson参数 $Q$ 的扩展KT函数 $G_z^{\rm EA}(t;Δ,ν_{\rm i},Q)$ 的参数与常规KT函数 $G_z^{\rm KT}(t;Δ_{\rm KT},ν_{\rm KT})$ 的参数相关联，并用该模型处理文献中电池材料的 $Δ_{\rm KT}$ 与 $ν_{\rm KT}$ 数据。

Result: 提出的公式成功再现了 $Δ_{\rm KT}(T)$ 与 $ν_{\rm KT}(T)$ 的异常温度依赖；将模型应用于电池材料文献数据，得到的Arrhenius方程 $ν_{\rm i}(T)=ν_{\rm i0} e^{-E_{\rm i}/kT}$ 中的活化能 $E_{\rm i}$ 与前指因子 $ν_{\rm i0}$ 与其他技术所得结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 该模型将传统KT拟合产生的"伪影"转化为可靠的诊断工具，可从已发表的μSR数据中准确提取离子扩散的活化能与前指因子。

Abstract: We present an empirical model that analytically relates the parameters of the extended Kubo-Toyabe (KT) function $G_z^{\rm EA}(t;Δ,ν_{\rm i},Q)$, which describes muon spin relaxation ($μ$SR) induced by jump diffusion of ions using the Edwards-Anderson parameter $Q$, to those of the conventional KT function $G_z^{\rm KT}(t;Δ_{\rm KT},ν_{\rm KT})$ that serves as an approximation of $G_z^{\rm EA}(t)$. The proposed formulae successfully reproduce the anomalous temperature dependences of the linewidth $Δ_{\rm KT}(T)$ and the fluctuation rate $ν_{\rm KT}(T)$ which are artifacts observed upon analyzing $μ$SR data on ion diffusion using $G_z^{\rm KT}(t)$. Applying this model to $Δ_{\rm KT}$ and $ν_{\rm KT}$ published in the literature of battery materials yields activation energy $E_{\rm i}$ and pre-exponential factor $ν_{\rm i0}$ in the Arrhenius equation for ion diffusion, $ν_{\rm i}(T)=ν_{\rm i0} e^{-E_{\rm i}/kT}$, that are in excellent agreement with those deduced using other techniques, transforming peculiar artifacts into a powerful diagnostic tool.

</details>


### [102] [Coherent Phonon Generation from Prethermal Carrier Populations in a Semiconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18279)
*Mingyu Shi,Matteo Savoini,Patrick Friebel,Andrea Annunziata,Thomas Metzger,Yunpei Deng,Laura Cattaneo,Steven L. Johnson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过测量与第一性原理计算碲中相干声子产生的光子能量依赖性，发现近带隙激发下全对称A₁模的相干振幅随波长变化很弱，与热化载流子模型预期不符；转而被"跃迁分辨的预热力"模型（含光学矩阵元及依赖能带/动量的形变势加权）所再现，拟合给出几飞秒的激发态寿命，表明跃迁选择性而非热化总载流子密度是控制相干声子产生的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为半导体中全对称相干光声子的产生可用热化瞬态载流子布居来解释。作者希望检验这一理解在近带隙激发条件下的适用性。

Method: 在碲（Te）上系统性测量超快激光驱动的相干声子产生的光子能量依赖性，激发波长覆盖电子带隙附近；并结合第一性原理计算进行对照分析。

Result: 与假设热化载流子布居的模型预期相反，全对称A₁模的相干振幅在跨带隙调谐波长时变化很弱。测量得到的谱依赖关系可由"跃迁分辨的预热力"模型再现，该模型以光学矩阵元及依赖能带和动量的A₁形变势进行加权；将模型拟合数据得到激发跃迁态寿命为几飞秒。

Conclusion: 在近带隙激发的半导体中，控制相干声子产生的关键因素是跃迁选择性，而非热化的总载流子密度。

Abstract: Ultrafast laser excitation of carriers in semiconductors often results in the creation of fully symmetric coherent optical phonons, typically understood in terms of a thermalized transient carrier population. We test this understanding with systematic measurements and first-principles calculations of the photon-energy dependence of ultrafast-laser-driven coherent phonon generation in tellurium, using excitation wavelengths around the electronic band gap. We observe that, contrary to the expectations of models assuming a thermalized carrier population, the coherent amplitude of the fully symmetric $A_1$ mode changes only weakly when tuning wavelength across the band gap. The measured spectral dependence is instead reproduced by a transition-resolved prethermal force weighted by optical matrix elements and band- and momentum-dependent $A_1$ deformation potentials. Fitting this model to the data yields an excited transition state lifetime of a few femtoseconds. These results identify transition selectivity, rather than a thermalized total carrier density, as a key factor controlling coherent-phonon generation in semiconductors excited near a band gap.

</details>


### [103] [Nonrelativistic Optically-Induced Spin Torque in Noncollinear Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16052)
*Zhiyuan Li,Jian Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作突破传统光学诱导自旋重取向受自旋轨道耦合强度限制的瓶颈，利用非共线磁构型将光学自旋操控拓展至非相对论区间。通过自旋群论分析对称算符对自旋力矩与量子几何（量子度规、贝里曲率）的调控及选择定则，并结合Kagomé格点模型与实际材料验证光照下的局域自旋力矩，发现非相对论光致自旋力矩显著强于自旋轨道诱导者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光学诱导自旋重取向效应通常与自旋轨道耦合强度成正比，这限制了材料必须含重元素，从而制约了光自旋电子学的材料平台与效应强度。作者希望突破这一限制，将光致自旋操控拓展到非相对论区间。

Method: 采用一般自旋群论方法，研究自旋空间与晶格空间中的对称算符如何共同调控自旋力矩以及Bloch波函数的量子几何（量子度规与贝里曲率）；给出位点分辨的自旋对称性并揭示其选择定则；进一步采用Kagomé格点模型和一类实际材料体系，展示光照下的局域自旋力矩。

Result: 非相对论自旋劈裂通常达电子伏特量级，远大于典型自旋轨道诱导的劈裂，因此非相对论光致自旋力矩也相应强得多；在Kagomé模型与实际材料家族中实现了光照下局域自旋力矩的展示。

Conclusion: 该工作将光自旋电子学从相对论极限拓展到非相对论极限，显著拓宽了其可实现平台与材料选择范围。

Abstract: Manipulating spin using contactless and non-invasive optical approaches is one of the forefront topic in the modern spintronic science and technology. Prior studies on optically-induced spin reorientation usually scales with spin-orbit coupling strength, setting a limitation on composing heavy elements. In this work, we extend such a limit into nonrelativistic regime by exploring noncollinear magnetic configuration. Using a general spin group-theoretical investigation, we show how the symmetric operators in both spin and lattice spaces control the spin torque and quantum geometry of Bloch wavefunction--including both quantum metric and Berry curvature. We describe their site-resolved spin symmetries and unravel their selection rules. Furthermore, we adopt a Kagomé lattice model and a realistic material family to showcase the local spin torque under light irradiation. Since the nonrelativistic spin splitting usually spans on the order of electron-volt, significantly larger than typical spin-orbit induced splitting, the nonrelativistic light-induced spin torque is concomitantly much stronger. This work extends the previous opto-spintronics from relativistic to nonrelativistic limit, significantly broadening its realization platform.

</details>


### [104] [Vibrational Fingerprints of Competing Structural and Polar Phases in CuInP2S6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16091)
*Pegah Mohammadi,Sobhit Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文用第一性原理DFT系统计算了CuInP2S6各候选相（C2/c、P-31c、Cc、P31c、P-1、P-3）的振动性质，给出拉曼/红外活性模式、特征指纹及依赖极化与取向的拉曼选择定则，为振动光谱识别其铁电/反铁电相提供理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状范德华CuInP2S6具有丰富的温度/压力驱动相变，产生多个物性不同的结构相；但各相结构相似、振动响应复杂，导致实验与理论上均难以明确识别这些相，亟需系统的振动光谱判据。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，系统研究顺电参考相C2/c、P-31c及其对应的动力学稳定铁电相Cc、P31c，以及反铁电相P-1、P-3的振动性质；结合声子谱与对称性分析确定各候选结构的拉曼与红外活性模式，并基于Porto记号建立不同背散射几何下依赖极化与晶体取向的拉曼选择定则。

Result: 确定了各候选结构的拉曼活性与红外活性模式，识别出可用于振动光谱相判定的特征振动指纹；建立了不同背散射几何下的极化与晶体取向相关拉曼选择定则，并指出在特定实验配置下应出现或消失的模式。

Conclusion: 研究为解释CuInP2S6的拉曼与红外测量、并在温度与压力驱动相变中可靠识别其竞争的结构相、铁电相与反铁电相提供了全面的理论框架。

Abstract: Layered van der Waals CuInP2S6 crystals exhibit a rich sequence of temperature- and pressure-driven phase transitions, giving rise to multiple structural phases with distinct physical properties. Despite extensive experimental and theoretical efforts, unambiguous identification of these phases remains challenging because of their structural similarities and complex vibrational responses. In this work, we employ first-principles density functional theory calculations to systematically investigate the vibrational properties of the reference paraelectric C2/c and P-31c phases and their corresponding dynamically stable ferroelectric Cc and P31c phases, as well as the antiferroelectric P-1 and P-3 phases. From calculated phonon spectra and symmetry analysis, we determine the Raman- and infrared-active modes of each candidate structure and identify characteristic vibrational fingerprints that enable their phase determination through vibrational spectroscopy. Furthermore, using Porto notation, we establish polarization- and crystal-orientation-dependent Raman selection rules for different backscattering geometries and identify the modes that are expected to emerge or vanish under specific experimental configurations. Thus, our results provide a comprehensive theoretical framework for interpreting Raman and infrared measurements of CuInP2S6 and for reliably identifying its competing structural, ferroelectric, and antiferroelectric phases across temperature- and pressure-driven phase transitions.

</details>


### [105] [Angle-Resolved Photoemission as a Sensitive Probe of Surface Quality: Sputtered versus Annealed Cu(001)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16421)
*Hyeonhee Na,Jeonghwan Oh,Jinju Lee,Hanjun Sim,Myeongun Go,Jimin Sohn,Jeonggu Kang,Byungmin Sohn*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文比较了Cu(001)单晶在四个表面制备阶段的LEED、XPS和ARPES结果，发现溅射后表面虽保持清晰(1×1) LEED图案与干净Cu 2p谱，但ARPES谱动量无关、能带色散消失；退火后色散恢复。表明LEED和XPS不足以判定表面是否适合ARPES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARPES对表面有序性要求高，通常以LEED和XPS作为表面质量判据。然而这些常规探针是否真能保证表面适合ARPES测量尚不明确，因此作者通过系统对比来检验这一假设。

Method: 对同一Cu(001)单晶在原位条件下分四个阶段测量：刚制备、6 K老化24小时、Ar+溅射后、退火后，分别采集LEED、XPS和ARPES数据并进行对比。

Result: 溅射表面与退火表面给出几乎相同的(1×1) LEED图案和Cu 2p芯能级；但溅射表面的ARPES谱与动量无关，退火后能带色散恢复。

Conclusion: 差异源于各探针对溅射缺陷的响应不同：LEED反映平均晶格，仍保持周期性；ARPES反映电子态，其晶体动量已不再良好定义。因此，锐利的LEED图案和干净的芯能级谱并不能保证表面适用于ARPES。

Abstract: Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) requires a well-ordered surface, whose quality is routinely judged from low-energy electron diffraction (LEED) and x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS). We compare LEED, XPS, and ARPES of a Cu(001) single crystal measured in situ at four stages of surface preparation: as-prepared, after aging for 24 h at 6 K, after Ar$^+$ sputtering, and after annealing. The sputtered and annealed surfaces show nearly identical $(1\times1)$ LEED patterns and Cu 2$p$ core levels, but the sputtered surface yields a momentum-independent ARPES spectrum, and the band dispersion recovers after annealing. We attribute this contrast to how each probe responds to sputtering-induced defects: LEED reflects the average lattice, which remains periodic, whereas ARPES reflects the electronic states, whose crystal momentum is no longer well defined. Our results indicate that a sharp LEED pattern and clean core-level spectra do not guarantee a surface suitable for ARPES.

</details>


### [106] [Decoupling Trap States in Amorphous Oxide Semiconductor Transistor Using a Conductive-Semiconducting-Conductive MOS Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16438)
*Kai Chen,Wei Zhang,Xuanyao Fong,Shengman Li,Yunlong Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种非晶氧化物半导体(AOS)基“导体-半导体-导体(CSC)”MOS测试结构，可解耦并分辨AOS晶体管中不同陷阱引起的电导损失，获得偏压分辨的陷阱分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试结构中非理想金属-AOS接触会与各类陷阱态产生的电导损失相互卷积，难以区分界面态与浅态等不同陷阱来源，亟需一种能抑制接触伪影、实现陷阱解耦的表征平台。

Method: 设计CSC(导电-半导-导电)MOS测试结构以缓解接触相关伪影；对漏电、串联电阻和器件几何效应进行识别与校准；基于偏压分辨测量提取陷阱分布，并结合时间常数分析区分界面态与浅态。

Result: 获得偏压分辨的陷阱分布，揭示三个区间：(1)耗尽区由界面态主导电导损失，时间常数达亚微秒级，密度随耗尽加深略增，呈施主型特征；(2)弱至中等积累区以浅态为主，体积态密度呈类高斯分布，响应时间从微秒到纳秒；(3)强积累区界面态重新主导且态数量增加，但时间常数保持不变。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了不同陷阱群体在各工作条件下对AOS晶体管的影响，为理解可靠性退化提供直观指标，并表明CSC结构是一种简单、可扩展且稳健的AOS晶体管陷阱表征平台。

Abstract: We propose a novel amorphous oxide semiconductor (AOS)-based conductive-semiconducting-conductive (CSC) MOS test structure to effectively sense, discriminate, and analyze distinct trap-induced conductance losses in AOS transistors. In conventional test structures, non-ideal metal-AOS contacts convolute the conductance loss generated by various trap states. The CSC architecture mitigates these contact-related artifacts, enabling the decoupling of interface states and shallow states. Furthermore, the contributions from leakage, series resistance, and device-geometry effects are identified and calibrated. Our results yield bias-resolved trap profiles and reveal three distinct regimes: (1) In the depletion region, interface states dominate the conductance loss. These states exhibit sub-microsecond time constants and their density increases slightly with deeper depletion, which is associated with donor-like states. (2) From weak to moderate accumulation, shallow states become the primary contributor, displaying a Gaussian-like volumetric density-of-states distribution with response times spanning from microseconds to nanoseconds. (3) In the strong accumulation regime, interface states regain dominance with an increased state quantity, while their time constants remain invariant. These bias-resolved profiles clarify how different trap populations impact AOS transistors under distinct operating conditions, providing intuitive metrics for understanding reliability degradation. Consequently, the CSC structure offers a simple, scalable, and robust platform for trap characterization in AOS transistors.

</details>


### [107] [Modelling the dynamics of the martensitic transformation in Ni$_2$MnGa with machine-learning force-fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16467)
*Markus E. Gruner,Yulia Klunnikova,Mike J. Bruckhoff,Konstatin P. Skokov,Olga N. Miroshkina*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过DFT训练的机器学习力场（ML-FF）结合经典分子动力学，以近DFT精度再现Ni₂MnGa的软TA₂声子与纳米孪晶马氏体偏好，并揭示自适应纳米孪晶促进正/逆马氏体相变、负孪晶界能稳定有序调制马氏体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ni₂MnGa等Heusler合金的马氏体相变受电子结构、磁性与晶格动力学微妙耦合支配；DFT虽能刻画调制马氏体能量景观，但难以覆盖有限温度下相关时空尺度的动力学，存在计算瓶颈。

Method: 从从头算分子动力学数据出发，构建DFT训练的机器学习力场（ML-FF），并用经典分子动力学模拟研究自适应纳米孪晶对奥氏体-马氏体相变的影响。

Result: ML-FF能重现Ni₂MnGa的关键特征，包括软TA₂声子和对纳米孪晶马氏体的偏好；自适应纳米孪晶促进正、逆相变动力学；DFT在T=0预测的沿整个相变路径的负孪晶界能支持有限温度下有序调制马氏体的形成。

Conclusion: 该ML-FF框架为大尺度、近DFT精度的磁性形状记忆合金模拟提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The martensitic transformations in stoichiometric Ni$_2$MnGa and related Heusler alloys are governed by a delicate interplay between electronic structure, magnetism, and lattice dynamics. While density functional theory (DFT) has successfully mapped the energy landscape of modulated martensites, it remains computationally prohibitive for capturing finite-temperature dynamics across relevant length and time scales. Here, we bridge this gap by constructing DFT-trained machine-learning force-fields (ML-FF) interpolated from ab initio molecular dynamics. We demonstrate that ML-FF trained on DFT data can reproduce key features of Ni$_2$MnGa, including the soft TA$_2$ phonon and a preference for nanotwinned martensites. Using classical molecular dynamics simulations, we explore the impact of adaptive nanotwinning on the austenite-martensite transition, which facilitates the dynamics of both, forward and backward transformation. We demonstrate, that the negative twin boundary energy along the entire transformations path predicted by DFT for $T=0$, supports the formation of ordered modulated martensites at finite temperatures. Our results pave the way for large-scale simulations of magnetic shape memory alloys with near-DFT accuracy.

</details>


### [108] [Theoretical Investigation of Oxygen-Vacancy Diffusion in SrTiO$_3$ using Neural Network Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16521)
*Kazutaka Nishiguchi,Ryota Yamamoto,Seiichiro Ten-no*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究基于AIMD数据训练了用于纯净及缺氧SrTiO₃的神经网络势（NNP），实现了对缺陷形成能与长时氧空位扩散的高精度、低成本模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧空位的形成与扩散决定了SrTiO₃的电子与原子输运性质，但基于DFT的从头算分子动力学（AIMD）因计算成本高，仅能处理小体系与短时间尺度，难以直接研究氧空位动力学。

Method: 以AIMD模拟生成的参考数据集训练神经网络势（NNP），覆盖纯净STO与含氧空位STO；验证其对DFT能量、原子力与virial的复现能力及超胞尺寸依赖性，随后用NNP进行大规模、长时程分子动力学（NNP-MD）模拟氧空位扩散。

Result: 训练后的NNP准确重现DFT参考能量、原子力与virial，并复现总能与氧空位形成能的超胞尺寸依赖关系（含外推至稀释极限值）；NNP-MD给出的氧空位扩散系数显著优于传统经典MD，与实验吻合更好；Arrhenius分析得到迁移焓0.64 eV，接近实验值0.67 eV。

Conclusion: 基于第一性原理数据训练的NNP为钙钛矿氧化物中缺陷形成与长时程空位输运研究提供了准确且计算高效的框架。

Abstract: Oxygen-vacancy formation and diffusion play important roles in determining the electronic and atomic transport properties of SrTiO$_3$ (STO). Accurate analysis of such point defects requires sufficiently large-scale and long-time molecular dynamics (MD) simulations; however, direct investigations of oxygen-vacancy dynamics using {\it ab initio} MD (AIMD) based on density functional theory (DFT) are limited to relatively small systems and short time scales because of their high computational cost. In this study, we develop a neural network potential (NNP) for pristine and oxygen-deficient STO using a reference dataset generated from AIMD simulations. The trained NNP model accurately reproduces the DFT reference energies, atomic forces, and virials for both pristine STO and STO containing an oxygen vacancy. The NNP also reproduces the supercell-size dependence of the total energies and oxygen-vacancy formation energies, including the formation energy extrapolated to the dilute limit. Large-scale and long-time MD simulations using the NNP (NNP-MD) are then performed to investigate oxygen-vacancy diffusion. The resulting oxygen-vacancy diffusion coefficients show substantially better agreement with experimental data than those obtained from conventional classical MD simulations. An Arrhenius analysis yields an oxygen-vacancy migration enthalpy of 0.64 eV, in close agreement with the experimental value of 0.67 eV. These results demonstrate that NNPs trained on first-principles data provide an accurate and computationally efficient framework for investigating defect formation and long-time vacancy transport in perovskite oxides.

</details>


### [109] [Interlayer Registry Controls Polar Topology in Twisted Bilayer h-BN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16528)
*Di Fan,Changming Ke,Shi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理训练的深度势能分子动力学模拟，系统揭示扭转双层h-BN中由层间配准控制的面内极化拓扑随扭转角、温度和机械应变的演化规律，并提出错配矩阵配准映射模型解释变形响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转范德瓦尔斯双层形成莫尔堆叠场，可实现空间图案化极化，但目前对配准控制极性拓扑如何随扭转角、温度和机械应变演化缺乏系统理解。

Method: 采用第一性原理训练的深度势能分子动力学模拟，研究扭转双层h-BN的空间极化织构，并建立错配矩阵配准映射模型。

Result: 扭转角减小时，莫尔极化织构从紧凑近圆形涡旋演变为扩展的三角形AB/BA畴，源于小扭转角下晶格弛豫增强；局部面内绕数仍与层间配准绑定并在高达1000 K下稳定。均匀形变只造成弱畸变，而选层形变强烈重构莫尔极化织构。

Conclusion: 层间配准是扭转双层h-BN中形成和调控莫尔极性拓扑的核心控制参量。

Abstract: Twisting van der Waals bilayers creates a moir'e stacking field with periodically varying local interlayer registry, enabling spatially patterned polarization. However, a systematic understanding of how this registry-controlled polar topology evolves with twist angle, temperature, and mechanical strain is still lacking. Here, using first-principles-trained deep-potential molecular dynamics simulations, we show that spatially varying interlayer registry governs the polarization texture and can be directly tuned by twist and relative layer deformation. As the twist angle decreases, the moir'e polarization texture evolves from compact, nearly circular vortex-like patterns to extended triangular AB/BA domains. This crossover arises from enhanced lattice relaxation at small twist angles, which expands low-energy, oppositely polarized AB/BA regions and confines in-plane polarization mainly to domain boundaries. Despite this reconstruction, the local in-plane winding remains tied to interlayer registry and persists up to 1000 K, indicating robust topology against thermal fluctuations. We further show that the mechanical response is dictated by whether deformation changes the relative registry: homogeneous deformation of both layers produces only weak distortions, whereas layer-selective deformation strongly reconstructs the moiré polarization texture. A mismatch-matrix registry-mapping model is developed to capture this response, explaining deformation-induced changes in vortex radii at larger twist angles and triangular-domain geometry at smaller twist angles. These results identify interlayer registry as the central control parameter for forming and tuning moir'e polar topology in twisted bilayer h-BN.

</details>


### [110] [Real-space obstruction control of the shift current in two-dimensional Quantum Spin Hall insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16544)
*Michele Bagaglini,Mattia Iannetti,Jonas Erhardt,Jörg Schäfer,Ralph Claessen,Giorgio Sangiovanni,Gianni Profeta*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在三角晶格量子自旋霍尔绝缘体的紧束缚模型中研究了平庸、量子自旋霍尔及高阶拓扑绝缘相的非线性光电流，发现受阻拓扑相因不同Wannier中心扇区之间的光学跃迁而具有显著增强的shift current响应，并可将indene烯（SiC(0001)上的In单层）作为候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找具有大体光伏效应的材料，拓展至可在红外波段工作的低带隙体系，并探索除光跃迁概率外由实空间位移决定的shift current机制。非中心对称且电子波函数受阻的材料被认为是理想候选者。

Method: 构建三角晶格上量子自旋霍尔绝缘体的紧束缚模型，系统研究其在平庸、量子自旋霍尔与高阶拓扑绝缘相下的非线性光电流（shift current），通过分析Wannier中心扇区之间的光学跃迁来解释增强机制。

Result: 受阻拓扑相在电子能带结构相似的情况下表现出显著增强的shift current响应；该增强源自属于不同Wannier中心扇区态之间的光学跃迁，此类跃迁可实现大的实空间位移；拓扑相的不同还可控制shift current的符号；indene烯被识别为实现该现象的有前景候选材料。

Conclusion: 将可控体光伏效应的探索拓展到以实空间电子受阻为特征的材料体系，表明拓扑受阻相是获得大且符号可控shift current的有效平台。

Abstract: The search for materials exhibiting a large bulk photovoltaic effect has recently expanded to low-band-gap systems capable of operating in the infrared regime, with potential applications in optoelectronics. Among the microscopic mechanisms contributing to this phenomenon, the shift current is one of the dominant sources of photocurrent generation. Owing to its geometric origin, the shift current is not only determined by the optical transition probability but also by the real-space displacement associated with the optical excitation, making non-centrosymmetric materials with obstructed electronic wave functions particularly promising candidates. In this work, using a tight-binding model of a quantum spin Hall insulator on a triangular lattice, we investigate the nonlinear photocurrent across trivial, quantum spin Hall, and higher-order topological insulating phases. We show that obstructed topological phases exhibit a substantially enhanced shift-current response, despite the similar electronic band structure. We identify the origin of this enhancement in optical transitions between states belonging to distinct Wannier-center sectors, which allow for a large real-space displacement upon photoexcitation and, depending on the topological phase, control the sign of the shift current. Our results extend the search for controllable bulk photovoltaic effects to materials characterized by real-space electronic obstruction and identify indenene, a monolayer of In on SiC(0001), as a promising candidate for realizing this phenomenon.

</details>


### [111] [Domain-wall-to-domain interconversion in a polar conducting oxide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16681)
*Martin F. Sarott,Majid Ahmadi,Johanna van Gent González,Ewout van der Veer,Mads C. Weber,Jacopo Pinna,Daniel A. Chaney,Arijit Sinha,Hamideh Hassani,Horatio R. J. Cox,Thomas Perrault,Amir Sabet Ghorabaei,Maria A. Loi,Philippe Ghosez,Bart J. Kooi,Beatriz Noheda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究在极性外延$\text{WO}_3$中实现了畴壁的可逆维度调控，将纳米级二维畴壁扩展为三维功能区域，并实现极性（畴）与导电性（畴壁）之间的可逆切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁性晶体中的畴壁是具有不同于畴内部对称性的拓扑缺陷，能够展现母体材料所禁戒的功能性。然而畴壁被严格限制在二维界面内，这既阻碍了对畴壁本质的深入理解，也限制了其应用潜力。

Method: 在极性外延$\text{WO}_3$中，利用具有导电、非极性特征且采用母相结构的畴壁，通过扫描探针针尖施加交变电场诱导局部加热，使畴壁相向相邻极性畴扩展，实现畴壁与畴之间的相互转化与可逆的空间调控。

Result: 实现了畴壁的可逆维度调控，将纳米尺度畴壁扩展为三维功能区域，并可在畴的极性属性与畴壁的导电属性之间可逆切换。

Conclusion: 该畴壁—畴相互转化策略为可重构氧化物电子学提供了新范式。

Abstract: Domain walls in ferroic crystals are topological defects with a symmetry distinct from the domain interior, allowing them to display functionalities that are otherwise forbidden in the host material. Despite their unique properties, domain walls are strictly confined to two-dimensional interfaces, which hinders unveiling their true nature and limits their applicability. Here, we demonstrate reversible dimensionality control by expanding nanoscale domain walls into three-dimensional functional regions in polar epitaxial tungsten trioxide ($\text{WO}_3$). This domain-wall-to-domain interconversion is enabled by the conducting, non-polar domain walls adopting the parent-phase structure. An alternating electric field applied through a scanning probe tip induces localized heating, which expands this domain-wall phase into the adjacent polar domains. This approach enables reversible spatial control to switch between mutually exclusive properties of domains (polarity) and domain walls (conductivity), offering a new paradigm for reconfigurable oxide electronics.

</details>


### [112] [Engineering Ferroelectric Phases in BaTiO$_3$ Films via Buffer-Layer Induced Misfit Strain Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16766)
*A. S. Mikheykin,N. V. Lyanguzov,A. S. Konstantinov,V. B. Shirokov,E. D. Linnik,T. Bondar,A. G. Razumnaya*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在MgO衬底上的BaTiO₃薄膜中,插入50 nm LSMO导电缓冲层可将有效应变由压应变翻转为张应变,并诱导出非常规相变序列(顺电→正交→单斜)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过应变工程调控铁电相对称性是定制极化拓扑与功能特性的关键。然而,衬底诱导的失配应变往往受到热膨胀失配等因素的干扰,如何有效且可控地调节铁电薄膜的有效应变状态与相结构仍具挑战。

Method: 在MgO(001)衬底上制备500 nm BaTiO₃薄膜,并插入50 nm La₀.₆₇Sr₀.₃₃MnO₃ (LSMO)缓冲层;采用温度依赖的偏振拉曼光谱、X射线衍射和压电力显微镜(PFM)表征其应变状态、相变序列及极化行为。

Result: 尽管MgO(001)的晶格参数预期产生张应变失配,但500 nm BTO薄膜中热膨胀失配占主导,冷却时呈有效压应力;插入LSMO后有效应变由压缩转为拉伸。冷却过程中观察到非常规相序:顺电→正交(aa相,~407 K)→单斜(r相,~300–345 K),并伴随面内与倾斜极化分量。LSMO不仅作为底电极,还改变应变状态,界面电荷再分布诱导内建电场,表现为自极化和非对称PFM回线。

Conclusion: 导电缓冲层在调控铁电对称性方面起关键作用,为实现具有定制极化织构的氧化物异质结构提供了途径,可应用于低功耗自旋电子学、可调微波器件和神经形态计算。

Abstract: Controlling ferroelectric phase symmetry through strain engineering is crucial for tailoring polarization topology and functional properties in oxide-based devices. Here, we show that a thin conductive buffer layer can override substrate-induced strain in BaTiO$_3$ (BTO) films on MgO. Although the lattice parameter of MgO(001) would be expected to induce tensile misfit strain, in 500~nm BaTiO$_3$ films the thermal-expansion mismatch dominates, resulting in effective compressive stress upon cooling. Inserting a 50-nm La$_{0.67}$Sr$_{0.33}$MnO$_3$ (LSMO) buffer layer reverses the effective strain state from compressive to tensile. Using temperature-dependent polarized Raman spectroscopy, X-ray diffraction, and piezoresponse force microscopy, we reveal an unconventional phase sequence upon cooling: paraelectric $\rightarrow$ orthorhombic ($\textit{aa}$-phase, $\sim$407 K) $\rightarrow$ monoclinic ($\textit{r}$-phase, $\sim$300-345 K), characterized in-plane and tilted polarization components. The LSMO layer not only serves as a bottom electrode but also modifies the strain state, while interfacial charge redistribution induces a built-in electric field, evidenced by self-polarization and asymmetric PFM hysteresis. These findings highlight the critical role of conductive buffer layers in controlling ferroelectric symmetry and enabling oxide heterostructures with tailored polarization textures for low-power spintronics, tunable microwave devices, and neuromorphic computing.

</details>


### [113] [Molecular Precursor Chemistry Shapes Early-Stage Reaction Pathways in Nitrogen-Doped Carbon Nanotube Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16831)
*Narayan N. Som,Lukasz Nowicki,Tomasz Gizewski,Karolina Z. Milowska,Anna Wroblewska,Caghan Tuncer,Maciej Trzaskowski,Olga Iwasinska-Kowalska,Tomasz Plocinski,Mariusz Zdrojek,Krzysztof K. Koziol,Malgorzata Jakubowska,Agnieszka Lekawa-Raus*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 碳纳米管的结构特征可在前驱体分解与催化剂形成的早期纳秒阶段就被决定；含氮前驱体（吡啶、吡嗪）通过改变Fe–C、Fe–SiO₂相互作用及分解路径，促进竹节状CNT形成并提高结构无序度，且掺氮量并不与供氮量简单成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上对碳纳米管结构特征的讨论多聚焦于已形成催化剂纳米颗粒后的成核与生长过程。本文旨在探究更早的阶段——前驱体分解与催化剂形成的最初几纳秒——是否已对CNT的结构轨迹产生影响，并厘清前驱体化学（而非单纯供氮量）的作用。

Method: 结合实验与反应分子动力学（reactive molecular dynamics）模拟，对比无氮CNT合成与两种结构相近的芳香族含氮前驱体（吡啶含1个氮原子、吡嗪含2个氮原子）的合成过程，考察催化剂演化过程中Fe–C与Fe–SiO₂局部相互作用及分解路径。

Result: 实验中，两种含氮前驱体均促进竹节状（bamboo-like）CNT的形成，并生成结构无序度显著更高的CNT；掺氮程度并不随供氮量线性增加，表明前驱体化学具有重要贡献。反应分子动力学显示，含氮前驱体显著改变催化剂演化过程中的局部Fe–碳和Fe–SiO₂相互作用；吡啶与吡嗪呈现不同的分解路径，其中吡嗪在模拟时间尺度内分解程度明显更高。

Conclusion: 前驱体化学会影响局部化学环境和催化剂的早期演化，从而在CNT生长的初始阶段发挥潜在的关键作用，说明CNT结构轨迹可追溯至前驱体分解与催化剂形成的最早期阶段。

Abstract: The structural characteristics of carbon nanotubes are usually discussed in terms of processes occurring during nanotube nucleation and subsequent growth from an already formed catalyst nanoparticle. Here, we show that their structural trajectory can begin much earlier, within the first nanoseconds of precursor decomposition and catalyst formation. We combine experiment and reactive molecular dynamics to compare nitrogen-free CNT synthesis with two closely related aromatic nitrogen precursors, pyridine and pyrazine, containing one and two nitrogen atoms per molecule, respectively. Experimentally, both nitrogen-containing precursors promote bamboo-like CNT formation and produce substantially more structurally disordered CNTs than the nitrogen-free reference. Notably, nitrogen incorporation does not scale simply with the amount of nitrogen supplied, demonstrating a strong contribution from precursor chemistry. Reactive molecular dynamics reveals that nitrogen-containing precursors significantly modify local Fe-carbon and Fe-SiO2 interactions during catalyst evolution, while pyridine and pyrazine exhibit distinct decomposition pathways, with pyrazine showing significantly greater decomposition within the simulated timescale. These results demonstrate that precursor chemistry influences the local chemical environment and early catalyst evolution, highlighting its potential role in the initial stages of CNT growth.

</details>


### [114] [Trust-Limited Machine-Learning-Accelerated Nudged Elastic Band Calculations for Small-Polaron Hopping in Rutile TiO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16850)
*Ashish Kumar Singh,Irina V. Lebedeva*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高斯过程辅助的NEB工作流，解决小极化子跳跃计算中电子态自洽收敛慢、局域化切换等难题，并在金红石TiO2中实现了可复现的势垒计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小极化子跳跃的NEB计算中，核运动与自洽局域电子态强耦合，在电荷转移区域存在自洽场收敛慢、局域化切换与竞争性电子解等问题，使代理模型辅助优化难以稳定进行，因此需要一个能在此区域可靠工作的计算流程。

Method: 采用高斯过程辅助的NEB工作流，能量-力联合GP配合Matérn协方差推进弹性带；以活跃原子均方根与最大原子位移作为约束限制代理运动，达到限制即触发第一性原理采样，而无需代理完全收敛；使用SIESTA进行自旋极化DFT+U，并在15图像计算中对中心图像重复采样时启用单独校准的局部GP。

Result: 三次独立DFT验证的96原子[001]计算（11、13、15图像，不同代理设置与GP参数化）给出势垒0.1645、0.1637、0.1640 eV，跨度仅0.793 meV；15图像计算最终全带DFT验证的最大投影NEB力为0.0346 eV/Å；324原子计算得到更低势垒0.1302 eV，表明超胞敏感性并验证了更大GP观测维度下的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作流将受控代理外推、电荷转移区定向插值与独立DFT验证结合，可稳健获取极化子迁移路径，并强调势垒可复现性与全带力收敛。

Abstract: Nudged elastic band (NEB) calculations of small-polaron hopping are demanding because nuclear motion is coupled to a self-consistent localized electronic state. Near the charge-transfer region, slow self-consistent-field convergence, localization switching, and competing electronic solutions complicate surrogate-assisted optimization. We develop a Gaussian-process-assisted NEB workflow for this regime and demonstrate electron small-polaron hopping in rutile TiO2 using spin-polarized density functional theory with a Hubbard U correction (DFT+U ) with SIESTA. A joint energy-force Gaussian process (GP) with a Matérn covariance model advances the elastic band, while active-atom root-mean-square and maximum-atom displacement limits constrain surrogate motion relative to evaluated configurations. Reaching these limits triggers first-principles sampling without requiring full surrogate convergence. Three independently DFT-verified 96-atom [001] calculations using 11, 13, and 15 images and different surrogate settings and GP parameterizations yield barriers of 0.1645, 0.1637, and 0.1640 eV, respectively, spanning 0.793 meV. In the 15-image calculation, repeated central-image sampling activates a separately calibrated local GP during surrogate optimization. Final full-band DFT verification gives a maximum projected NEB force of 0.0346 eV/Å. A 324-atom calculation yields a lower barrier of 0.1302 eV, indicating supercell sensitivity and demonstrating feasibility at larger GP observation dimension. The workflow combines controlled surrogate extrapolation, targeted charge-transfer-region interpolation, and independent DFT verification to obtain polaron migration pathways, emphasizing reproducible barriers and full-band force convergence.

</details>


### [115] [First-Principles Calculations of the Electron-Hole-Pair Creation Energy in Semiconductors and Dielectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16949)
*Dallin O. Nielsen,Massimo V. Fischetti*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用第一性原理方法计算了AlN、3C-SiC、4H-SiC、Si和SiO2中电子-空穴对产生能，并指出声子发射与电离的竞争以及等离激元峰的位置和形状是决定产生能的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确获取半导体和绝缘体中的电子-空穴对产生能对理解电子热化、辐射效应及器件性能具有重要意义，而现有计算值与实验值（尤其是Si）仍存在偏差，需要更精确的理论方法。

Method: 结合DFT计算能带结构、密度泛函微扰理论获取声子色散、TDDFT计算介电函数与电子能量损失函数，并进行全带Monte Carlo模拟电子热化（能量至约100 eV）；对Si进一步采用GW方法和Bethe-Salpeter方程（BSE）修正准粒子能带与介电函数。

Result: 得到AlN、3C-SiC、4H-SiC、Si和SiO2的对产生能分别约为12.2、6.5、7.3、3.10和16.7 eV/pair，与实验吻合良好；其中Si偏差最大（比实验值约3.6 eV/pair低约14.9%），采用GW/BSE后Si的产生能约为3.50 eV/pair，精度显著提高。

Conclusion: GW/BSE方法对Si精度的提升主要源于约10–15 eV以下电子能量损失函数幅度的略微降低；总体而言，声子发射与电离速率交叉点附近的竞争，以及能量损失函数中等离激元峰的位置和形状，是决定对产生能的主要因素。

Abstract: We present first-principles calculations of the electron-hole-pair creation energy in semiconductors and dielectrics, focusing in particular on AlN, 3C-SiC, 4H-SiC, Si, and SiO2. We have employed density functional theory (DFT) to calculate the band structure, density-functional perturbation theory to obtain the phonon dispersion, and time-dependent DFT (TDDFT) to calculate the electron energy loss function (via the dielectric function). We performed full-band Monte Carlo simulations of electron thermalization for energies up to ~100 eV and calculated the average energy required to excite an electron-hole pair. We calculated pair-creation energies of approximately 12.2, 6.5, 7.3, 3.10, and 16.7 eV/pair for AlN, 3C-SiC, 4H-SiC, Si, and SiO2, respectively. These values are in good agreement with measured data, with the largest discrepancy being that of Si (about 14.9% lower than the accepted experimental value of ~3.6 eV/pair). To improve the agreement for Si, we used the GW approach and the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) to calculate the quasiparticle shifts of the electronic band structure and the dielectric function, respectively. Finally, using the GW/BSE data, we calculated a creation energy of ~3.50 eV/pair. We find that the marked improvement in the accuracy of the calculated creation energy, when using the GW/BSE approach, is primarily attributable to the slight decrease in the magnitude of the electron energy-loss function when using the GW/BSE approach for energies below ~10-15 eV. Overall, it is clear that the competition between phonon emission and ionization near the intersection of the phonon scattering and ionization rates is largely responsible for determining the value of the creation energy, with the other major factor being the location and shape of the plasmon peak in the energy-loss function.

</details>


### [116] [Capturing Coordination Core Topology in Metal--Organic Frameworks Using Persistent Mayer Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17056)
*Caleb Simiyu Khaemba,Chun-Long Chen,Guo-Wei Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出持久Mayer同调（PMH），用于定量刻画MOF与POM中局部配位核心的几何变异，并与ERPH结合提升吸附与自扩散性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOF和POM的局部配位核心决定其化学与结构性质，但现有方法（如元素分辨持久同调ERPH）难以将配位核心转化为定量、可扩展且可解释的信息。

Method: 引入持久Mayer同调（PMH），探测经典拓扑无法捕捉的几何变异；聚焦周期性金属中心重原子、碳及配位原子通道构成的配位核心，区分局部配位环境、连接体框架与潜在配位原子的排布。考察N=3与N=5阶可解释PMH，并分别与ERPH结合，用于预测8种氮氧相关吸附与自扩散性质。

Result: 配位核心PMH表示在预测性能上一致优于单独使用ERPH，其中三阶Mayer表示总体表现最佳。

Conclusion: PMH捕捉的内层配位构型包含常规元素分辨拓扑描述符未充分表达的关键结构信息，为理解局部配位环境提供了定量可解释的拓扑框架，有望促进MOF与POM基材料的理性设计。

Abstract: Local coordination cores are critically important in metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) and polyoxometalates (POMs), as they govern the fundamental chemical and structural properties of these materials. Existing methods, including element-resolved persistent homology (ERPH), have limited ability to translate local coordination cores into quantitative, scalable, and interpretable information. Here, we introduce persistent Mayer homology (PMH) to enable the detection of the geometric variability invisible by classical topology. By focusing on coordination cores formed by periodic metal-centered heavy-atom, carbon, and donor channels, PMH distinguishes different arrangements of the local coordination environment, linker framework, and potential coordinating atoms. We examine explainable PMH of orders $N=3$ and $N=5$ and separately combine each with ERPH, which characterizes the multiscale organization of chemically defined element groups across the extended framework, to predict eight nitrogen- and oxygen-related adsorption and self-diffusion properties. The resulting coordination-core PMH representations consistently improve predictive performance over ERPH alone, with the third-order Mayer representation providing the strongest overall performance. These results demonstrate that the inner coordination configurations captured by PMH contain essential structural information that is not fully represented by conventional element-resolved topological descriptors. This finding provides a quantitative interpretable topological framework for understanding local coordination environments and may facilitate the rational design of MOFs and POM-based materials.

</details>


### [117] [Symmetry-constrained coexistence of charge-three and charge-one Weyl points in nonmagnetic crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17071)
*Ze-Xin Xue,Ke-Xin Pang,Yun-Yun Bai,Yanfeng Ge,Yan Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合拓扑荷守恒与空间群对称性分析，系统分类了230个空间群中含两个C-3与六个C-1外尔点（即两个{3,-1,-1,-1}不可约外尔分子）的八节点构型，并预测硼同素异形体TBR-B48与手性OBD-B48可实现该构型，揭示其费米弧及手性对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高荷外尔半金属（尤其含三荷外尔点C-3 WP）因非常规拓扑性质和增强的边界现象备受关注，但理想C-3外尔半金属稀少，且非磁性晶体中C-3与C-1外尔点受对称性约束的共存问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 结合拓扑荷守恒与空间群对称性分析，对全部230个空间群进行系统分类；通过第一性原理计算（含/不含自旋-轨道耦合SOC）筛选候选材料，对TBR-B48和手性OBD-B48进行能带与表面态计算，并研究破坏C6对称性及反转结构手性的影响。

Result: 识别出不含SOC时12个、含SOC时23个候选空间群；无SOC第一性原理计算表明TBR-B48（沙漏型C-3外尔点）和手性OBD-B48（非沙漏型）可实现该八节点构型。表面态计算显示C-3外尔点对共享投影处有六条费米弧分支，各自投影处各有三条。反转OBD-B48结构手性可反转所有外尔荷符号，建立晶体手性与动量空间手性的直接对应；破坏TBR-B48的C6对称性可使每个C-3外尔点分裂为三个C-1外尔点并重构表面费米弧。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了设计与搜寻同时承载三荷与单荷外尔点共存的非磁性外尔半金属的对称性指导准则，并给出可行材料候选与可调控的边界态行为。

Abstract: High-charge Weyl semimetals (WSMs), particularly those hosting charge-three Weyl point (C-3 WPs), have attracted considerable interest owing to their unconventional topological properties and enhanced boundary phenomena. However, ideal C-3 WSMs remain rare, and the symmetry-constrained coexistence of charge-three and charge-one Weyl point (C-1 WPs) in nonmagnetic crystals remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate a representative eight-node Weyl configuration, consisting of two C-3 WPs and six C-1 WPs with opposite chirality, corresponding to two identical $\{3,-1,-1,-1\}$ irreducible Weyl molecules. By combining topological charge conservation with space-group symmetry analysis, we systematically classify all 230 space groups and identify 12 and 23 candidate groups without and with spin--orbit coupling (SOC), respectively. First-principles calculations without SOC reveal that the boron allotropes TBR-B$_{48}$ and chiral OBD-B$_{48}$ realize this configuration, hosting hourglass-type and non-hourglass-type C-3 WPs, respectively. Surface-state calculations reveal six Fermi-arc branches at the shared projection of the charge-three pair and three at each individual projection. Reversing the structural chirality of OBD-B$_{48}$ reverses the signs of all Weyl charges, establishing a direct correspondence between crystal and momentum-space chirality. Furthermore, breaking the protecting $C_6$ symmetry in TBR-B$_{48}$ splits each C-3 WP into three C-1 WPs and reconstructs the surface Fermi arcs. Our work establishes symmetry-based guidelines for designing and searching for nonmagnetic WSMs hosting charge-three and charge-one Weyl-point coexistence.

</details>


### [118] [Spin-orbit torque effects in all-electrical spin-wave spectroscopy experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17095)
*José Solano Córdova,Jone Mencos,Richard Schlitz,Quentin Rossi,Denise Reustlen,Javed Ali,Sebastian Sailler,Michaela Lammel,Matthieu Bailleul,Daniel Stoeffler,Felix Casanova,Sebastian T. B. Goennenwein,Helena Reichlova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过Pt天线在105 nm厚外延YIG薄膜中激发相干自旋波，在扣除刻蚀损伤与非均匀界面贡献后，发现波传播信号与奥斯特场和自旋轨道矩的联合激发机制一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
