Motivation: 自旋波的电学激发与探测是磁子器件的核心。传统Au、Cu等非磁性金属天线主要通过射频奥斯特场激发自旋波，而含Pt天线还可经由自旋霍尔效应产生自旋轨道矩（SOT）。近期理论提出RF-SOT可作为相干自旋波激发的替代机制，并预测其特性有别于常规感应激发，但缺乏实验验证。

Method: 在105 nm厚外延Y3Fe5O12（YIG）薄膜上制备Pt天线，针对两种潜在机制——振荡奥斯特场与振荡自旋流——开展自旋波激发实验；分析原始透射谱中由刻蚀工艺引起的YIG改性以及Pt导致的空间非均匀界面条件的贡献，并将其从谱中剥离。

Result: 原始实验透射谱受到刻蚀工艺导致的YIG薄膜改性以及Pt引入的空间非均匀界面条件的强烈影响；在扣除这些贡献后，剩余的两支波传播信号与奥斯特场和SOT共同激发的预期相符。

Conclusion: 实验支持RF-SOT作为相干自旋波激发的附加机制，Pt天线激发是奥斯特场与自旋轨道矩联合作用的结果；同时表明工艺与界面效应对谱线的干扰必须被谨慎扣除才能提取本征激发特征。

Abstract: Electrical excitation and detection of propagating spin waves are key elements of magnonic devices. While conventional antennas consisting of non-magnetic metals such as Au or Cu excite spin waves predominantly through radio-frequency (RF) Oersted fields, antennas comprising Pt can additionally generate spin-orbit torques (SOTs) via the spin Hall effect. Recent theoretical work has proposed RF-SOT as an alternative mechanism for coherent spin-wave excitation, predicting distinct characteristics compared with conventional inductive excitation. Here, we investigate Pt antennas for the excitation of coherent spin waves in 105-nm-thick epitaxial $\text{Y}_3\text{Fe}_5\text{O}_{12}$ (YIG) films through two potential mechanisms: an oscillating Oersted field and an oscillating spin current. The raw experimental transmission spectra are strongly affected by fabrication-induced modifications to the YIG film associated with the etching process together with spatially inhomogeneous interface conditions induced by Pt. After removing these contributions, we find the remaining two wave propagation consistent with a combined excitation from Oersted fields and SOTs.

</details>


### [119] [Comprehensive Workflow for Uncertainty-Aware Ionic Liquid Solvent Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17171)
*Alexander Lobo,Urvi Awasthi,Leonid Zhukov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种将数据处理、深度学习建模与物理计算耦合的离子液体性质预测与最优候选筛选工作流，通过结合不确定性估计与物理过滤，实现可靠的分布外排序，并用于设计烟道气CO2捕集的新型离子液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计具有目标性质的可合成材料是材料科学核心目标之一。离子液体因可调性适合该目标，但其数据稀疏、噪声大、分布不均，现有数据驱动性质模型在分布外筛选时脆弱：点预测看似合理却难以一致比较候选、量化下游影响或信任排序，难以指导分子发现。

Method: 构建性质预测与最优候选选择一体化工作流，将数据预处理、深度学习建模和基于物理的计算视为耦合系统，把性质预测与不确定性估计及物理过滤相连接，使预测性质与下游筛选标准紧密关联。

Result: 该工作流支持可靠的分布外排序，可优先选出此前未测试、预测权衡有利的离子液体候选。以烟道气CO2捕集为例，预测新型离子液体性质，并基于CO2溶解度、离子液体黏度和工作容量筛选Pareto最优候选。

Conclusion: 将数据处理、深度学习与物理计算耦合，并强化预测性质与筛选标准之间的联系，可提升离子液体在稀疏、噪声、分布外数据条件下候选排序的可靠性，为面向CO2捕集的离子液体分子发现提供可行路径。

Abstract: Designing synthesizable materials with targeted properties remains one of the central objectives in materials sciences. Ionic liquids (ILs) are well suited to this goal due to their "tunability", where physical and chemical properties can be precisely customized by modifying their molecular structure, specifically by varying the combination of cations and anions. At the same time, current data-driven property models for ionic liquids often degrade in scarce, noisy, and uneven data regimes, where measurements cover only a small fraction of chemical space and a limited range of thermodynamic conditions. This makes out-of-distribution screening brittle: even when point predictions look plausible, it is difficult to compare candidates consistently, quantify downstream implications, or trust rankings enough to guide molecular discovery. We present a property-prediction and optimal candidate selection workflow that treats data processing, deep learning modeling, and physics-based calculations as a coupled system, linking property prediction with uncertainty estimation and physics-based filtering. By tightening the link between predicted properties and the criteria used for down-selection, the workflow supports reliable out-of-distribution ranking and enables prioritization of previously untested ionic liquid candidates with favorable predicted trade-offs. We demonstrate the application of our workflow by designing novel ionic liquids for CO2 capture from flue gas, predicting their properties and selecting Pareto-optimal candidates based on CO2 solubility, IL viscosity and working capacity.

</details>


### [120] [Evidence of an anisotropic Griffiths phase in a two-dimensional van der Waals magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17315)
*Anupam K. Singh,Dhananjay Nair,Holger Meyerheim,Stuart S. P. Parkin,Banabir Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在Fe5GeTe2中发现由自插层驱动的高度各向异性Griffiths相，表现为布里渊区内的短程铁磁团簇嵌入顺磁基体，并在面内磁场下呈现异常响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维范德华磁性研究主要集中于长程有序磁相，无序磁相（如Griffiths相）此前在很大程度上未被探索。

Method: 以自插层范德华磁体Fe5GeTe2为研究对象，通过磁化率测量、Lee-Yang标度分析以及熵异常等手段表征其磁行为，并比较面内与面外磁场下的响应差异。

Result: 观测到短程有序铁磁团簇嵌入顺磁基体，在居里温度与更高的Griffiths温度之间主导磁响应；磁化率呈异常幂律发散，具有普适Lee-Yang标度及明显熵异常；Griffiths响应仅在面内磁场下出现，面外磁场则突然改变该行为。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了维度受限、无序驱动的磁相行为，并表明自插层范德华磁体是研究磁无序、各向异性与降维相互作用的理想平台。

Abstract: Over the last decade, the emerging field of two-dimensional van der Waals magnetism has focused mainly on long-range-ordered magnetic phases. Disordered magnetic phases, however, remain largely unexplored. Here, we report a highly anisotropic Griffiths phase driven by self-intercalation in the van der Waals magnet Fe5GeTe2. We find evidence of short-range-ordered ferromagnetic clusters embedded in a paramagnetic matrix, which dominate the magnetic response between the Curie temperature and a higher characteristic Griffiths temperature. An anomalous power-law divergence in the magnetic susceptibility, universal Lee Yang scaling, and distinct entropy anomalies provide further evidence for this phase. Strikingly, the non-analytic Griffiths response emerges exclusively under in-plane magnetic fields, whereas out-of-plane fields abruptly alter this behavior. This directional dependence indicates that the local magnetic interactions within nanoscale clusters are closely coupled to the layered lattice geometry. Our findings establish a regime of dimensionally constrained, disorder-driven magnetism and identify self-intercalated van der Waals magnets as a platform for investigating the interplay between magnetic disorder, anisotropy, and reduced dimensionality.

</details>


### [121] [Can Universal Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials Capture Small Polarons? A Geometry-Based Benchmark in Transition-Metal Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17352)
*Vladislav Korostelev,Pjotrs Žguns,Konstantin Klyukin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）描述过渡金属氧化物中小极化子的能力，发现其表现高度依赖训练数据与电子关联处理，均无法稳定描述极化子与给体空间分离的构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小极化子在过渡金属氧化物的电荷输运、催化和降解过程中起关键作用，但uMLIPs缺乏显式电子自由度，能否描述极化子尚不明确。

Method: 以氢诱导电子局域化为研究对象，构建基于TM-O键长膨胀（Z-score）的几何描述符识别极化畸变，并在多种过渡金属氧化物中对比uMLIPs与DFT+U的预测行为。

Result: uMLIPs表现出从离域态到亚稳小极化子的不同程度的电子局域化，且强烈依赖训练数据与电子关联处理；训练数据中缺失特定TM阳离子的Hubbard U修正会导致电子离域，与DFT+U预测相悖；部分uMLIPs能重现耦合质子-极化子转移中的跳跃势垒，但均无法稳定空间分离的极化子-给体构型，反而倾向于在H掺杂附近局域化。

Conclusion: uMLIPs目前尚不足以可靠描述过渡金属氧化物中的小极化子，其可靠性受训练数据与电子关联修正处理制约，尤其难以刻画极化子与给体的空间分离。

Abstract: Small polarons, formed when excess electrons become localized through lattice distortions, play a critical role in charge transport, catalysis, and degradation processes in transition-metal (TM) oxides. Whether universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs), which lack explicit electronic degrees of freedom, can describe them remains unclear. Here, we investigate H-induced electron localization across a range of TM oxides using a geometric descriptor based on TM-O bond-length expansion (Z-score) to identify polaronic distortions. We find that uMLIPs exhibit varying degrees of electron localization, from delocalized states to metastable small polarons, with their behavior strongly dependent on the training data and treatment of electronic correlations. In particular, the absence of Hubbard U corrections for specific TM cations in the training data leads to electron delocalization, in contrast to DFT+U predictions. Although some uMLIPs reproduce polaron hopping barriers during coupled proton-polaron transfer, all of them fail to stabilize spatially separated polaron-donor configurations, instead favoring localization near the H dopant.

</details>


### [122] [Collision Cascade Morphology and Defect Nucleation in Pure Tungsten and an Equimolar Refractory W-Ta-Cr-V Alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17361)
*Mark Fedorov,Francisco Javier Dominguez-Gutierrez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用机器学习势(tabGAP+ZBL)的分子动力学模拟，在0.1–100 keV初级撞出原子能量下对比纯W与WTaCrV难熔高熵合金的级联碰撞与缺陷形成机制，发现合金中更频繁的级联分裂及轻元素(Cr、V)的点缺陷容纳作用使其抗辐照损伤能力优于纯W。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估并理解WTaCrV难熔高熵合金相较于纯W在初级辐照损伤下的抗性机制，明确级联分裂与轻元素在缺陷形成与演化中的作用，为核材料（如面向等离子体材料）的抗辐照设计提供原子尺度依据。

Method: 分子动力学模拟：PKA能量范围0.1–100 keV；势函数采用机器学习表格化高斯近似势(tabGAP)，并辅以短程Ziegler-Biersack-Littmark (ZBL)相互作用描述高能近距离碰撞；分析热峰寿命、受影响区域、原子重定位及存活缺陷。

Result: 分析表明子级联(subcascade)形成及合金中轻元素的存在十分重要：级联分裂为子级联使PKA能量分布到更大区域，从而形核更少位错和更小空洞——空洞仅存在于纯W中，WTaCrV中未出现。级联分裂在WTaCrV中更频繁，故其抗初级辐照损伤能力优于纯W。此外，较轻较小的Cr(及程度较轻的V)以点缺陷形式容纳损伤，有助于抑制位错环形成。

Conclusion: WTaCrV难熔高熵合金因级联分裂更频繁以及Cr、V轻元素的点缺陷容纳效应，表现出比纯W更优的抗初级辐照损伤能力，其中子级联形成与轻元素作用是关键机制。

Abstract: Collision cascades and defect-formation mechanisms in pure W and the WTaCrV refractory high-entropy alloy were investigated using molecular dynamics simulations over primary knock-on atom (PKA) energies of 0.1-100 keV. Machine-learning tabulated Gaussian approximation potentials (tabGAP), complemented by short-range Ziegler-Biersack-Littmark (ZBL) interactions, were employed. Analysis of the heat spike lifetime, affected region, atomic relocations and surviving defects reveals an importance of subcascade formation and presence of light elements in the alloy. Cascade splitting into subcascades contributes to the distribution of the PKA energy over a larger region in the material, nucleating less dislocations and smaller voids - latter being present only in W and absent in WTaCrV. Since cascade splitting is more frequently observed in WTaCrV, the alloy shows better resistance to primary irradiation damage compared to pure W. Apart from cascade splitting, analysis of interstitials shows that Cr and to lesser extent V, being lighter and smaller elements, accommodate the damage in form of point defects, contributing to the mitigation of formation of the dislocation loops.

</details>


### [123] [High-Resolution X-ray Spectro-Microscopy of Au Microcrystallites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17417)
*Chaitali Sow,Martin Seyrich,Johannes Hagemann,Giridhar U. Kulkarni,Thomas F. Keller,Christian G. Schroer,Andreas Schropp*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用X射线光谱叠层成像（spectro-ptychography）在Au L3吸收边（约12 keV）实现了对金微晶的振幅与相位对比XANES成像，空间分辨率达45 nm，为高能X射线下的纳米化学成像提供可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异相催化剂功能的理解亟需高分辨纳米化学成像工具；X射线光谱叠层成像可基于XANES实现高分辨振幅与相位对比成像，但10 keV以上（如Au L边）因吸收截面弱、信号低而报道有限，尤其对同时含fcc与非立方晶格的金微晶催化体系尚缺高分辨成像手段。

Method: 对同时具有常规面心立方（fcc）与非立方晶格的金微晶开展多模态光谱显微成像；在Au L3吸收边采集振幅与相位对比数据，样品厚度150 nm至1.71 μm，空间分辨率45 nm；将所得XANES谱与标准透射和荧光对比谱进行对照，并定量反演约12 keV的异常相位变化。

Result: 在45 nm空间分辨率下成功分辨了跨L3边的振幅与相位对比；所得XANES谱与标准透射、荧光对比结果高度一致，同时提供了空间分辨率更高的互补化学信息；实现了约12 keV异常相位变化的定量反演。

Conclusion: 该工作证明高能X射线光谱叠层成像可在纳米尺度对金微晶进行可靠的化学成像，方法适用于广泛功能材料的高分辨化学成像。

Abstract: Understanding the functionalities of heterogeneous catalysts requires high-resolution nanoscale chemical imaging tools. X-ray spectro-ptychography offers this capability by enabling X-ray absorption near edge structure (XANES) based amplitude and phase contrasts imaging with high spatial resolution. However, such demonstrations at energies above 10 keV, such as the Au L-edges, remain limited due to weak X-ray absorption cross-sections and correspondingly, low signal levels. Here, multimodal spectro-microscopy has been performed on Au microcrystallites that host both conventional face-centered cubic (fcc) and unconventional non-cubic lattices, particularly important for catalysis. Amplitude and phase contrasts across the $\mathrm{L}_3$ edge are resolved on thin Au crystallites (150 nm to 1.71 $μ\mathrm{m}$) with 45 nm spatial resolution. The resulting XANES spectra exhibit excellent agreement with standard transmission and fluorescence contrasts, while providing complementary chemical insights with improved spatial resolution. Quantitative retrieval of anomalous phase variations ~12 keV further demonstrates the suitability of this method for high-resolution chemical imaging across a wide range of functional materials.

</details>


### [124] [Electric Edelstein Effect in Two Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17535)
*Xiaoran Lv,Tianqi Zhang,Chaoxi Cui,Yaling Li,Yi Shen,Weikang Wu,Ziming Zhu,Yugui Yao,Zhi-Ming Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出二维体系中的"电场Edelstein效应（EEE）"：面外栅压与面内电场协同作用产生自旋极化，实现栅压可调的自旋操控，并预言了MnSe₂、SnSe等材料中的显著效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性Edelstein效应（EE）的自旋极化方向由材料对称性固定，缺乏可调性；且在线性EE被对称性禁戒的体系（如中心对称材料）中无法产生自旋极化。作者希望找到一种普适、可调控的电学自旋操控机制，扩展自旋极化的控制维度。

Method: (1) 提出电场Edelstein效应：用交叉的面外栅电场与面内电场共同诱导自旋极化；(2) 对全部磁性层点群做对称性分析，判断EEE的容许性；(3) 建立微观理论，将EEE追溯到能带几何量（在带隙较小时显著增强）；(4) 结合第一性原理计算，在中心对称铁磁体MnSe₂与非磁体SnSe中验证。

Result: 对称性分析表明EEE在铁磁、反铁磁、交替磁体及非磁体系中广泛允许。EEE具有两种作用：在线性EE被禁戒的中心对称材料中产生自旋极化；在EE已存在的体系中叠加一个通常与常规分量非共线、且栅压可调的新分量。两个电场成为独立调控旋钮，将自旋极化控制维度从一维提升到二维。第一性原理计算预言MnSe₂（中心对称铁磁体，线性EE被禁戒）中效应显著；在EE与EEE共存的SnSe中，两个旋钮可覆盖自旋球面的扩展区域。

Conclusion: EEE确立为一种基于对称性、栅压可调的电学自旋操控途径，为二维量子材料中的多态自旋电子学开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We propose the electric Edelstein effect (EEE) in two-dimensional (2D) systems, where crossed out-of-plane and in-plane electric fields generate a spin polarization. The gate field converts the Edelstein response from a material-fixed spin polarization into a gate-tunable one. A symmetry analysis over all magnetic layer point groups shows that the EEE is allowed in a wide range of magnetic and nonmagnetic systems, including ferromagnets, antiferromagnets, and altermagnets. The EEE plays two distinct roles: it generates spin polarization where the linear Edelstein effect (EE) is symmetry-forbidden, as in centrosymmetric materials; and it adds a new gate-tunable component, generally non-collinear with the conventional one, where the EE is already present. The two fields then act as independent knobs and raise the control dimension of the spin polarization from one to two. Our microscopic theory traces the EEE to band-geometric quantities, which are strongly enhanced near small band gaps. First-principles calculations predict pronounced effects in the centrosymmetric ferromagnet MnSe2, where the linear EE is symmetry-forbidden. In the nonmagnet SnSe where the EE and EEE coexist, the two knobs cover an extended region of the spin sphere. Our findings establish the EEE as a symmetry-guided, gate-tunable route to electrical spin manipulation and open avenues for multistate spintronics in 2D quantum materials.

</details>


### [125] [A grand potential for molten-salt electrochemical phase diagrams from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17611)
*Lorenzo Bastonero,Lucio Colombi Ciacchi,Nicola Marzari*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出第一性原理框架，通过构建多组分体系的电化学相图来加速熔盐电解中电极与电解质的设计，并以硅-熔融CaCl₂体系和铝电解用卤化物电解质筛选验证了其预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 清洁能源转型推动金属需求激增，而金属提取是最大的工业碳排放源之一。熔盐电解有望实现铝、铁、钛及关键金属的无碳生产，但腐蚀性工况和复杂多组分原料使实验开发成本高、难度大，亟需理论工具指导电极、电解质及兼容材料与操作条件的选择。

Method: 建立多组分体系电化学相图的第一性原理框架：推导可规模化生成相图的大势（grand potential），并借助微调的基础机器学习原子间势获取第一性原理自由能数据。以硅在熔融CaCl₂中的体系进行验证，并用三个热力学判据筛选十种碱金属与碱土金属卤化物电解质用于铝电解。

Result: 对硅-熔融CaCl₂体系，该框架在有限结构集下即可重现实验相图，扩展到更大晶体结构集后还预测出新的相区；在铝电解筛选中，三个热力学判据识别出NaF体系，其中冰晶石（cryolite）成为稳定的含铝相，复现了Hall和Héroult于1886年凭经验确立的化学过程。

Conclusion: 该框架为从复杂原料出发、面向低碳金属生产选择兼容的电极、电解质和操作条件提供了第一性原理依据。

Abstract: The clean-energy transition is driving unprecedented demand for metals, whose extraction is among the largest industrial sources of carbon emissions. Molten-salt electrolysis offers a path toward carbon-free production of aluminum, iron, titanium, and critical metals. However, corrosive operating conditions and complex, multicomponent feedstocks make experimental development costly and challenging. Here, we introduce a first-principles framework for constructing electrochemical phase diagrams of multicomponent systems to accelerate electrode and electrolyte design and guide the selection of compatible materials and operating conditions for electrochemical cells. We derive a grand potential that generates these diagrams at scale, and supply free energies using first-principles data obtained through a fine-tuned foundation machine-learned interatomic potential. For silicon in molten CaCl$_2$, the construction not only recovers the experimental diagram when using the first-principles framework on a limited set of structures, but also predicts further domains when extended to a larger set of crystal structures. Finally, we demonstrate the predictive power of the approach by screening ten alkali and alkaline-earth halide electrolytes for aluminum electrowinning. Three thermodynamic criteria identify NaF, where cryolite emerges as the stable aluminum-bearing phase, recovering the chemistry that Hall and Héroult established empirically in 1886. The framework provides a first-principles basis for selecting compatible electrodes, electrolytes, and operating conditions toward low-carbon metal production from complex source materials.

</details>


### [126] [A Parameter-Free Reference Hamiltonian for Tight-Binding Vanadium Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17687)
*Jun-qiang Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文指出现有密度泛函紧束缚（DFTB）方法对钒氧化物的描述不可迁移，进而构建了一个无拟合参数、基于中性原子叠加参考与全PBE交换关联势的紧束缚模型，实现了与PAW/PBE参考高度吻合的能量与电荷响应，并揭示了自旋无关模型的凸性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准二阶DFTB对钒氧化物的描述缺乏可迁移性。在其近似中，V 3d与O 2p带在带指数质心分离上存在电子伏量级的偏差且相互抵消：中性双中心参考忽略了电子伏量级的环境项，而Mulliken电荷模型给紧致V 3d壳层分配的自洽势移不及PBE所观测值的一半。因此需要一个更准确的紧束缚描述。

Method: 构建了一个紧束缚模型：参考哈密顿量由叠加中性原子、完整PBE交换关联势和冻结芯正交化导出，不含可调项；轨道分辨的电荷响应用三个由PBE线性响应获得的屏蔽因子及一个未屏蔽库仑范围描述。

Result: 在固定几何与既定数值设置下，参考能量与VO2的平面波Harris-Foulkes能量差一致：M1相对金红石为+2.6 meV/atom（能量差为92 meV/atom），3%体积压缩与膨胀下分别为+6.2与-5.2 meV/atom。电荷响应在容差内捕获22个拟合PBE响应中的21个及全部8个留出响应，V 3d壳层自洽势移接近PBE（金红石中-3.89对-3.92 eV）。

Conclusion: 自旋无关模型在固定几何下的凸性，使其在实现算符与采样所遵从的对称性下无法自发区分对称等价格点。因此，Magnéli相中的此类区分必然源于电子-晶格耦合或显式的自旋/关联项。

Abstract: Density-functional tight binding has yet to yield a transferable description of vanadium oxides; for the standard second-order construction and the tested cells, we demonstrate that two of its approximations deviate by electron volts and offset each other in the band-index centroid separation between the V 3d and O 2p bands: the neutral two-centre reference omits electron-volt environmental terms, whereas the Mulliken charge model assigns the compact V 3d shell less than half of the self-consistent potential shift observed in PBE. Instead, we build a tight-binding model where the reference Hamiltonian derives from superposed neutral atoms, the full PBE exchange-correlation potential and frozen-core orthogonality, without adjustable terms, and where the orbital-resolved charge response employs three screening factors obtained from PBE linear response and an unscreened Coulomb range. Against the primary PAW reference, the reference energy at fixed geometry and the stated numerical settings matches the plane-wave Harris-Foulkes energy differences of VO2, exhibiting signed deviations of +2.6 meV/atom for M1 relative to rutile (an energy difference of 92 meV/atom) and +6.2 and -5.2 meV/atom under 3% volume compression and expansion; meanwhile, the charge response captures 21 of 22 fitted and all 8 held-out matched PBE responses within tolerance, providing the V 3d shell with nearly the same self-consistent shift as PBE (-3.89 versus -3.92 eV in rutile). The convexity of the spinless model at fixed geometry prevents it from spontaneously differentiating symmetry-equivalent sites under the symmetries respected by its implemented operators and sampling, so within this model such differentiation in the Magnéli phases must arise from electron-lattice coupling or an explicit spin or correlation term.

</details>


### [127] [Hydrogen-Bond Density Drives the Order-Disorder Phase Transition from Orientational to Vibrational in Polyalcohol Plastic Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17774)
*Ares Sanuy,Carlos Escorihuela-Sayalero,Pol Lloveras,María del Barrio,Hassan Allouchi,Josep-Lluís Tamarit,Luis Carlos Pardo,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文通过经典分子动力学模拟研究NPG、PG、PE三种新戊烷基塑晶的相变熵来源，发现随氢键连通性增加，相变熵从取向无序主导（NPG）反转为晶格振动主导（PE），PG处于两者平衡的中间区。振动通道对压力不敏感，因此氢键密度可作为调控压卡效应大小与压力稳定性的化学设计手柄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新戊烷基塑晶的有序-无序相变伴随极大熵变，是固态储热与压卡制冷的候选材料；但该熵变的微观本质究竟是各化合物的固有属性，还是可通过分子设计普遍理解并系统调控，尚不清楚。

Method: 对新戊戊基塑晶体系中的新戊二醇（NPG）、五甘油（PG）和季戊四醇（PE）进行经典分子动力学模拟，并以实验相变温度、焓变及相图进行验证；通过对比有序相与无序相的声子态密度、分子对称性以及不同熵通道（取向、构象、振动）的贡献随氢键连通性的变化来分析机制。

Result: 随氢键连通性增加，相变熵特征发生完全反转：NPG由取向无序主导；PE由晶格振动主导且对压力异常稳健；PG处于取向、构象、振动三通道贡献相当的平衡中间区。该反转源于两个耦合微观机制——氢键网络致密化使有序/无序相声子态密度间的低频间隙增大，以及分子对称性提高逐步抑制取向无序。

Conclusion: 氢键密度是化学上可及的实用设计参数，可调控氢键塑晶压卡响应的大小及其压力稳健性；因振动通道对压力远不如取向通道敏感，该规律对寻找新型压卡与储热材料具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: Neopentane-derived plastic crystals undergo order-disorder phase transitions accompanied by exceptionally large entropy changes, making them attractive solid-state materials for thermal energy storage and barocaloric cooling. Yet it remains unclear whether the microscopic nature of this entropy change is an intrinsic property of each compound or can be understood generally and tuned systematically through molecular design. Here, we use classical molecular dynamics simulations of neopentyl glycol (NPG), pentaglycerine (PG), and pentaerythritol (PE), validated against experimental transition temperatures, enthalpy changes, and phase diagrams, to show that the character of the phase-transition entropy undergoes a complete inversion as the hydrogen-bond connectivity of the molecule increases: orientational disordering dominates in NPG, whereas lattice vibrations dominate in PE and become remarkably robust against pressure. PG occupies a balanced intermediate regime in which the orientational, conformational, and vibrational channels contribute comparably. This inversion reflects two coupled microscopic mechanisms: a growing low-frequency gap between the phonon densities of states of the ordered and disordered phases as the hydrogen-bond network densifies, and an increase in molecular symmetry that progressively suppresses orientational disordering. Because the vibrational channel is substantially less sensitive to pressure than the orientational one, hydrogen-bond density emerges as a practical, chemically accessible design handle for tailoring not only the magnitude but also the pressure robustness of the caloric response in hydrogen-bonded plastic crystals, with direct implications for the search for new barocaloric and thermal-energy-storage materials.

</details>


### [128] [Learning from fracture: Teaching language models to design through physical experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17804)
*Bo Ni*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出FracLearn经验抽象-泛化框架，让语言模型通过"提出架构—模拟断裂响应—抽象条件性原理—在新架构中组合原理"的闭环，在无需更新模型权重的情况下实现物理设计的渐进优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型能否通过经验学习优化物理设计，即把计算实验转化为可复用的物理知识，从而支撑材料发现与工程设计中的科学智能体。

Method: 以刻意陌生的类俄罗斯方块断裂游戏为测试场景：模型先提出结构架构并观察模拟断裂响应以积累经验；再通过抽象将观察组织为条件性原理，关联加强件布置、裂纹演化与载荷传递，并附证据与失效条件；最后通过泛化，在新架构中组合这些原理以超越既有设计记录。学习依赖外部知识与决策，不进行模型权重更新。

Result: 多轮实验展示出渐进式改进，并通过假设修正实现性能恢复；最佳综合得分较经验阶段记录高出11.0%。断裂分析表明性能提升源于裂纹偏转与暂时捕获等增韧机制，同时残余承载能力提高。

Conclusion: 该框架证明语言模型可在不更新权重的前提下，将计算实验提炼为可复用的物理知识并用于设计优化，为材料发现与工程设计中的科学智能体提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: How can language models learn to optimize physical designs through experience? We introduce FracLearn, an experience-abstraction-generalization framework evaluated through a deliberately unfamiliar, Tetris-inspired fracture game. The model first proposes architectures and examines simulated fracture responses to accumulate experience. Abstraction organizes these observations into conditional principles linking reinforcement placement, crack evolution and load transfer, with supporting evidence and failure conditions. Generalization challenges the model to combine these principles in new architectures and surpass the earlier design record. Campaigns demonstrate progressive refinement and recovery through hypothesis revision, yielding a best composite score 11.0% above the experience-stage record. Fracture analyses connect improved performance to crack deflection and temporary trapping as toughening mechanisms, alongside greater residual load capacity. Learning occurs through external knowledge and decisions, without model-weight updates. This approach offers a route to scientific agents that turn computational experiments into reusable physical knowledge for materials discovery and engineering design.

</details>


### [129] [Polarons in Lead-Halide Perovskites: What the Structural Evidence Does and Does Not Establish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17809)
*Inhee Maeng,Young Mi Lee,Jinwoo Park,Seung Jae Oh,Min-Cherl Jung*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文质疑铅卤钙钛矿中"大极化子通过屏蔽带电缺陷实现缺陷容忍"的主流图景，并提出通过测量晶格畸变随激发密度的变化来分离极化子形成与缺陷容忍的实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流观点认为铅卤钙钛矿中的光生载流子以大极化子形式运动，其伴随的晶格畸变屏蔽载流子与带电缺陷的相互作用，从而解释缺陷容忍性。然而三项近期发现对此提出质疑：热运动本身即可提高载流子有效质量，使极化子判据失效；载流子-晶格耦合随维度降低而增强，且不同二维家族耦合通道不同；首个缺陷容忍直接测试未指向极化子屏蔽，而将缺陷容忍归因于柔软无序的晶格。因此需要重新审视极化子形成与缺陷容忍之间的因果联系。

Method: 本文为观点/综述性分析：梳理时间分辨结构探针、2D钙钛矿（Ruddlesden-Popper与Dion-Jacobson）载流子-晶格耦合通道对比、缺陷容忍直接测试及输运数据等证据，指出"极化子形成"与"极化子导致缺陷容忍"是两个独立命题，并提出需在跨越太阳光下低激发密度至超快实验高激发密度的范围内测量晶格畸变随激发密度的变化。

Result: 计算表明仅晶格热运动即可提高载流子有效质量，故有效质量增加不能作为极化子证据；二维钙钛矿中载流子-晶格耦合增强，RP与DJ家族行为差异由主导耦合通道决定而非仅由晶格刚度决定；首个缺陷容忍直接测试未支持极化子屏蔽机制；新输运数据显示极化子屏蔽降低载流子间散射，但未证明其保护载流子免受缺陷影响。

Conclusion: 极化子的存在（即便被证实）并不等于极化子是缺陷容忍的原因。区分二者需测量晶格畸变随激发密度的依赖关系，覆盖太阳能电池工作低密度与超快实验高密度范围。基于此，作者提出可厘清铅卤钙钛矿光生载流子行为的实验方案。

Abstract: Photogenerated carriers in lead-halide perovskites are widely believed to move as large polarons, whose accompanying lattice distortion is thought to screen the carrier from charged defects. Three recent findings call this picture into question. First, time-resolved structural probes now measure the photoinduced lattice distortion directly; yet calculations show that thermal motion of the lattice alone can raise the carrier effective mass-an increase previously taken as evidence for polarons-so that it remains unclear whether the lattice distortion around a carrier is really a polaron. Second, the carrier-lattice coupling strengthens as the material is thinned from 3D to 2D. In the two main families of 2D perovskites, Ruddlesden-Popper and Dion-Jacobson, the carrier couples to the lattice through different channels, and their contrasting behavior is set by which coupling channel dominates rather than by lattice stiffness alone. Third, the first direct test of defect tolerance does not point to polaron screening; its authors attribute defect tolerance to the soft, disordered lattice. New transport data show that polaron screening reduces scattering between carriers, but not that it protects carriers from defects. Polaron formation, even if established, therefore does not establish polarons as the cause of defect tolerance. Separating the two requires measuring the lattice distortion as a function of excitation density, over a range spanning the low densities of a solar cell under sunlight and the much higher densities used in most ultrafast experiments. On this basis, we propose experiments that can clarify how photogenerated carriers behave in lead-halide perovskites.

</details>


### [130] [Orthoexciton-to-paraexciton conversion rate in Cu$_2$O at sub-Kelvin temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17812)
*Yusuke Morita,Yukihiro Akiyama,Yuki Haita,Yuga Hirota,Kosuke Yoshioka*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用稀释制冷机，通过时间分辨发光光谱测量了Cu₂O中受限1s正激子向1s仲激子的转换速率，发现在1 K以下转换速率继续下降且对温度依赖很弱，与以往预期不符，需新理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究预期正激子向仲激子的转换速率在约1.7 K以下趋于常数，但缺乏低于1 K的低温实验验证，因此需要直接测量极低温下的转换速率及其温度依赖关系。

Method: 在低于1 K、施加2.2 kbar应力条件下，使用稀释制冷机对限制在应变诱导势阱中的1s正激子和1s仲激子进行时间分辨发光光谱测量，通过分析正激子Γ₃⁻-LO声子辅助发光的时问演化来确定转换速率。

Result: 在混合室温度64 mK下测得转换速率为5.9×10⁷ s⁻¹；在64至800 mK范围内转换速率仅弱依赖于温度，且低于同应力下2 K时报道的1.6×10⁸ s⁻¹的一半。

Conclusion: 转换速率在2 K以下持续下降，而非先前预期在约1.7 K以下趋于常数；这一现象可用正激子动量分布在低温转换过程中未与晶格达到平衡来解释，亟需能同时解释转换速率偏小及其弱温度依赖性的新理论。

Abstract: We measured the conversion rate from 1s orthoexcitons to 1s paraexcitons in Cu$_2$O below 1 K using a dilution refrigerator. To determine the conversion rate, we performed time-resolved luminescence spectroscopy of 1s orthoexcitons and 1s paraexcitons confined in a strain-induced trap potential. From the time evolution of the $Γ$$_3$$^{-}$-LO-phonon-assisted luminescence of 1s orthoexcitons, we obtained a conversion rate of $5.9 \times 10^7$ s${^{-1}}$ at a mixing-chamber temperature of 64 mK under an applied stress of 2.2 kbar. Between 64 and 800 mK, the conversion rate depends only weakly on temperature, and it is less than half of the value of $1.6 \times 10^8$ s${^{-1}}$ reported at 2 K under the same applied stress. The conversion rate therefore continues to decrease below 2 K, contrary to the previous expectation that it becomes nearly constant below about 1.7 K. We discuss these results in terms of the momentum distribution of orthoexcitons, which does not reach equilibrium with the lattice during the conversion at these low temperatures. These findings call for a theory that accounts for both the small magnitude of the conversion rate and its weak temperature dependence below 1 K.

</details>


### [131] [Colossal Spin--Lattice--Charge Coupling in Noncollinear Ferrimagnetic NbMnX$_2$ ($X$ = S, Se, Te) Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17849)
*Peng Wang,Nanshu Liu,Hongkuan Yuan,Xue Jiang,Jijun Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文预测NbMnX₂（X=S、Se、Te）单层为非共线亚铁磁体，居里温度最高达313 K，并展现出磁弹耦合、磁热效应与巨大各向异性磁电阻等多功能特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共线亚铁磁体兼具净磁矩与内部自旋自由度，有望将磁、力、热、电响应耦合起来，但缺乏稳定且居里温度接近室温的二维材料平台。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算、对称性分析、原子尺度自旋动力学模拟与输运建模，系统研究NbMnX₂单层的磁基态与物性。

Result: 确认非共线亚铁磁基态，居里温度最高313 K；交换作用、Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用与磁各向异性共同形成场响应倾斜磁态；NbMnTe₂磁化转动使弹性模量变化达36%；NbMnS₂与NbMnSe₂的磁热响应因晶格熵而增强；NbMnSe₂中转动恢复镜面对称保护的能带交叉，连续闭合约0.13 eV能隙，100 K下电导型各向异性磁电阻比高达10,000%。

Conclusion: NbMnX₂单层可作为二维自旋电子学、纳米力学与热调控的多功能材料平台。

Abstract: Noncollinear ferrimagnets combine a net magnetic moment with internal spin degrees of freedom, creating opportunities to couple magnetic, mechanical, thermal, and electronic responses. Using first-principles calculations, symmetry analysis, atomistic spin simulations, and transport modeling, we identify stable NbMnX$_2$ ($X$ = S, Se, Te) monolayers with noncollinear ferrimagnetic ground states and Curie temperatures up to 313 K. Competing exchange, Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions, and magnetic anisotropy produce field-responsive canted states that couple strongly to the lattice and charge carriers. Magnetization rotation changes the elastic modulus of NbMnTe$_2$ by up to 36\%, while lattice entropy substantially enhances the near-room-temperature magnetocaloric response of NbMnS$_2$ and NbMnSe$_2$. In NbMnSe$_2$, rotation restores a mirror-protected crossing, continuously closes an approximately 0.13 eV gap, and produces a conductivity-based anisotropic magnetoresistance ratio up to 10,000\% at 100 K. The combined results establish noncollinear NbMnX$_2$ monolayers as a multifunctional platform for two-dimensional spintronics, nanomechanics, and thermal control.

</details>


### [132] [ARES: a plane-wave density-functional-theory code in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17854)
*Zehan Liu,Qian Wang,Xiangyu Zheng,Jiaxiang Li,Chuntian Wu,Yongshuo Chen,Xuecheng Shao,Qiang Xu,Yu Xie,Yanming Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: ARES是一个完全用Rust编写、以22个crate分层组织的平面波DFT软件，其核心特色是自动化、可复现的验证体系：所有数值组件通过与Quantum ESPRESSO在同一输入下的对比校验，并以可再生的编号fixture形式维护；在六种材料上费米能级1 eV内带能中位偏差低于0.4 meV，65个元素的赝势delta因子中位值0.696 meV/atom。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平面波DFT代码多以功能覆盖为卖点，但数值正确性的验证往往依赖一次性抓取的参考数据，缺乏可复现、可持续更新的验证机制。本文旨在构建一个以"验证装置"为设计核心的DFT实现，使每个数值组件都能在与成熟参考代码（Quantum ESPRESSO）相同物理与数值输入下被系统比对。

Method: 以Rust实现平面波DFT代码ARES，采用22-crate分层工作区架构。理论层面支持模守恒赝势与PAW方法、LDA与PBE泛函、Γ点与显式k点采样，并包含展宽与混合、力、应力、结构弛豫、能带、态密度与色散修正。并行方面支持串行或MPI按k点池、能带组、平面波行分布。验证方法上，将每个数值组件与Quantum ESPRESSO基于相同物理和数值输入进行对比，对比以仓库内编号fixture组织，参考数据由脚本重新生成而非一次性固定。

Result: 在覆盖一个半导体、三个金属和两个二维体系的六种材料上，费米能级1 eV范围内的带能与参考值对比，中位偏差低于0.4 meV。代码所读取的赝势亦与全电子参考数据校验，全部65对元素的中位delta因子为0.696 meV/atom。

Conclusion: ARES通过"与参考代码同输入对比+可再生fixture"的验证框架，展示了Rust实现的平面波DFT代码可达到meV量级的数值一致性。作者同时明确列出能力边界：仅两种泛函、无自旋极化与自旋轨道路径、应力实现有明确适用范围、不支持加速器执行。

Abstract: ARES (Ab initio atomic mateRial modEling Software at JLU) is a plane-wave density-functional-theory (DFT) code written entirely in Rust as a layered 22-crate workspace. It supports norm-conserving pseudopotentials and the projector augmented-wave (PAW) method with the local-density approximation (LDA) and Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof (PBE) functionals, Γ-only and explicit k-point sampling, smearing and mixing, forces, stress, relaxation, band structures, densities of states (DOS) and dispersion corrections; execution is serial or MPI-distributed over k-point pools, band groups and plane-wave rows. The code's distinguishing engineering choice is its validation apparatus. Every numerical component is checked against Quantum ESPRESSO driven from the same physical and numerical inputs, and the comparison is organized as numbered fixtures kept in the repository whose reference data is regenerated by a script instead of being captured once. On six materials spanning a semiconductor, three metals and two two-dimensional systems, band energies within one electronvolt of the Fermi level agree with the reference to a median below 0.4 meV. The pseudopotentials the code reads are validated against all-electron reference data as well: the median delta factor is 0.696 meV/atom over all 65 paired elements. Restrictions are stated with the capabilities: two functionals, no spin-polarized or spin-orbit path, a stress implementation with an explicit scope, and no accelerator execution.

</details>


### [133] [Thermal stability and structural ordering of thick Co$_2$MnAl films grown on MgO and Si substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17955)
*David Robles-Cuenca,Juan Antonio González,Juan Pedro Andrés,Miguel Ángel Arranz,Mohamed Salaheldeen,Ricardo López Antón*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究制备了750 nm厚Co₂MnAl薄膜，比较MgO(001)与Si衬底上的热稳定性，确立厚膜热集成工艺窗口，为CMOS兼容多功能平台提供路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Co₂MnAl研究多聚焦超薄自旋电子学结，而异常能斯特效应能量收集器、集成功率电感与磁MEMS等按磁体积缩放的应用需要更厚的薄膜，厚膜的衬底热集成行为尚不明确。

Method: 在MgO(001)和Si衬底上生长750 nm厚CMA薄膜，进行结构与磁学表征，考察退火温度下的B2/A2有序度演化、界面扩散、硅化物与Kirkendall空洞形成及微裂纹和电阻率变化。

Result: MgO上600 °C时B2有序度最优；Si衬底样品发生向A2相的无序转变，500 °C以上Si互扩散生成界面硅化物并引起微裂纹与电阻率跃升；尽管Mn贫化学计量（Co2.25Mn0.7Al1.05），饱和磁化强度仍稳定在约600 emu/cm³，与离化学计量Heusler合金的理论模型一致。

Conclusion: 研究界定了厚CMA层集成到多功能CMOS兼容平台的工艺窗口：MgO衬底有利于B2有序化，而Si衬底在500 °C以上因扩散退化需限制热处理温度。

Abstract: While most studies on Co$2$MnAl (CMA) focus on the ultrathin regime for spintronic junctions, applications scaling with magnetic volume--such as Anomalous Nernst Effect (ANE) energy harvesters, integrated power inductors, and magnetic MEMS--require significantly thicker films. In this work, 750 nm thick CMA films were grown on MgO (001) and Si substrates to establish a roadmap for their thermal integration. Structural analysis reveals that B2 ordering is optimized on MgO at 600 $^\circ$C, which is important for functional devices. In contrast, samples on Si substrates exhibit a disorder transition toward the A2 phase, closely linked to diffusion-driven degradation processes. Above 500 $^\circ$C, Si interdiffusion into the Heusler layer triggers the formation of interfacial silicides and Kirkendall voids, leading to microcracking, electrical resistivity jumps, and a final fully disordered A2 phase. Despite the Mn-poor stoichiometry (Co$2.25$Mn$0.7$Al$1.05$), the films maintain a robust saturation magnetization of ~600 emu/cm$^3$, consistent with recent theoretical models for off-stoichiometric Heusler alloys. These results define the processing windows for integrating thick CMA layers into multifunctional CMOS-compatible platforms.

</details>


### [134] [Field-free Magnetoacoustic Nonreciprocity via Exchange-bias Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17979)
*Weiqi Du,Mingyuan Ma,Zheng Sun,Chong Chen,Shixuan Liang,Deqi Tao,Shihai An,Yifan Fu,Sulei Fu,Feng Pan,Cheng Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过在IrMn/FeGaB/Cu/FeGaB多层膜与剪切水平波延迟线集成结构中利用交换偏置工程稳定零场剩余磁态，实现了无外场磁声非互易传播，归一化隔离度达21 dB/mm且符号可编程，数值优化预测可达91 dB/mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易波传播对定向信号处理与隔离至关重要，磁声系统因小尺寸、无源工作而具吸引力，但显著非互易性通常依赖外偏置磁场，限制了器件小型化与集成。本文旨在实现无需外场的磁声非互易性。

Method: 构建IrMn/FeGaB/Cu/FeGaB多层膜并集成剪切水平波延迟线；采用交换偏置工程稳定同时打破时间反演与空间反演对称性的剩余磁态，并调控自旋波色散以在零场下实现共振磁声耦合；进一步通过数值优化增大动态磁不对称性。

Result: 实验实现零场归一化隔离度21 dB/mm，其符号可由磁场扫描历史编程；数值优化预测通过增大动态磁不对称性可将零场归一化隔离度提升至91 dB/mm。

Conclusion: 交换偏置工程为紧凑型射频隔离器与环行器提供了可行路径，也是实现无需外磁场的磁声器件的通用策略。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal wave propagation is essential for directional signal processing and isolation. Magnetoacoustic systems are attractive platforms for nonreciprocal devices because of their small footprint and passive operation. However, pronounced nonreciprocity typically requires an external bias field,limiting device miniaturization and integration. Here, we demonstrate field-free magnetoacoustic nonreciprocity in an IrMn/FeGaB/Cu/FeGaB multilayer integrated with a shear-horizontal-wave delay line. Exchange-bias engineering stabilizes a remanent magnetic state with broken time-reversal and spatial-inversion symmetries while tailoring the spin-wave dispersion to achieve resonant magnetoacoustic coupling at zero field. The normalized isolation reaches 21 dB/mm, with its sign programmable by the field-sweep history. Further numerical optimization predicts a zero-field normalized isolation of 91 dB/mm by increasing the dynamical magnetic asymmetry. Our work establishes exchange-bias engineering as a promising route toward compact RF isolators and circulators and as a general strategy for realizing magnetoacoustic devices without external magnetic fields.

</details>


### [135] [Knowledge-Guided Construction of Machine Learning Potentials for Reactive Behavior in Energetic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17987)
*Guanchen Dong,Huanrong Liu,Lin Wang,Yuxuan Chen,Zhengyu Tu,Qing-an Li,Bin Xu,Jian Li,Shan Zhang,Xiaotong Li,Linghua Tan,Guangcheng Yang,Jun Kang,Rui Su,Pengfei Guan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出sphmulti球谐基组并结合主动学习策略，仅用13680个构型即构建出高可靠性的神经网络势，成功描述含能材料DAAzF的多种关键性质与动力学机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含能材料涉及多重复杂相互作用，构建可靠的机器学习势通常依赖大规模数据集，数据成本高、效率低，亟需在有限数据下提升势函数的描述能力。

Method: 提出精细化球谐基组sphmulti，在每条径向通道中结合额外径向基与元素特异性化学权重，增强神经网络原子间势（NNAP）对分子体系的描述能力；并将超活跃学习、最远点采样、迭代充分性判据与知识引导的关键事件相结合，实现高效数据采样。

Result: 针对3,3'-二氨基-4,4'-偶氮氧化呋咱（DAAzF），仅用13,680个构型训练的NNAP即可准确再现异构化势垒、热解活化能和冲击雨贡纽行为；模拟揭示热解经历呋咱环开环后链式反应，冲击加载则因层间结构变形诱发各向异性氢转移。

Conclusion: 该工作为含能材料提供了高效、可靠的机器学习势构建方案，在显著降低数据需求的同时保持对关键性能与反应机理的准确描述。

Abstract: Energetic materials involve multiple interactions, making reliable machine-learning potentials (MLPs) difficult to construct without extensive datasets. Here, we enhance the ability of the neural network interatomic potential (NNAP) to describe molecular systems using sphmulti, a refined spherical-harmonic basis set that combines extra radial basis with element-specific chemical weighting in each radial channel. We further integrate hyperactive learning, farthest-point sampling, and iterative sufficiency criteria with knowledge-guided key events, enabling the NNAP to retain essential information for describing the material system. For 3,3'-diamino-4,4'-azoxyfurazan, the resulting NNAP is trained on only 13,680 configurations yet captures key properties, including the isomerization barrier, pyrolysis activation energy, and shock Hugoniot behavior. NNAP-driven molecular dynamics simulations further reveal that pyrolysis proceeds through furazan-ring opening followed by chain reactions, whereas shock loading induces anisotropic hydrogen transfer driven by interlayer structural deformations. This work enables efficient and reliable MLPs for energetic materials.

</details>


### [136] [Hydrogen partial pressure as a key parameter in the reactive sputtering of photochromic YHO thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18068)
*E. Strods,M. Zubkins,E. Letko,V. Vibornijs,A. Sarakovskis,J. Purans*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过反应磁控溅射在不同H2/Ar流量比下制备含氧氢化钇（YHO）薄膜并后续空气氧化，发现低H2/Ar有利于提高光学透明性、光致变色对比度和漂白速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究氢气分压（H2/Ar流量比）对YHO薄膜的晶体织构、光学性能及光致变色行为的影响，并厘清氧含量与光致变色响应之间的关系。

Method: 反应磁控溅射沉积YHO薄膜（H2/Ar范围宽），随后空气中后氧化；等离子体光学发射光谱原位监测沉积过程；XRD分析织构与晶粒尺寸；光学透射与太阳模拟器光照测试光致变色对比度；双指数模型拟合漂白动力学；XPS表征氧 incorporation。

Result: 低H2/Ar时形成高度<100>织构，随氢含量增加逐渐多晶化且晶粒尺寸略减；透射率从约85%降至70%（H2/Ar升至0.6），更高氢含量时急剧下降；690-820 nm厚膜的光致变色对比度随H2/Ar从0.08升至0.60由14%降至7%，薄膜（360-490 nm）趋势相似但较弱；H2/Ar<0.25时有效漂白时间常数约1-15 min；低H2/Ar样品氧 incorporation 更高，偏离常规氧含量与光致变色响应的相关性。

Conclusion: 反应溅射过程中低氢分压有利于获得更高光学透明性、增强的光致变色对比度及更快漂白动力学的YHO薄膜。

Abstract: Oxygen-containing yttrium hydride (YHO) thin films were synthesised by reactive magnetron sputtering over a wide range of hydrogen-to-argon gas flow ratios (H2/Ar) during yttrium hydride deposition, followed by post-oxidation in air. The deposition process was monitored in situ by plasma optical emission spectroscopy. X-ray diffraction reveals a strong dependence of crystallographic texture on the H2/Ar ratio: highly <100>-textured films formed at low H2/Ar ratios progressively evolve into more polycrystalline microstructures with slightly reduced crystallite size as the hydrogen content increases. The optical transmittance gradually decreases from approximately 85 % to 70 % as the H2/Ar ratio is raised to 0.6, followed by a sharp drop at higher hydrogen contents. The photochromic contrast of 690-820 nm thick YHO films, measured after 10 min of illumination with a solar simulator, decreases from 14 % to 7 % as the H2/Ar ratio increases from 0.08 to 0.60. Thinner films (360-490 nm) exhibit a similar but less pronounced trend. Bleaching kinetics, analysed using a double-exponential model, indicate faster recovery, with effective bleaching time constants of approximately 1-15 min for films deposited at H2/Ar < 0.25. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy reveals higher oxygen incorporation in films deposited at lower H2/Ar ratios, deviating from the commonly reported correlation between oxygen content and photochromic response in YHO. These results demonstrate that low hydrogen partial pressure during reactive sputtering is advantageous for achieving higher optical transparency, enhanced photochromic contrast, and faster bleaching kinetics in YHO thin films.

</details>


### [137] [Tellurium trioxide as an n-type wide-gap semiconductor with ultrahigh work functions from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18076)
*Tianwei Wang,Teruya Nagafuji,Kota Hanzawa,Hidenori Hiramatsu,Fumiyasu Oba*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算,提出宽禁带β-TeO₃是一种具有超高功函数(~8 eV)的n型半导体,并系统研究了各类点缺陷与表面构型对其电子性质及功函数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代电子与光电器件需要兼具宽禁带和极端功函数的材料。β-TeO₃具有超过3 eV的宽禁带,且可能具备超高功函数,有望用于空穴注入/提取/传输层及透明电极,但其可实现的掺杂类型、点缺陷行为以及表面/功函数调控机制尚不清楚,亟需系统研究。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算,评估β-TeO₃的禁带宽度与功函数;系统考察间隙杂质(H、Li、Na、Cu、Mg、Zn)和替位F杂质以及氧空位的缺陷行为;并对一系列低指数非极性表面的功函数进行计算,分析有序氧空位对表面功函数的影响。

Result: β-TeO₃禁带宽度超过3 eV,功函数高达约8 eV,为n型半导体。间隙H、Li、Na、Cu、Mg、Zn以及替位F均表现为浅施主;氧空位是次要的载流子补偿缺陷,会在邻近Te位点局域俘获电子。在所研究的低指数非极性表面上,有序氧空位的形成在能量上有利,并倾向于降低相应表面的功函数。

Conclusion: 精确控制氧化学势以抑制表面氧空位形成,对保持β-TeO₃超高功函数的优异电子性能至关重要。β-TeO₃在空穴注入/提取/传输层以及透明电极(如发光器件、光伏电池)方面具有应用潜力,且通过阐明点缺陷与表面构型对电子性质的影响,可为载流子掺杂与表面工程提供指导。

Abstract: The exploration of next-generation wide-gap semiconductors with extreme work functions is highly desirable for the continued advancement of electronic and optoelectronic devices. By using first principles calculations, we show that \b{eta}-TeO3, with a wide band gap exceeding 3 eV, emerges as a promising n-type semiconductor exhibiting ultrahigh work functions of up to ~8 eV. Intentionally or unintentionally incorporated interstitial H, Li, Na, Cu, Mg, and Zn impurities and substitutional F impurities are identified as shallow donors, whereas O vacancies act as minor carrier-compensating defects that locally trap electrons at neighbouring Te sites. The work function is evaluated across a series of low-index nonpolar surfaces. The presence of ordered O vacancies is energetically favoured on these surfaces and tends to reduce the corresponding work functions. These findings underscore the significance of precise control over the O chemical potential to suppress surface O-vacancy formation, thereby preserving the exceptional electronic performance of \b{eta}-TeO3 associated with ultrahigh work functions. Collectively, the results highlight the potential of \b{eta}-TeO3 for hole injection/extraction/transport layers as well as transparent electrodes for applications such as light-emitting devices and photovoltaic cells, while providing useful guidance on carrier doping and surface tuning for these practical applications through elucidating the effects of the point-defect and surface configurations on the electronic properties.

</details>


### [138] [Local-resolvent formulation and assessment of linear-scaling Kohn-Sham calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18137)
*Albert Linda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出局域预解式密度泛函理论(LRes-DFT)，用少量复能量点上单电子预解式的对角元替代轨道，避免动能泛函，进而用于大规模绝缘体计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kohn-Sham DFT依赖覆盖全体系的轨道，计算代价高。对绝缘体希望避免显式求解轨道，寻找只需局域信息的等价表述。

Method: 利用预解式对角元 g(r,z)=⟨r|(z−ĥ)⁻¹|r⟩，在M个环绕占据能级的圆周上等间距复能量点取值；求和等价于以平滑阶跃 1/(1+u^M) 占据各能级。结合Gauss-Legendre与Zolotarev求积规则；三维体系中对每个分子小窗口取本征对，实现带不同占据函数的分治(DC)方案。

Result: 对含至1024电子的H₂分子晶体，与Kohn-Sham符合到每电子2.5×10⁻⁷ Ha；单核下约500电子以下比稠密对角化慢，1024电子时快3.5倍；对无序、压缩体系及弥散基函数精度下降。

Conclusion: LRes-DFT通过复能量点预解式对角元实现无需轨道与动能泛函的绝缘体DFT，随体系增大显现效率优势，但精度受体系无序、压缩及基组弥散程度限制。

Abstract: Kohn-Sham density-functional theory is expensive because it needs orbitals that extend over the whole system. For insulators this can be avoided by using only the diagonal of the one-electron resolvent, $g(\mathbf{r},z)=\langle\mathbf{r}|(z-\hat h)^{-1}|\mathbf{r}\rangle$, at a few complex energies $z$. We call the scheme local-resolvent density-functional theory (LRes-DFT). The density and the band energy are linear in $g$, so neither orbitals nor a kinetic-energy functional are needed. The complex energies are $M$ equally spaced points on a circle that encloses the occupied levels, offset by half a spacing so that no point lies on the real axis. Summing the resolvent over them is equivalent to occupying each level with $1/(1+u^M)$, where $u$ is the level's energy measured from the circle's center in units of its radius. This is a smooth step that is near 1 inside the circle and near 0 outside, and it stays between 0 and 1 for every $M$. If the circle moves with the chemical potential, forces at fixed electron number are exact. Gauss-Legendre and Zolotarev rules need fewer points when the gap is small. In three dimensions we use eigenpairs of small windows around each molecule instead of resolvents. This is divide-and-conquer (DC) with a different occupation function, and it is as accurate as DC with Fermi-Dirac occupations. For H$_2$ molecular crystals of up to 1024 electrons it agrees with Kohn-Sham to $2.5\times10^{-7}$ Ha per electron. On one core it is slower than dense diagonalization below about 500 electrons and 3.5 times faster at 1024 electrons. It loses accuracy for disordered or compressed systems and for diffuse basis functions.

</details>


### [139] [High-throughput screening of Heusler alloys for thermoelectrics combining machine-learned densities of states and first-principles transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18179)
*Shivam Randive,Ayana Ghosh,Rahul Dixit,Himanshu Pandey*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在DXMag数据库全部106,235个Heusler结构中系统检验了基于态密度(DOS)的筛选指标的有效性：对51,257个结构使用DFT谱、54,978个使用神经网络预测谱进行筛选，并以3,625次能带结构与输运计算作为判据。结果显示DOS导出的Seebeck指标可将大多数通过全部输运与稳定性测试的化合物排在族群前1%，但预测谱会丢失大部分带隙，而谱相似性度量对此不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热电材料可将废热转化为电能，但发现新材料所需的输运计算成本过高，无法对整库材料逐一计算。因此搜索流程通常依据电子态密度(DOS)来决定"计算什么"，且DOS越来越多地由机器学习预测。然而，这种基于DOS的筛选选择此前很少被系统检验，其可靠性存疑。

Method: 以DXMag数据库中全部106,235个Heusler结构为对象，其中51,257个基于密度泛函(DFT)谱筛选、54,978个基于神经网络预测谱筛选；随后用3,625次能带结构与输运计算对两种筛选结果进行统一评判，比较DOS导出的Seebeck指标与谱相似性度量的排序表现。

Result: DOS导出的Seebeck指标将大多数通过全部输运与稳定性测试的化合物排在其族群前1%以内，11个已确立的半Heusler热电材料中有10个通过计算检验。神经网络预测谱丢失了大部分带隙，且这一缺陷未被谱相似性度量察觉，但预测谱仍能将通过的化合物排在前列。筛选最终确认80个计算候选材料，其中61个尚无实验测量报道。

Conclusion: 无论采用计算谱还是预测谱，DOS都可用于决定"计算什么"，即作为高效的高通量预筛选指标；但只有实际的能带结构才能判断带隙是否存在。因此机器学习预测的DOS可用于排序筛选，却不能替代能带结构计算来确认关键电子结构特征。

Abstract: Thermoelectrics turn waste heat into electricity, but finding new ones requires transport calculations too costly for whole databases. Searches therefore choose what to calculate from the electronic density of states (DOS), increasingly predicted by machine learning, yet such choices are rarely tested. We test them on all 106,235 Heusler structures in the DXMag database, screening 51,257 on density-functional spectra and 54,978 on neural-network predictions, and judging both by 3,625 band-structure and transport calculations. A DOS-derived Seebeck indicator ranks most compounds passing every transport and stability test within the top 1% of their population, and ten of the eleven established half-Heusler thermoelectrics calculated pass. Predicted spectra lose most band gaps, unnoticed by spectral-similarity metrics, yet still rank passing compounds near the top. The screen certifies 80 computational candidates, 61 without reported measurements. Computed or predicted, the DOS can choose what to calculate; only a band structure shows whether a gap exists.

</details>


### [140] [Orientation-Resolved Nanoindentation of Single Crystal Ti-15Mo: Inverted Elastic Anisotropy and Yield-Onset Behavior Beyond Simple CRSS Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18181)
*Kidus Abebe,David Gaetano,Aurelia Moriyama-Gurish,Jana Šmilauerová,Yanfei Gao,Udo D. Schwarz,Amit Datye*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合EBSD与纳米压痕技术量化了单晶Ti-15Mo的纳米力学各向异性，发现弹性各向异性相对立方β相预期发生反转（<111>取向模量最低），屈服起始应力跨度达41%且最软取向在<111>附近，而硬度仅变化6.6%且排序与模量、屈服均不一致，并揭示应变速率敏感性与塑性机制的取向依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ti-15Mo的力学响应受相稳定性与单晶尺度变形机制控制，这些晶内机制具有本征各向异性，因此理解弹性与塑性响应的取向依赖性对织构工程化锻造加工至关重要。然而现有简单CRSS/Schmid因子模型难以描述该合金的取向相关屈服行为，需要实验量化其纳米力学各向异性并澄清其微观机制来源。

Method: 采用EBSD与纳米压痕相结合，将硬度、压痕模量、应变速率敏感性和压痕屈服响应与靠近⟨111⟩、⟨124⟩、⟨011⟩、⟨023⟩、⟨013⟩方向的晶体学表面法向进行关联；并通过应变速率跳跃试验测定激活体积V*，与CRSS/Schmid因子模型对比。

Result: 模量各向异性较小（约10%），但取向近⟨111⟩时模量最低，与A>1立方晶体预期相反，出现弹性各向异性反转。屈服起始应力在近⟨111⟩处最软，跨度达41%；硬度跨度仅6.6%，其排序既不遵循模量也不遵循屈服次序。屈服行为无法被所评估的简单CRSS/Schmid因子模型捕获。应变速率跳跃试验得到V*=18–31b³，符合扭结对形核控制的塑性；基于V*可将取向分为两类，分别对应滑移主导与孪生/相变参与的响应。

Conclusion: Ti-15Mo单晶表现出与均质立方β相预期相反的弹性各向异性反转，以及无法用简单Schmid因子模型重现的取向相关屈服起始响应。提出残余无热ω相是主要贡献者：β→ω塌缩发生在{111}β面上，并可通过可逆的ω→β转变提供滞弹性柔量。

Abstract: Ti-15Mo (wt%) is a well-established $β$-stabilized alloy whose mechanical response is strongly governed by phase stability and deformation mechanisms that operate at the single-grain scale. Because these intragranular mechanisms are intrinsically anisotropic, understanding the orientation dependence of the elastic and plastic response is critical to the texture-engineered processing of wrought components. Here, we quantify the nanomechanical anisotropy of single-crystal Ti-15Mo by combining EBSD with nanoindentation to correlate hardness, indentation modulus, strain rate sensitivity, and indentation yield response with the crystallographic surface normal near $\langle111\rangle,\langle124\rangle,\langle011\rangle,\langle023\rangle$, and $\langle013\rangle$ directions. While the modulus anisotropy is small (~10%), contrary to the prediction for a cubic crystal with A>1, orientations near $\langle111\rangle$ exhibit the lowest moduli. Yield onset is likewise softest near $\langle111\rangle$ and spans 41%, while hardness spans only 6.6% and follows neither the modulus nor the yield ordering. The alloy also exhibits yield behavior not captured by the simple CRSS/Schmid-factor models evaluated here. Strain-rate-jump tests yield V*=18-31b$^3$, consistent with kink-pair-nucleation-controlled plasticity; based on V*, the orientations separate into two groups suggestive of glide-dominated versus twin/transformation-participating response. We reveal an inversion of the elastic anisotropy expected for a homogeneous cubic $β$-phase and an orientation-dependent yield-onset response that is not reproduced by the simple Schmid-factor models examined here. Retained athermal $ω$ is proposed as the contributor because $β\rightarrowω$ collapse occurs on {111}$_β$ planes and may provide anelastic compliance through the reversible $ω\rightarrowβ$ transformation.

</details>


### [141] [MINFF: A Family of Classical Force Fields for Minerals with Tunable Angle Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18220)
*Michael Holmboe,Nathan Lavauzelle,Rogers E. Swai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出MINFF力场家族，用于Si、Al、Fe、Mg、Ca、Ti、Li、F等元素构成的层状与非层状氧化物及氢氧化物。该力场摒弃传统M-O键项，改用电荷与色散作用确定M-O距离，并以单一谐波角项控制配位几何，在30余种参考矿物上同时进行全局（GMINFF）与单矿物（TMINFF）参数拟合。结果显示MINFF可将晶胞参数误差控制在0.5%以内，原子间距与键角误差分别为0.7-1.4%和0.1-0.4%，全局不稳定性指数低至0.11，优于CLAYFF（0.34）与INTERFACE（0.21），并可通过角力常数调节精度—可迁移性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矿物原子模拟的精度与可迁移性受限于力场质量：传统力场依赖显式M-O键项且参数迁移性差，难以兼顾多种矿物体系。作者旨在构建一套无需M-O键项、能够同时适用于层状与非层状氧化物/氢氧化物、并与水模型兼容的通用力场框架。

Method: MINFF不设M-O键项，M-O距离由静电作用与色散作用自然产生，配位几何通过作用于所有M-O三体的单一谐波角项控制。金属电荷取自参考矿物结构的DDEC6电荷，氧电荷由最近邻电荷涂抹法分配，覆盖基面、顶面、羟基、边缘位点及同晶取代邻近位点。氧的Lennard-Jones参数基于OPC3水模型（再优化后变化小于5%），金属LJ参数以晶胞参数、键长和键角为拟合目标，分别在30余种参考矿物上同步拟合（通用GMINFF）和按单矿物拟合（定制TMINFF），每组均在0、250、500、1500 kJ/mol/rad²四种角力常数下完成。

Result: 含角项时，MINFF将实验晶胞参数复现误差控制在0.5%以内，原子类型间距与键角误差分别为0.7-1.4%和0.1-0.4%，全局不稳定性指数低至0.11，优于CLAYFF（0.34）和INTERFACE（0.21）。中等角力常数可复现层状硅酸盐刚性的面内弹性常数与较软的剪切弹性常数，并改善高岭石摇摆振动带；在含水Na-蒙脱石中，MINFF对2W 00l衍射图谱的残差因子在所测模型中最低。

Conclusion: MINFF提供了一套无M-O键项、涵盖多元素氧化物/氢氧化物、与OPC3水模型相容的力场家族，通过通用与定制两组参数及四种角刚度，实现对可迁移性与保真度权衡的直接调控。拓扑结构可由MATLAB的atom库和Python的atomipy库生成，亦可访问www.atomipy.io获取。

Abstract: Atomistic simulations of minerals are limited by the accuracy and transferability of the force field. We introduce MINFF, a force field family for layered and non-layered oxides and hydroxides of M being Si, Al, Fe, Mg, Ca, Ti, Li and F. MINFF has no M-O bond terms. Instead M-O distances follow from electrostatics and dispersion, while a single harmonic angle term on all M-O triplets controls coordination geometry. Metal charges are DDEC6 charges from reference mineral structures, and oxygen charges are assigned by nearest-neighbour charge smearing, covering basal, apical, hydroxyl and edge sites and sites adjacent to isomorphous substitutions. The oxygen Lennard-Jones parameters are based on the OPC3 water model and change by less than 5% when re-optimised per mineral, so compatibility with OPC3 and related ion-pair potentials is measured rather than assumed. Metal Lennard-Jones parameters were fitted to unit-cell metrics, bond distances and angles, both synchronously across more than 30 reference minerals (general, GMINFF) and for individual minerals (tailored, TMINFF), each at four angle force constants (0, 250, 500 and 1500 kJ/mol/rad^2). With angle terms, MINFF reproduces experimental unit-cells metrics to within 0.5% and atom-type distances and angles to within 0.7-1.4% and 0.1-0.4%, with Global Instability Index values as low as 0.11, below those of CLAYFF (0.34) and INTERFACE (0.21). A moderate angle constant reproduces the stiff in-plane and soft shear elastic constants of phyllosilicates and improves the Kaolinite librational bands, and in hydrated Na-Montmorillonite MINFF gives the lowest residual factor against the 2W 00l pattern of the models tested. The general and tailored sets at four angle stiffnesses give direct control over the transferability-fidelity trade-off. Topologies can be generated with the atom (MATLAB) and atomipy (Python) libraries and at www.atomipy.io.

</details>


### [142] [A Topological Spin on Racetrack Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18297)
*Benjamin Zingsem*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在空心铁磁纳米管中，360°畴壁可将快速运动、抗扰动与可探测信号集成于单一连续磁壳，实现无需DMI和维持场的电流驱动高速赛道存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赛道存储器需要局域化载流子同时兼具高速运动、抗扰动能力和可探测信号，但现有方案常依赖Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用或外加维持场，增加了材料与结构约束，因此需要寻找在单一连续磁壳内即可满足上述要求的磁结构。

Method: 以空心铁磁管中的360°畴壁为研究对象，通过两次连续磁化反转形成束缚态，使两侧磁畴取向一致；结合解析能量学分析与微磁学模拟，考察其有限间距、抗扰动恢复、电流驱动输运行为，并开展弯曲轨道与差分电流测试，分析径向刚度与驱动均匀性的作用。

Result: 该360°畴壁具有有限间距，可在受测扰动后恢复，并在无微观DMI、无维持场条件下实现电流驱动输运；其绕数抵抗解绕，径向旋转带来能量代价从而提供稳定性调控，组成自旋的相对旋转可定量解释电流开启初期的额外位移；运动速度达数百米每秒，强驱动下瞬态速度接近1 km/s；预置的七位比特图案在脉冲输运与恢复后仍保持载流子与局域读出对比度。

Conclusion: 研究确立了快速、可局域区分的管状磁比特的物理基础，并指出可通过定制激励靶向调控的连续翻转路径，为高性能赛道存储提供新方案。

Abstract: Magnetic racetrack memory requires localized carriers that combine rapid motion, resistance to perturbations and a detectable signal. Here we show how a 360° domain wall in a hollow ferromagnetic tube combines these properties within a single continuous magnetic shell. Two successive magnetization reversals form a bound object whose surrounding domains point in the same direction. Analytical energetics and micromagnetic simulations establish its finite separation, recovery from tested perturbations and current driven transport without microscopic Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction or a maintaining field. The texture's winding resists unwinding while radial rotation carries an energy cost, providing a specific control over stability. Relative rotation of the constituent spins accounts quantitatively for additional displacement during current onset. The wall supports motion at hundreds of metres per second, with transient velocities approaching one kilometre per second at stronger drive. A prepared seven-bit pattern retains its carriers and local readout contrast after pulsed transport and recovery. Curved-track and differential-current tests identify radial stiffness and drive uniformity as controls for intact motion. These results establish a physical basis for fast, locally distinguishable tubular magnetic bits and identify a continuous switching pathway that can be targeted by tailored excitation.

</details>


### [143] [Transition-Metal-Induced Uniaxial Anisotropy in YFe$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18303)
*M. Nur Hasan,Bruno L. Fertig,Santa Pile,Rafael M. Vieira,Alena Vishina,Alexander Kovacs,Gotthard Rieger,Stefan Denneler,Samuel J. R. Holt,Martin Lang,Swapneel Pathak,Andrea Petrocchi,Hans Fangohr,Thomas George Woodcock,Thomas Schrefl,Heike C. Herper*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过过渡金属（V、Nb、Ta）选择性替代，实现YFe₃从易面到易轴磁晶各向异性转变，同时保持高磁化强度与热稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富Fe永磁体需兼具单轴磁晶各向异性、高磁化强度与热稳定性，YFe₃因饱和磁化强度和居里温度有潜力，但其本征易面各向异性限制了永磁应用。

Method: 第一性原理系统筛选过渡金属掺杂元素；计算交换相互作用并进行原子尺度蒙特卡罗模拟；开展微磁学模拟；对V合金化YFe₃进行实验表征，并结合浓度依赖的MAE计算、EBSD与Kerr显微分析。

Result: V、Nb、Ta为有效掺杂元素；YFe₂.₃₃Ta₀.₆₇获得2.3 MJ/m³的大单轴磁晶各向异性能；其矫顽力最高、抗不可逆退磁能力最强；YFe₂.₃₃V₀.₆₇在300 K下最大磁能积相当；低V名义浓度（1–3 at.%）可保留YFe₃相，高V含量导致多相；MAE在约11–14 at.% V间发生符号反转，对应易面到易轴转变；低V区实验观测到面内磁畴结构。

Conclusion: 本工作提出了调控YFe₃磁晶各向异性并保持高居里温度的有效途径。

Abstract: Achieving uniaxial magnetocrystalline anisotropy while preserving high magnetization and thermal stability remains a central challenge in the development of Fe-rich permanent magnets. YFe$_3$ is a promising candidate owing to its saturation magnetization and Curie temperature, but its intrinsic easy-plane anisotropy limits its potential for permanent-magnet applications. Here, we show that selective transition-metal substitution drives an easy-plane-to-easy-axis transition in YFe$_3$. Systematic first-principles screening identifies V, Nb, and Ta as effective dopants, with YFe$_{2.33}$Ta$_{0.67}$ exhibiting a large uniaxial magnetocrystalline anisotropy energy of 2.3~MJ/m$^{3}$. Calculated exchange interactions and atomistic Monte Carlo simulations show that the enhanced anisotropy is achieved while preserving robust ferromagnetic order and high thermal stability. Micromagnetic simulations further reveal that YFe$_{2.33}$Ta$_{0.67}$ exhibits the highest coercivity and strongest resistance to irreversible demagnetization, whereas YFe$_{2.33}$V$_{0.67}$ achieves a comparable maximum energy product at 300~K. Experimental characterization of V-alloyed YFe$_3$ shows that the YFe$_3$ phase can be retained at low nominal V concentrations (1-3 at.\%) under the employed synthesis and heat-treatment conditions, whereas higher V contents lead to increasingly multiphase samples. Concentration-dependent MAE calculations reveal a sign reversal between approximately 11 and 14~at.\% V, marking the transition from easy-plane to uniaxial easy-axis anisotropy. Consistent with this concentration dependence, electron backscatter diffraction and Kerr microscopy reveal in-plane magnetic domain structures in the experimentally accessible low-V regime. Therefore, our work offers a promising approach to tuning the magnetocrystalline anisotropy of YFe$_3$ while retaining high Curie temperatures.

</details>


### [144] [Accurately Generalizing Deep-Learning Hamiltonian Methods to Plane-Wave Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18317)
*Chuntian Wu,Jiaxiang Li,Qiang Xu,Xuecheng Shao,Congwei Xie,Yu Xie,Yanming Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种投影性引导的非正交原子轨道哈密顿量构建方法，从小规模精选的平面波本征态与本征值中构造适用于混合泛函学习的紧凑AO哈密顿量，无需经验位移，能准确复现能带并可由等变神经网络学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将局域原子轨道（AO）哈密顿量学习从传统计算扩展到使用杂化泛函的平面波（PW）计算，需要一种紧凑的AO表示来保留非局域算符效应；现有方法依赖实空间重构或以经验位移处理高能态，导致学习目标引入任意成分。

Method: 提出投影性引导的非正交AO哈密顿量构建方案：从PW计算中选取少量本征态与本征值，保留高投影性的态，并用精选的高能态补全AO子空间，避免经验位移；由于直接使用PW本征态与本征值，非局域贡献被自然包含，无需显式重建其在AO表象下的矩阵元。

Result: 在MoS2和钠相的基准测试中，所构建的AO基组比实空间重构更小，却能准确复现PW能带；生成的PBE与HSE哈密顿量可由等变神经网络学习，从而将PW参考计算与电子结构预测相连接。

Conclusion: 该方法拓展了可用于AO哈密顿量学习的PW DFT计算范围，为开发更具普适性的电子结构模型提供了紧凑而准确的训练目标。

Abstract: Deep neural networks that predict density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonians from material structures offer a route to efficient electronic-structure calculations. However, extending localized atomic-orbital (AO) Hamiltonian learning to plane-wave (PW) calculations with hybrid functionals requires a compact AO representation that preserves the effects of nonlocal operators. Here we propose a projectability-guided construction of nonorthogonal AO Hamiltonians from a small, selected set of PW eigenstates and eigenvalues. High-projectability states are retained, and selected higher-energy states complete the AO subspace, avoiding an empirical shift and its arbitrary contribution to the learning targets. Because the construction uses PW eigenstates and eigenvalues, it incorporates nonlocal contributions present in the reference calculation without explicitly reconstructing their AO matrix elements. Benchmarks for MoS2 and sodium phases show accurate reproduction of PW bands with smaller AO bases than real-space reconstruction. The resulting PBE and HSE Hamiltonians can be learned by equivariant neural networks, connecting PW reference calculations with electronic-structure prediction. This approach broadens the range of PW DFT calculations available for AO Hamiltonian learning and enables compact, accurate training targets for developing more widely applicable electronic-structure models.

</details>


### [145] [Reversible Magneto-ionic Modification of Metallic Magnetic Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18396)
*Md Golam Hafiz,Hari Babu Vasili,Philippa Shepley,Mannan Ali,Andrew J. Britton,Oscar Cespedes,Weibin Li,Manuel Valvidares,Rohit Pachat,Wilfried Fotso,Mourad Cherif,Shimpei Ono,Yves Roussigne,Mohamed Belmeguenai,Gavin Burnell*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文利用离子液体门控电驱动氧迁移，在金属多层膜中实现可控、非易失、可逆的局域磁学性能调控，并给出HM/FM界面氧化态改变的直接证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有结构反演不对称、垂直磁各向异性（PMA）和强Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）的磁性多层膜，是畴壁与斯格明子数据处理器件的核心材料，因此需要一种能够可控、可逆、非易失地原位调节其磁学参数的手段。

Method: 采用离子液体门控，在完全金属的薄膜结构中通过电场驱动氧离子迁移，对重金属（HM）/铁磁体（FM）界面的氧化态进行调控，并测量有效各向异性常数（Keff）、矫顽场（Hc）、磁矩、DMI及畴成核与畴壁钉扎行为的变化。

Result: 氧化时Keff与Hc增大，氧离子还原时二者减小；正栅压从磁性层抽取氧离子，显著降低畴成核场；负栅压将氧离子驱入磁性层，使近饱和区畴壁钉扎程度可逆增强；负电压同时使磁矩与DMI降低，并直接观测到HM/FM界面氧化态的改变。

Conclusion: 该磁离子调控在全金属结构中可行，可用于自旋电子器件，尤其适合场可编程的畴壁与斯格明子器件。

Abstract: Magnetic multilayers with structural inversion asymmetry, perpendicular magnetic anisotropy (PMA), and large Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) are widely studied for magnetic domain wall- and skyrmion-based data processing applications. In this work, we report a controlled, nonvolatile, but reversible local modification of such a system by electrically driven oxygen migration through the metallic film structure by using ionic liquid gating. Our findings provide direct evidence for the modification of the oxidation state at the heavy metal (HM)/ferromagnet (FM) interface. As a result, we observed changes in fundamental magnetic properties, such as an increase in the effective anisotropy constant (Keff) and coercive field (Hc) with oxidation, and a decrease with the reduction of oxygen ions. Positive gate voltages relative to the thin film extract oxygen ions from the magnetic layer, significantly reducing the domain nucleation field. In contrast, negative voltages drive oxygen ions into the magnetic layer, leading to a reversible increase in the extent of domain wall pinning near saturation. We observe a corresponding decrease in the magnetic moment and DMI, with negative voltage reducing both. This magneto-ionic modulation in fully metallic structures is beneficial for spintronic device applications, particularly in field-programmable domain wall and skyrmion devices.

</details>


### [146] [Substrate-controlled epitaxial-like growth and photochromic properties of yttrium oxyhydride thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18448)
*M. Zubkins,E. Strods,E. Letko,H. Mändar,D. Moldarev,K. Mizohata,K. Sarakinos,V. Vibornijs,K. Kundzins,J. Purans*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用反应磁控溅射在YSZ(111)/(100)等衬底上制备YHO薄膜并空气氧化；衬底取向显著影响微结构与织构（外延样生长，镶嵌度~3-4°），但光致变色主要由成分与局部O/H构型决定，而非长程阳离子有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清YHO（钇氧氢化物）薄膜中晶体学织构、化学成分与光致变色性能之间的关系，探索外延样生长是否能提升光学响应，为光学与光电子应用提供材料理解。

Method: 以β-YHx前驱膜为起点，在200 °C下通过反应磁控溅射沉积于单晶YSZ(111)与(100)衬底，随后空气后氧化；并在非晶SiO2与钠钙玻璃上于200 °C及室温制备参考样品。联合使用XRD、SEM、弹性反冲探测分析（ERDA）及光学（含光致变色）测试进行系统表征。

Result: 衬底取向强烈影响薄膜微结构与织构，获得镶嵌度约3-4°的外延样生长；YSZ(100)上晶粒更大（10-20 nm）并存在残余面内压应变，而对衬底基本弛豫，YSZ(111)上约8 nm。200 °C沉积膜氧含量较低（O/Y = 0.71-0.82），室温膜较高（O/Y = 0.92-1.26）。所有薄膜在太阳模拟器光照下均表现光致变色，10 min后相对透射率变化7-22%；尽管结构差异显著，外延样薄膜未见光致变色响应的系统性提升。

Conclusion: YHO薄膜的光致变色效应主要由成分及局部O/H构型主导，而非长程阳离子有序；研究揭示了织构、成分与光致变色性能的关联，为YHO在光学与光电子领域的应用提供依据。

Abstract: Yttrium oxyhydride (YHO) thin films are synthesised by reactive magnetron sputtering of \b{eta}-YHx precursor films onto single-crystalline yttria-stabilised zirconia (YSZ) (111) and (100) substrates at 200 °C, followed by post-deposition oxidation in air. For comparison, reference films are also prepared on amorphous SiO2 and soda-lime glass substrates at both 200 °C and room temperature (RT). The structural (X-ray diffraction and scanning electron microscopy), compositional (elastic recoil detection analysis), and optical properties, including photochromic behaviour, of the films are systematically investigated. The results reveal a strong influence of substrate orientation on the film microstructure and crystallographic texture, leading to epitaxial-like growth with a mosaic spread of ~3-4°. YHO deposited on YSZ(100) exhibits larger crystallite sizes (10-20 nm) than YHO grown on YSZ(111) (~8 nm) and exhibits residual in-plane compressive strain while remaining largely relaxed with respect to the substrate. Films deposited at 200 °C exhibit lower oxygen concentrations (O/Y = 0.71-0.82) than films deposited at RT (O/Y = 0.92-1.26). All films exhibit photochromic behaviour under solar simulator illumination, with relative transmittance changes ranging from 7 to 22 % after 10 min of illumination. Despite the pronounced structural differences, the epitaxial-like films show no systematic improvement in photochromic response, indicating that the effect is governed primarily by composition and the local O/H configuration rather than by long-range cation ordering. These findings provide new insights into the relationship between crystallographic texture, composition, and photochromic properties in YHO thin films, contributing to the understanding of this material for optical and optoelectronic applications.

</details>


### [147] [Quantitative Modelling of Solvation in 2D Materials via Alchemical Decoupling and Potential Energy Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18490)
*Lindsey N. Ioos,Duncan A. Peterson,Scott C. Warren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的二维材料溶剂化与稳定性建模策略，利用溶剂化吉布斯自由能标定完全去溶剂化与完全溶剂化两套势能面，并将其连接，从而预测二维材料在多种溶剂中的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料在溶液中为何保持悬浮，是理解其团聚与剥离机制的关键；但相关模拟涉及长时标与高活化能，建模困难。

Method: 以溶剂化吉布斯自由能为桥梁，对完全去溶剂化和完全溶剂化两套彼此不连通的势能面进行精确标定，并通过溶剂化能将二者连接，进而预测二维材料在不同溶剂中的稳定性。

Result: 预测结果在定性上与以往模拟不同，且与实验高度一致；新方法给出远高于以往估计的完全去溶剂化能垒，表明看似团聚的材料在相邻二维层之间实际仍保留单层溶剂。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对溶剂与二维材料相互作用的理解，可为后续实验研究提供指导。

Abstract: Understanding why 2D materials remain suspended in solutions is essential to understanding the mechanisms of aggregation and exfoliation. These dynamics have been difficult to model because of the long timescales and large activation energies involved in these simulations. Here we describe an improved strategy for modelling the solvation and stability of 2D materials that uses the Gibbs free energy of solvation to accurately calibrate two disconnected potential energy surfaces for the fully desolvated and fully solvated 2D materials. By joining these surfaces using solvation energy, we obtain predictions for 2D material stability in various solvents that are qualitatively different than prior simulations and that have excellent agreement with experimental results. Our approach predicts a much higher barrier to full desolvation of the material, suggesting that materials that appear to be aggregated in fact still retain monolayers of solvent between adjacent layers of 2D materials. This work provides much needed understanding on the interactions between the solvent and 2D material that can guide future experimental work.

</details>


### [148] [Flexoelectricity and Flexomagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18525)
*Alexander Edström,Massimiliano Stengel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述章节指出，应变梯度（而非均匀应变）在空间反演下为奇宇称，能够解锁母体晶格中严格禁戒的耦合。在纳米尺度及弯曲低维体系中，这类梯度极易甚至不可避免，因此曲率可作为写入晶格的对称性破缺场。文中系统梳理了应变梯度与曲率耦合到电极化和磁性的相关效应：先以挠曲电性为较成熟的参照，讨论其体/表面/动态贡献、微观机制、二维材料表现及第一性原理计算方法；再以磁电效应为概念桥梁，发展广义挠曲磁性，涵盖磁性与应变梯度的一般耦合，重点讨论曲率诱导的线性磁化响应与Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用；最后简要介绍非线性效应、非均匀磁电与Rashba型现象，以及与交错磁性的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀应变受晶体对称性约束，无法产生某些特定耦合；而应变梯度在空间反演下为奇宇称，能解锁母体晶体中严格禁戒的耦合。在纳米尺度、尤其是弯曲低维体系中，应变梯度普遍存在且往往不可避免，曲率实际上成为一种写入晶格的对称性破缺场。因此，有必要系统综述应变梯度与曲率所驱动的电极化与磁性耦合效应，梳理其机制、材料表现与计算方法。

Method: 这是一篇综述，采用"几何—机制—材料—计算—拓展"的组织框架。首先建立应变梯度与曲率之间的几何关系；随后以挠曲电性作为较成熟的参照案例，讨论其体、表面与动态贡献所导致的定义复杂性问题、微观机制、在二维材料中的表现，以及可用的第一性原理计算方法；再以磁电效应作为概念桥梁，发展广义"挠曲磁性"，涵盖磁性与应变梯度的各类耦合现象，并具体讨论曲率诱导的线性磁化响应与曲率诱导的Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用；最后归纳若干拓展效应。

Result: 章节梳理了应变梯度与曲率的几何关系；阐明挠曲电性的体、表面与动态贡献如何使其定义复杂化，并给出其微观机制与第一性原理计算方法；将挠曲磁性推广为磁性与应变梯度耦合的一般框架，重点涵盖曲率诱导的线性磁化响应及曲率诱导的Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用，并指出其超出既有曲线坐标处理的范围；同时列举了非线性效应、非均匀磁电与Rashba型现象以及与交错磁性的联系等拓展效应。

Conclusion: 曲率可作为写入晶格的对称性破缺场，应变梯度所解锁的耦合构成一类独立的物理效应体系。挠曲电性为较成熟的参照，挠曲磁性则作为广义框架统一了磁性与应变梯度的各种耦合，包括曲率诱导的线性磁化响应与Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用，并进一步拓展至非线性、非均匀磁电、Rashba型现象及交错磁性等领域。

Abstract: Strain gradients, unlike homogeneous strain, are odd under space inversion and unlock couplings that are strictly forbidden in the parent crystal. At the nanoscale, and in curved low-dimensional systems in particular, such gradients are readily attainable and often unavoidable, so that curvature acts as a symmetry-breaking field written into the lattice itself. This chapter reviews the resulting couplings to electric polarization and magnetism. After establishing the geometric relation between strain gradients and curvature, we review flexoelectricity as the better-established reference case: the bulk, surface and dynamic contributions that complicate its definition, its microscopic mechanisms, its manifestation in two-dimensional materials, and the first-principles methodology now available for its calculation. Using the magnetoelectric effect as a conceptual bridge, we then develop flexomagnetism in a broad sense, covering any coupling phenomena between magnetism and strain gradients, and specifically discuss the linear magnetization response to curvature and curvature-induced Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions, beyond the established curvilinear treatment. Finally, we briefly describe some additional effects fitting under this broad umbrella of flexomagnetism, including nonlinear ones, inhomogeneous magnetoelectric and Rashba-type phenomena, and connections to altermagnetism.

</details>


### [149] [Imaging Gate-Tunable Single-Layer Molecular Kagome Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18539)
*Ziyi Wang,Rafal Zuzak,Weichen Tang,Haowen Pang,Yutang Qiao,Dhanvanth Balakrishnan,Yuqiao Yan,Aining Hu,Andrew Sun,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Feng Wang,Steven G. Louie,Michael F. Crommie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用金属盐分子束外延在范德华衬底（含石墨烯场效应器件）上生长单层Ni₃(HITP)₂金属有机框架kagome网络，通过低温扫描隧道谱观测到平带与范霍夫奇点特征，并借助栅压实现kagome电子态的静电调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统kagome材料的奇异电子态通常嵌入化学复杂的三维晶体中，难以隔离或调控；而分子基kagome网络虽具备结构精确性与可调性，却大多局限于与二维器件不兼容的金属衬底。因此需要在兼容器件的范德华衬底上实现可栅控的kagome电子晶格。

Method: 采用金属盐分子束外延（metal-salt MBE）技术在范德华衬底上生长单层Ni₃(HITP)₂ MOF，衬底包括石墨烯基场效应器件；结合低温扫描隧道谱（STS）与第一性原理计算表征其电子结构；并通过栅压对杂化二维MOF器件进行静电调控。

Result: 低温STS分辨出与第一性原理计算一致的kagome衍生平带和基于范霍夫奇点的谱学特征；对杂化二维MOF器件施加栅压可使kagome谱学特征发生能量位移，实现了对其电子态的静电控制。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了在范德华衬底及器件平台上生长化学可编程二维kagome电子晶格并实现静电调控的可行性，为将kagome量子态集成到二维器件中提供了新路径。

Abstract: Kagome lattices provide a simple geometry for engineering electronic states that exhibit novel quantum behavior. In conventional kagome materials, however, the relevant states are often embedded in chemically complex three-dimensional crystals and are difficult to isolate or tune. Molecule-based kagome networks offer structural precision and tunability, but have largely been confined to metallic substrates incompatible with 2D devices. Here we report growth of single-layer Ni$_3$(HITP)$_2$ metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) on van der Waals substrates including graphene-based field-effect devices using metal-salt molecular beam epitaxy. Low-temperature scanning tunneling spectroscopy resolves kagome-derived flat-band and van Hove singularity-based spectral features consistent with first-principles calculations. Gating hybrid 2D MOF devices allows us to energetically shift kagome spectral features, thus establishing electrostatic control of chemically programmable 2D kagome electronic lattices.

</details>


### [150] [A Transferable Machine-Learned Potential Enabling Large-Scale Copper-Carbon Simulations from Equilibrium to Extreme Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18545)
*Feiyu Zhou,Wei Qiu,Yan Chen,Jiahui Liu,Yilun Liu,Shengying Yue,Penghua Ying*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了可迁移的神经演化势NEP-CuC，用2,727个均匀重标注构型训练，并通过ZBL短程修正与D3(BJ)色散修正提升描述能力，系统验证了Cu-C体系在多性质、多界面及非平衡过程下的物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cu-C材料与界面在金刚石热管理、金属化、石墨烯生长及碳涂层等技术中具有核心地位，但相关原子尺度模拟需要同时准确描述金属Cu、多种碳成键、异质Cu-C相互作用以及近距离原子碰撞的势函数，现有势函数难以兼顾这些需求。

Method: 构建了NEP-CuC神经演化势：从异质Cu-C候选池中选取2,727个均匀重标注构型作为训练集；用五个独立训练模型组成的集成模型间分歧作为模型敏感性的内部指标；引入ZBL修正描述短程核排斥，并在运行时加入D3(BJ)修正以补充色散敏感计算；随后在源数据留出集与独立外部数据集，以及结构、弹性、缺陷、界面、振动和有限温度输运等基准上进行测试，并开展非晶碳形成与Cu取向相关冲击压缩等大规模非平衡模拟。

Result: 测试表明NEP-CuC在元素Cu、多种碳环境和异质Cu-C构型上具有广泛但依赖于具体性质的物理保真度；在Cu/金刚石界面中，同一势函数可描述沉积、退火与拉伸响应，制备历史会在界面相结构中留下持久差异；当初始沉积阶段入射Cu能量从1 eV增至50 eV时，500 K下平均峰值名义拉伸应力由12.35±0.71 GPa降至6.65±0.98 GPa。

Conclusion: NEP-CuC为跨Cu-C体系关联加工过程、界面结构与物理响应提供了原子尺度框架，可直接应用于Cu/金刚石热管理界面、金刚石金属化及非平衡Cu-C加工等方向。

Abstract: Cu-C materials and interfaces are central to diamond thermal management, metallization, graphene growth and carbon-coating technologies, but atomistic simulations across these applications require a potential that describes metallic Cu, diverse carbon bonding, heterogeneous Cu-C interactions and close atomic encounters. Here we develop NEP-CuC, a transferable neuroevolution potential trained on a dataset of 2,727 uniformly relabelled configurations selected from a heterogeneous Cu-C candidate pool. Disagreement within an ensemble of five independently trained models provides an internal indicator of model sensitivity. A Ziegler-Biersack-Littmark (ZBL) correction describes short-range nuclear repulsion, while a runtime D3(BJ) correction supplements dispersion-sensitive calculations. Tests on source-derived held-out and independent external datasets, together with structural, elastic, defect, interfacial, vibrational and finite-temperature transport benchmarks, establish broad but property-dependent physical fidelity across elemental Cu, diverse carbon environments and heterogeneous Cu-C configurations. Large-scale simulations of amorphous-carbon formation and orientation-dependent shock compression of Cu further test transferability under strongly non-equilibrium conditions. For Cu/diamond interfaces, the same potential describes deposition, annealing and tensile response: preparation history leaves persistent differences in interphase structure, while the mean peak nominal tensile stress at 500 K decreases from $12.35\pm0.71$ to $6.65\pm0.98$ GPa as the incident Cu energy during the initial deposition stage increases from 1 to 50 eV. NEP-CuC provides an atomistic framework for linking processing, interfacial structure and physical response across Cu-C systems, with direct relevance to Cu/diamond thermal-management interfaces, diamond metallization and non-equilibrium Cu-C processing.

</details>


### [151] [Direct growth of crystalline spinel GaON films: A wide-bandgap mixed-anion semiconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18550)
*Baikui Li,Zecheng Yang,Wenhao Yin,Zijing Wu,Haitao Zhao,Yunwei Sheng,Honglei Wu,Jiannong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过氮等离子体辅助脉冲激光沉积直接生长出结晶尖晶石型γ-GaON薄膜，并测定其室温带边跃迁约4.3 eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合阴离子半导体为带隙工程提供了超越传统阳离子合金的途径，但竞争相与无序性使合成和电子性质鉴定困难。GaON有望连接GaN与Ga2O3体系，但其晶体结构、组分与带隙关系尚不明确。

Method: 采用氮等离子体辅助脉冲激光沉积（PLD）生长结晶尖晶石γ-GaON薄膜；通过调节氮氧供给控制产物相；结合衍射、原子分辨率显微术与元素分析确认尖晶石结构及氮掺入；利用变温光电流谱表征光学响应。

Result: 改变氮氧供给可得单斜β-Ga2O3、以无序为主的薄膜及结晶尖晶石GaON。结构表征证实了尖晶石结构与氮的掺入。变温光电流谱显示结晶γ-GaON响应明显红移，支持室温下约4.3 eV的带边跃迁归属。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了结晶γ-GaON薄膜的直接生长并厘清其结构与带边特性，为研究混合阴离子宽禁带半导体物理及构建异质结构提供了机会。

Abstract: Mixed-anion semiconductors offer opportunities for bandgap engineering beyond conventional multication alloys, but competing phases and disorder complicate their synthesis and the identification of their electronic properties. Gallium oxynitride (GaON) could connect the technologically important GaN and Ga2O3 systems, yet establishing a clear relation between its crystal structure, composition and bandgap remains challenging. Here we report the direct growth of crystalline spinel gamma-GaON films by nitrogen-plasma-assisted pulsed-laser deposition. Varying the nitrogen and oxygen supply yields monoclinic beta-Ga2O3, predominantly disordered films and crystalline spinel GaON. Complementary diffraction, atomic-resolution microscopy and elemental analysis establish the spinel structure and nitrogen incorporation.
