Temperature-dependent photocurrent spectroscopy reveals a clear redshift of the crystalline gamma-GaON response, supporting assignment of a band-edge transition near 4.3 eV at room temperature. These results open opportunities to investigate mixed-anion wide-bandgap semiconductor physics and develop heterostructures.

</details>


### [152] [Time-Resolved Phonon Dispersions of Thermally Excited Graphite at the Nanoscale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18561)
*Mairi McCauley,Christoph T. Koch,Benedikt Haas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文首次将动量分辨振动电子能量损失谱（EELS）与脉冲激光加热及门控电子探测同步，实现了微秒时间尺度上石墨声子色散的时分辨测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格振动决定材料中的热传导，通过时分辨声子色散测量研究其动力学可深入理解这些激发及其相互作用。但此前尚无能在纳米尺度直接分辨声子色散的时分辨测量手段，亟需发展兼具能量、动量与时间分辨的探测方法。

Method: 将动量分辨振动EELS与脉冲激光加热、同步门控电子探测相结合，在受控加热与冷却过程中于微秒时标观测石墨声子色散。通过记录相对于激光激发特定时刻的增益与损失谱，确定各延迟时刻的温度，并追踪声子支随温度的变化。探测器门控时间可达50 ns。

Result: 动量分辨谱显示：随温度升高，光学声子支能量下降，同时峰宽增大。这些变化与温度依赖的声子重正化及非谐相互作用引起的散射相符。

Conclusion: 本工作展示了基于振动EELS的时分辨声子色散谱学，为在纳米尺度、同时具备能量与动量分辨条件下探测温度依赖的声子动力学提供了途径，并可通过门控探测扩展至更快的动力学过程。

Abstract: Lattice vibrations govern the propagation of heat in materials, and investigating their dynamics through time-resolved phonon dispersion measurements would provide insight into these excitations and their interactions. However, time-resolved measurements that directly resolve the phonon dispersion at the nanoscale have not been previously demonstrated. Momentum-resolved vibrational electron energy loss spectroscopy (EELS) provides access to phonon energies and momenta with nanoscale spatial resolution. Here, we combine momentum-resolved vibrational EELS with pulsed laser heating and synchronised gated electron detection to observe the phonon dispersion of graphite during controlled heating and cooling on the microsecond timescale. Recording both the gain and loss spectra at defined times relative to laser excitation, we determine the temperature at each time delay and follow changes to the phonon branches as a function of temperature. The momentum-resolved spectra reveal a decrease in energy of the optical phonon branch with increasing temperature accompanied by an increase in peak width. These changes are consistent with temperature-dependent phonon renormalisation and scattering associated with anharmonic interactions. The approach is compatible with detector gating on timescales down to 50 ns, providing a route towards extending these measurements to faster dynamics. This work demonstrates time-resolved phonon dispersion spectroscopy using vibrational EELS and provides a route towards probing temperature-dependent phonon dynamics at the nanoscale with simultaneous energy and momentum resolution.

</details>


### [153] [Composition-controlled optical fingerprints and anisotropy of self-trapped excitons in double perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18665)
*Simone Grillo,Offir Zachs Maishlos,Liya Koren,Rotem Strassberg,Rachel Lifer,Edi Maizenberg,Umberto De Giovannini,Angel Rubio,Yehonadav Bekenstein,Ofer Neufeld*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合光谱学与第一性原理计算，研究B位合金化（Na→K）的Sb掺杂Cs2BInCl6纳米晶，揭示掺杂与合金化对自陷激子（STE）及发光性质的独立调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双钙钛矿（DPs）是光催化与光电子学的通用平台，但其软晶格导致强激子-声子耦合与自陷激子，产生宽化、红移的光致发光（PL）。掺杂与合金化各自的作用及STE波函数特性尚缺乏清晰解耦，需要可探测STE波函数的实验手段。

Method: 将光学光谱学与从头算（ab initio）计算相结合，研究无铅Sb掺杂Cs2BInCl6纳米晶，并通过Na→K进行B位合金化；采用时间分辨各向异性（偏振分辨）光谱测量并与理论预测对比。

Result: 合金化可将PL能量从约2.48 eV调至2.70 eV，而约3.8 eV的吸收特征基本不变；计算表明Sb掺杂引入明亮的辐射复合通道并使激子波函数离域化；STE是PL大尺度红移的成因，与掺杂剂杂化带隙内态上移相关；Na→K取代选择性改变STE晶格畸变；理论预测显著的PL偏振各向异性并依赖B位组分，时间分辨各向异性测量与之高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作解耦了无铅双钙钛矿中掺杂与合金化的作用，确立偏振分辨光谱学作为探测STE波函数空间结构及其随合金化演化的有效手段。

Abstract: Double perovskites (DPs) offer a compositionally versatile platform for photocatalysis and optoelectronics, but their soft lattices favor strong exciton-phonon coupling and self-trapped excitons (STEs), which produce broad, red-shifted photoluminescence (PL). Here, we combine optical spectroscopy and ab initio calculations to study lead-free, Sb-doped Cs2BInCl6 nanocrystals with B-site alloying from Na to K. Alloying tunes the PL energy from about 2.48 to 2.70 eV, while absorption features near 3.8 eV remain nearly unchanged. Calculations reveal that Sb doping enables bright radiative recombination channels and delocalizes the excitonic wavefunction. We identify STEs responsible for the large PL red shift, associated with an upward shift of dopant-hybridized in-gap states. Na-to-K substitution selectively changes the STE lattice distortion, explaining why PL varies strongly with composition while absorption does not. Theory further predicts pronounced PL polarization anisotropy that reflects the STE wavefunction's spatial structure and depends on B-site composition. Time-resolved anisotropy measurements closely agree with these predictions, providing experimental fingerprints of the excitonic wavefunction and its evolution with alloying. Together, these results disentangle the roles of doping and alloying in lead-free DPs and establish polarization-resolved spectroscopy as a probe of STE wavefunctions.

</details>
